# Steps M27-M29: Kettle stories, Saltglass Flats, the third stone, and the road to the sky

Issue-ready specs for S-236 to S-261. Lands in the repo as `docs/roadmap/steps/M27-M29.md`; every bible link below is
relative to that path (`../bible/<file>.md#<anchor>`). Order of authority: `docs/roadmap/CANON-NOW.md` LOCKED > `docs/roadmap/CANON.md` > the reconciliation
rows in `docs/roadmap/steps/*.md` (for the topics they name, until the named close-out edits the losing file) > the
bible (`docs/roadmap/bible/*.md`) > the INDEX row > the issue. Where two bible files disagree, this file names the reading it takes in the step's
**Decisions** paragraph, so the implementer does not have to choose.

**State of the game when M27 starts** (what earlier steps have built, by INDEX): save **v11** (S-233); flags,
items, quests, collect and the per-id maps (`chests`, `secrets`, `bosses`, `plushies`, `rematches`); realms with
`hidden`, portals, palettes, bundles (S-018-S-029); the condition language `js/cond.js` and `tests/content.test.js`
(S-025); text tables `js/text/en/*.js` and `js/dialogue.js` (S-032); scenes `js/scenes.js` (S-034); items and the
`tool` action (S-035); quests and Field Notes (S-036-S-038); the boss framework `js/boss/defs.js` with handover,
plushie and Wake rematch as data (S-043-S-045); the bestiary (S-046); abilities, gate objects `js/gates.js` and the
progression solver `js/progress-graph.js` (S-048, whole graph S-108); the dungeon compiler and solver
`js/realms/dungeon-compile.js`, the dungeon recipe, interior kit and puzzle entities `js/dungeon-mod.js`
(S-049-S-053); interiors (S-054); Hollows (S-073); `collect` (S-071); Completion v2 and `js/realms/stats.js`
(S-074); the economy ledger `js/content/economy.js` + `tests/economy.test.js` (S-078); benches and travel (S-080);
the Lamplighter kit (S-082); Clover's Dash framework (S-112); Rook's Races framework (S-113); pathfinding and
schedules (S-174); the chapter partner that walks ahead (Digby, S-191); the Ember Sling (S-227); Kettle Isle unhidden
with its Stair Stone (S-232); Starsteel (S-233); the stone-set count beat and memory knots (S-190, S-212); chapter
tests `tests/chapter.mjs` (S-030). File names below follow those steps. Where an earlier step chose a different file
or field name than the bible predicted, **keep the shipped name** and note it in the PR body.

**Shared rules for every step in this file.**
- Issue body = the six sections below (`Why`, `What`, `Acceptance criteria`, `Verification plan`, `Out of scope`,
  `Risk & rollback`) plus the step's **Assets**. Title = the step heading after `·`, verbatim.
- Every step's acceptance criteria include the **invariants block** from
  [charter.md#invariants-block](../bible/charter.md#invariants-block), word for word, with the lines that cannot apply
  deleted. Each step below says which lines it keeps ("Invariants: keep all" or a list).
- Every new line of text goes into a `js/text/en/*.js` table by id, is ≤ 220 characters, uses only known `{tokens}`,
  keeps `~` for Miso and `!!` for Kinako only, and Pip never speaks. The PR body carries the
  [tone checklist](../bible/charter.md#tone-check).
- Every generated image follows [art-audio.md#preamble](../bible/art-audio.md#preamble) + the kind suffix from
  [art-audio.md#kinds](../bible/art-audio.md#kinds) + the subject given here; it is made by a GPT Sol subagent
  (T3 `delegate_task`, Codex `image_gen.imagegen`) or a Gemini model, processed with the repo tools, saved as webp,
  logged in `docs/art/prompts.md`, registered in `assets/manifest.js` with its `realm` tag, and has the fallback
  named here. Raw generations are never committed.
- One heavy job at a time on the 12 GB machine: run `free -h` first; never two browsers.
- Standard local verification for every step (each step adds its own lines after these):
  `bun test` · `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (when assets or files change) ·
  `bun run test:smoke`.

## M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain

**Goal.** Kettle Isle's side stories and two activities, the optional Snowglobe dungeon under Frostcap with
Great-Aunt Flurry, the twelve-person Parcel With No Name that pays the Chime Bell, and the end of the twins' sulk.

**Entry criteria.** M26 closed (S-235 stacked or merged): Kettle Isle unhidden, `stone:kettle` reachable, the Ember
Sling owned in its dungeon, the Snowglobe ice wall placed on home by `placeBellTargets` (S-227), Starsteel and save
v11 shipped, Granny Mochi's cottage and ss-03 part 1 shipped (S-134), the Catchers' plaza evenings shipped (S-170).

**Exit criteria.** Steps S-236a, S-236b, S-237a, S-237b, S-238, S-239a, S-239b, S-240, S-241, S-242 stacked or merged; `bun test`,
`test:smoke`, `test:pwa`, `test:opening`, `test:playthrough`, every in-page suite and every chapter test green;
`docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-12.md` written; `progress.md` entry for M27.

**What the player can do at the end.** Help Pitch try to forge a star (paid off in ch21); set Soakley's five baths
right and soak to full hearts; sail Miso to meet Master Scoria and learn Kinako sold her swords at cost; play the
Sling Gallery (heart petal at 25 points) and Steam Hop (heart petal under 90 s); melt the ice wall under Frostcap,
solve the ten-room Snowglobe of sliding ice, calm Great-Aunt Flurry for a heart petal and the free Snowdrift trim;
carry a nameless parcel through twelve hands to Forgefall and open it to get the Chime Bell; watch Miso and Kinako
hang one sign together.

**Playtime added.** +4.5 h (cumulative 67.0 h): ss-39 15 min, ss-40 20, ss-41 15, ss-12 15, the Snowglobe 75,
Flurry 10, ss-10 35, ss-03 part 2 10, Sling Gallery 25, Steam Hop 25, travel between them about 25.

**Save-version plan.** No bump. Everything lands in `flags` (`ss39.*`, `ss40.*`, `ss10.*`, `act.*`, `gate:*`,
`seen:*`), `items` (`parcel`, `chime_bell`, `sign_kinako`, `scarf`, `trim_snowdrift`, gifts), `quests`, `collect`
(`petal`), `chests`, `bosses`, `rematches` and `S.dungeons.snowglobe`. Save stays v11.

<a id="m28"></a>

## M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone

**Goal.** The desert of the buried Academy: a 640 m dune-and-salt-pan realm, its people and creatures, the Ivy
Gloves, the 14-room Buried Academy, Tutor Scarabelle's three-paper exam and the last Stair Stone.

**Entry criteria.** M27 closed (S-242 stacked or merged). The Glassway's Saltglass buoy portal
`glassway:buoy_saltglass` exists as a fog-locked stub (S-200/S-222 pattern).

**Exit criteria.** Steps S-243 to S-257 (with the splits S-248a/b, S-249a/b and the added S-254b, S-254c and S-254d) stacked or merged; `saltglass`
unhidden by S-254 with its chapter test `ch-saltglass`; the six Stair Stone orders proven by S-256; the full local
suite green; `progress.md` entry with Saltglass's genMs, swap time and bundle size.

**What the player can do at the end.** Sail to Saltglass Flats, meet Sable's dig camp, dig out the Academy's roof
hatch, ring the hand-bell and enrol; solve the Buried Academy's sand rivers and nameplate doors, find the Ivy Gloves,
climb ivy everywhere (including up the Great Tree's trunk to the Crown Balcony and vista v4 on home); sit Tutor
Scarabelle's exam (dodging, aiming, the "light, give, climb" puzzle) and carry the bell's weight home as the third
Stair Stone (memories 10-12 when it is the third); clear Saltglass's three Hollows, run its Dash course and win
Rook's fifth race for his feather.

**Playtime added.** +5 h (cumulative 72.0 h): main chain 4.0 h (people 0.5, the Buried Academy 2.0, exam 0.5,
overworld finds on the way 0.6, memories 10-12 0.4), Hollows 25 min, Dash 10 min, Rook part 5 10 min, home ivy finds 15 min.

**Save-version plan.** No bump. New ids are `saltglass:<id>` in `chests`, `secrets`, `collect`, `travel`;
`S.dungeons.buried_academy` (v8 container) holds puzzle state; the boss key is `buried_academy:scarabelle`
([systems.md#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids)). Save stays v11.

<a id="m29"></a>

## M29 · Saltglass stories and the road to the sky

**Goal.** Saltglass's three side stories, the villagers' and Wenna's Act III reactions, and performance
checkpoint 6 over every shipped realm and the precache before the sky lands are built.

**Entry criteria.** M28 closed (S-257 stacked or merged).

**Exit criteria.** S-258a, S-258b, S-259, S-260, S-261 stacked or merged; checkpoint 6 numbers recorded in
`progress.md` and DESIGN §14; core precache ≤ 30 MB and every realm bundle ≤ 2.5 MB; `docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-13.md`
written.

**What the player can do at the end.** Brush Sable's six dig sites into a museum tent; gather nine student
nameplates, hang them in the Academy library and answer Scarabelle's dawn roll call for Wick and Pelagia; sit the
gossip Exam Retake for an honorary certificate; hear Wenna's next 16 lines (stages S8-S11) and every villager's
Act III memory line and stone reactions, with Shellby gone quiet after memory 12.

**Playtime added.** +2 h (cumulative 74.0 h): ss-42 20 min, ss-43 20, ss-44 15, travel and the library 15, Act III
reaction lines and Wenna's lines heard across visits about 30, re-reading the Academy finds 20.
The scripted floor for M29 is about 1.2 h; S-261 records the measured figure and, if the gap to +2 h is above 25 %,
logs it per [charter.md#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone) step 7.

**Save-version plan.** No bump (flags `ss42.*`, `ss43.*`, `ss44.*`, `wennaLine`, `seen:*`, `mem:<npc>:3`; items for the
nameplates and the certificate gift).

### S-236a · feat: side story 39 Pitch Wants a Star

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-232a, S-078 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-39](../bible/side-content.md#ss-39), [cast#pitch](../bible/cast.md#pitch),
[atlas-far#kettle-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-pois), [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes),
[systems#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**
(`Roadmap: docs/roadmap/INDEX.md S-236 (M27 Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain)`, part a).
Kettle Isle's main chain is done; its smallest resident gets her story. Pitch's wish plants a promise the finale keeps
(the ch21 star splinter). This part also adds the two shared pieces every later Kettle story uses: Kettle's
`placeSideMarks` pass and the quest engine's per-step `needs`. (Split from S-236 so each side story is one reviewable
PR; ss-40 is S-236b.)

#### What
1. **Quest data** in `js/content/quests/kettle.js` (create it if S-232a put Kettle's quests elsewhere, follow that):

   ```js
   { id: 'ss-39', n: 139, realm: 'kettle', kind: 'side', owner: 'pitch', title: 'ke.ss39.title',
     unlock: ['realm:kettle.visited'],
     steps: [
       { id: 'ask', text: 'ke.ss39.s1', target: { npc: 'pitch' }, done: 'flag:ss39.asked', on: 'TALK_END',
         hint: ['ke.ss39.h1.n', 'ke.ss39.h1.a'] },
       { id: 'hot', text: 'ke.ss39.s2', done: 'flag:ss39.hot>=2', on: 'ITEM_GET', parallel: true,
         hint: ['ke.ss39.h2.n', 'ke.ss39.h2.a'] },
       { id: 'try', text: 'ke.ss39.s3', target: { npc: 'pitch' }, done: 'flag:ss39.promise', on: 'SCENE_END',
         hint: ['ke.ss39.h3.n', 'ke.ss39.h3.a'] },
       { id: 'splinter', text: 'ke.ss39.s4', target: { npc: 'pitch' }, needs: 'flag:mainEnding',
         done: 'flag:ss39.done', on: 'SCENE_END', hint: ['ke.ss39.h4.n', 'ke.ss39.h4.a'] } ],
     reward: ['gems:+40'], set: ['set:ss39.done'], mark: 'ss39.mark', minutes: 15 }
   ```
   - `needs` on step 4 of ss-39: the step stays hidden in the journal and shows "To be continued ✿" until
     `flag:mainEnding` ([side-content#rules](../bible/side-content.md#rules) rule 5). If the quest engine has no
     per-step `needs`, add it in `js/quests.js` (a step whose `needs` fails is not shown by `objective()` and its
     journal row reads `side.common.tbc`), with a unit test.
   - Register the flags in `js/content/flags.js`: `ss39.asked`, `ss39.promise`, `ss39.done` (kind `story`),
     `ss39.hot` (kind `counter`).
   - **Kettle's `placeSideMarks` pass** (`new RNG(40261 * 107 + 19)`, [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes))
     is created here, append-only, run after Kettle's prints are taken; S-236b, S-237a and later Kettle steps append to
     its list.
2. **ss-39 Pitch Wants a Star.**
   - Step `hot`, item 1, **the ember**: a clay ember pot prop on the Spout Mouth lip (`w:spoutMouth`, nearest walkable
     within 6 m, ≥ 3 m from chest `kettle:c10`), placed by Kettle's `placeSideMarks` pass (`new RNG(40261 * 107 +
     19)`, [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes)). `[E] Pick up · Ember` while ss-39 is at `hot`
     and the ember is not yet held: item-get "An ember in a clay pot. Hot hot hot! says the label." (key item
     `ss39_ember`, kind `delivery`), `inc:ss39.hot`. The pot prop stays (it refills; nothing is taken back).
   - Item 2, **the bath pebble**: if `boss:kettles_belly:fumarole` (use the boss key S-231 shipped), Talk with Lady
     Fumarole at her after spot gives it (line `ke.ss39.fumarole.1`); otherwise Talk with Soakley gives his spare
     (`ke.ss39.soakley.1`). Either gives key item `ss39_pebble` (kind `delivery`) and `inc:ss39.hot`. Only one
     pebble can be got.
   - Step `try`: Talk with Pitch with both items: scene `ss39-try` (12 s, `replay: true`): Pitch at the anvil, two
     hammer clangs, a glow, she holds up a glowing lump; the camera dips 6° and back in 0.4 s (Pip's nod, the
     first-person nod used elsewhere); lines `pitch.2`; effects `take:ss39_ember`, `take:ss39_pebble`,
     `set:ss39.promise`. The "glowing potato" prop (an orange sphere 0.18 m, `nightGlow` 0.6) appears on Scoria's
     shelf in the forge interior `scoria_forge` with a label card "Pitch's first star" (`[E] Look · Glowing lump`:
     `ke.ss39.look.potato`).
   - Step `splinter` (data now, live from ch21): `[E] Give · Star splinter` on Pitch when `item:star_splinter` and
     `flag:mainEnding`: scene `ss39-star` (15 s): `take:star_splinter`, lines `pitch.3`, and the world mark below.
     The chapter test for ch21 (S-28x) will exercise it; this PR proves it with a unit test on the quest data and
     an in-page check that uses `G.debug.give('star_splinter')` + `G.debug.flags.set('mainEnding', true)`.
   - World mark (on `ss39.done`): a tiny star pendant (code-built: a 5-point star 0.25 m, `#ffd36b`, `nightGlow` 1.0,
     on a chain) hangs over Forgefall's gate; it glows gold at night.
3. **Lines** (`js/text/en/ke.js`, exact text from
   [side-content#ss-39](../bible/side-content.md#ss-39)):
   - `ke.ss39.pitch.1` "A star! A real one! Scoria says you can't forge a star. Scoria's never TRIED."
   - `ke.ss39.pitch.2` "It's... a potato. A glowing potato. ...You'll bring me a real bit of star one day? Nod if yes. ...You nodded!"
   - `ke.ss39.pitch.3` "A star. A real one. In my hands. I'm going to need a bigger anvil. And a hat. A star-forging hat."
   - `ke.ss39.fumarole.1` "A pebble from my bath? Take two. Take ten. No, one. They're MY pebbles. ...Fine, one. It's still warm."
   - `ke.ss39.soakley.1` "My spare bath pebble. Lovely and warm. No rush bringing it back. Don't bring it back, actually. No rush."
   - `ke.ss39.look.potato` "A glowing lump on a shelf. The label says: 'Pitch's first star'. It is still warm."
   - Journal: `ke.ss39.title` "Pitch Wants a Star"; `.s1` "Talk to Pitch at the forge"; `.s2` "Bring Pitch the
     hottest things on the isle ({n}/2)"; `.s3` "Watch Pitch try"; `.s4` "Bring Pitch a star splinter";
     `.open` "Pitch intends to forge a star. Stars are not forged. I have not said so. Pitch is very determined.";
     `.done` "Pitch forged a star. I have revised several papers."
   - Hints: `h1` ["Pitch is the small one with big goggles.", "Talk to Pitch beside Master Scoria at the forge in
     Forgefall."]; `h2` ["The hottest things: one up high, one in a bath.", "Pick up the ember pot at the Spout Mouth,
     and ask Lady Fumarole (or Soakley) for a bath pebble."]; `h3` ["Pitch is ready to try.", "Bring both hot things
     to Pitch and talk to her."]; `h4` ["A real bit of star. You'll know it when you have it.", "After the Second
     Sneeze, give Pitch the star splinter."]
3. **Dialogue priority** (`js/content/dialogue/kettle.js`): the ss-39 start entry at priority 40 with
   `quest:ss-39.start`; progress lines at 40; after done, one idle line: Pitch `ke.pitch.idle.ss39` "Hot hot hot! I'm
   practising stars on potatoes. Scoria says that's a waste of potatoes."
4. **Economy**: add ss-39 (40) to the side-story table that `js/content/economy.js` sums.

#### Assets
- `kettle_star_pendant` (≤ 80 tris): flat 5-point star on a chain, `#ffd36b`, `nightGlow` 1.0. Fallback the same
  shape (it is the code model).
- `kettle_glowing_lump` (≤ 60 tris): an orange sphere 0.18 m, `#e66f4f` with `nightGlow` 0.6, label card. Fallback is
  the model.
- No generated images in this step.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all lines except "Frozen home island" (no home change) and "Precache" (unless a file is added to
  the site; then keep it).
- ss-39 appears in Field Notes once `realm:kettle.visited` holds; it has a journal entry, a two-part hint per step and a
  world mark that appears within 10 s of its last step.
- ss-39 step 4 is hidden ("To be continued ✿") before `mainEnding`; with `mainEnding` and `star_splinter` the give
  completes the story and pays 40 gems once.
- The per-step `needs` unit test passes (a failing `needs` hides the step from `objective()` and shows `side.common.tbc`).
- Kettle's realm prints unchanged; content lint green (every line ≤ 220 characters, `~` and `!!` unused here, every
  flag registered); `tests/economy.test.js` passes with the new reward.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (extended `tests/content.test.js`, `tests/economy.test.js`, quest-data test for `needs`; the Kettle
  passes test for the new `placeSideMarks` stream).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side-kettle` (new suite file `js/debug/tests/side-kettle.js`, CI matrix entry `side-kettle`):
  (1) from fixture `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-kettle-end.json` (written by S-232a; if it is named differently, use that):
  start ss-39, `G.debug.warpTo('kettle:ss39_ember')`, pick up, get the pebble from Soakley, talk to Pitch, skip the
  scene, assert `ss39.promise`; (2) give `star_splinter` + set `mainEnding`, give, assert `ss39.done` and +40 gems, the
  pendant over Forgefall's gate.
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- ss-40 Soakley's Baths (S-236b). Miso's Supplier, Sling Gallery and Steam Hop (S-237a, S-237b). The star splinter's
  source (ch21, M32).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR. Everything is additive data, props from a new `placeSideMarks` stream on Kettle Isle (realm prints
unchanged: the pass runs after prints are taken, as every post-pass) and flags that nothing else reads. Reverting also
removes per-step `needs`, which S-236b and S-240 use; revert those first if they are stacked.

<a id="s-236b"></a>

### S-236b · feat: side story 40 Soakley's Baths

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-236a, S-078 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-40](../bible/side-content.md#ss-40), [cast#soakley](../bible/cast.md#soakley),
[atlas-far#kettle-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-pois), [systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates),
[systems#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**
(`Roadmap: docs/roadmap/INDEX.md S-236 (M27 Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain)`, part b).
Soakley's baths turn the Bathhouse Terraces into a place the player comes back to (a free full heal once per visit),
with a small five-dial temperature puzzle solved with the Swing and the Ember Sling. Depends on S-236a for Kettle's
`placeSideMarks` pass and the `side-kettle` suite.

#### What
1. **Quest data** appended to `js/content/quests/kettle.js`:

   ```js
   { id: 'ss-40', n: 140, realm: 'kettle', kind: 'side', owner: 'soakley', title: 'ke.ss40.title',
     unlock: ['ability:sling'],
     steps: [
       { id: 'ask', text: 'ke.ss40.s1', target: { npc: 'soakley' }, done: 'flag:ss40.asked', on: 'TALK_END', hint: [...] },
       { id: 'baths', text: 'ke.ss40.s2', done: 'flag:ss40.right', on: 'PUZZLE', hint: [...] },
       { id: 'soak', text: 'ke.ss40.s3', done: 'flag:ss40.soaked', on: 'SCENE_END', hint: [...] } ],
     reward: ['gems:+35'], set: ['set:ss40.done'], mark: 'ss40.mark', minutes: 20 }
   ```
   - Register the flags in `js/content/flags.js`: `ss40.asked`, `ss40.right`, `ss40.soaked`, `ss40.done` (kind
     `story`), `ss40.bath<n>.lit`, `ss40.bath<n>.valve` (kind `toggle`/rewritable, values 0/1 and 0/1/2: these are
     player-controlled puzzle settings, so they are rewritable like the tide toggles; record that in the registry doc),
     and `ss40.visit` (runtime only, not saved: see 2).
2. **ss-40 Soakley's Baths.**
   - **The five baths** are the basins at the atlas anchors (−55, 115), (−20, 105), (10, 100), (35, 100), (55, 108),
     numbered 1-5 west to east. Each gets three small props placed by `placeSideMarks` beside its basin rim (nearest
     walkable to the basin's north rim, in a 1.5 m row): a **frog-shaped gauge** (0.5 m, the frog's belly shows the
     colour), a **valve wheel** (0.6 m, brass) and a **brazier** with a bell-shaped cap (0.9 m, the shared
     `cold_brazier` gate look from [systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates) with a brass cap on a chain).
   - Controls: Swing on a valve cycles open → half → shut → open (one step per hit, 0.3 s turn); an Ember Sling hit on
     a brazier lights it; a Swing on a lit brazier's bell-cap puts it out (cap drops, puff of steam). No prompts are
     needed (Swing and the Sling have none), but each prop shows `[E] Look · Gauge` with the reading line.
   - **Temperature model** (pure module `js/content/kettle-baths.js`, unit-tested): spring warmth `b = [1, 2, 2, 3, 1]`
     for baths 1-5; heat `h = lit ? 2 : 0`; cold flow `f = { open: 2, half: 1, shut: 0 }`; **baths 2 and 3 share a
     pipe**, so bath 3's cold flow is `f3 + f2`. `T1 = b1 + h1 − f1`, `T2 = b2 + h2 − f2`, `T3 = b3 + h3 − f3 − f2`,
     `T4 = b4 + h4 − f4`, `T5 = b5 + h5 − f5`. Reading: `T ≤ 0` "cold" (blue `#6fb0e0`), `T = 1` "lukewarm"
     (blue-green `#8fc7b8`), `T = 2` "just right" (green `#8fc7a0`), `T ≥ 3` "scalding" (red `#e66f4f`).
   - **Start state** (gives the bible's "cold, scalding, cold, scalding, lukewarm"): bath 1 out/half (T 0), bath 2
     lit/shut (T 4), bath 3 out/open with bath 2 shut (T 0), bath 4 lit/shut (T 5), bath 5 out/shut (T 1).
   - **The bible's solution** (unit test asserts all five read "just right"): 1 lit/half, 2 out/shut, 3 lit/open,
     4 out/half, 5 lit/half. Any other setting with all five green also completes (the model admits one more: 2
     lit/open, 3 lit/shut; that is fine). The gauge needle swings to its new reading within 2 s of a change; when all
     five are green, `set:ss40.right` and `EV.PUZZLE { id: 'kettle:baths', on: true }`.
   - The settings are saved in the rewritable flags so a reload keeps them; once `ss40.right` is set the props lock
     (Swing does nothing; a Look line says "Just right. Soakley says don't touch it, lovingly.").
   - Step `soak`: `[E] Sit · Bath` at any of the five basins once `ss40.right`: scene `ss40-soak` (10 s): the camera
     lowers 0.4 m, steam card fills the view at 60 % opacity, the realm music drops to its pad layer
     (`G.audio.layer(0)` for 10 s, then back), hearts refill (`hp = maxHp`), `set:ss40.soaked`.
   - **After `ss40.done`**: `[E] Sit · Bath` stays; it refills hearts **once per visit** (a runtime flag cleared on
     `EV.REALM` leaving Kettle Isle; not saved, not an economy source). Steam rises from all five basins (an
     instanced steam card per basin, the shared `fx` puff, 1 draw); two capybara bathers (`rodent` rig, `tail: none`,
     Soakley's palette without the towel, look records `bather_1`, `bather_2`) and, from 10:00 to 16:00, one Wickling
     (the S-088 look) sit in baths 2 and 4.
   - Lines: `ke.ss40.soakley.1` "Cold, scalding, cold, scalding, lukewarm. Lukewarm is the worst. Nobody complains
     about lukewarm. That's how you know." · `ke.ss40.soakley.2` "There. Now you know what calm is for. It's for
     baths." · gauge Look lines `ke.ss40.gauge.cold` "The frog gauge is blue. Brr.", `.luke` "The frog gauge is
     blue-green. Lukewarm. The worst.", `.right` "The frog gauge is green. Just right.", `.hot` "The frog gauge is red.
     Hot hot hot!" (Pitch's tic on a gauge is fine: it is her painted label, not her speech; if the lint flags it, use
     "Scalding.") · journal `.title` "Soakley's Baths", `.s1` "Talk to Soakley at the bathhouse", `.s2` "Set all five
     baths to just right", `.s3` "Sit in a bath", `.open` "Five baths, five temperatures, none of them right. Soakley is
     unbothered. Soakley is always unbothered.", `.done` "The baths are right. I sat in one. I took no notes. This is a
     first." · hints `h1` ["The one with the towel on his head.", "Talk to Soakley at the bathhouse desk on the
     Bathhouse Terraces."], `h2` ["Each bath has a fire and a tap. Two baths share a pipe.", "Light the braziers in
     baths 1, 3 and 5 with the Sling; set valves to half, shut, open, half, half from west to east."], `h3` ["You've
     earned it.", "Sit in any bath with {interact}."]
3. **Dialogue priority** (`js/content/dialogue/kettle.js`): the ss-40 start entry at priority 40 with
   `quest:ss-40.start`; progress lines at 40; after done, one idle line: Soakley `ke.soakley.idle.ss40` "Lovely baths.
   Lovely day. No rush, Pip."
4. **Economy**: add ss-40 (35) to the side-story table that `js/content/economy.js` sums.

#### Assets
- `kettle_bath_gauge` (code-built in the Kettle props module, ≤ 200 tris): a squat frog on a post whose round belly is a
  dial (a disc with a needle); belly colour from the reading. ART BRIEF: silhouette a sitting frog statue on a 0.3 m
  post, belly dial; 0.5 m; palette brass `#c99a4a`, frog `#8fc7a0`, dial cream `#f4d1a6`; toon metal; 200 / 800 tris;
  needle rotates; image prompt: preamble + `card` + "a little brass frog statue on a post whose round belly is a
  temperature dial with a needle, for a cosy hot-spring bathhouse. Palette: #c99a4a #8fc7a0 #f4d1a6 #e66f4f." 3D
  prompt: "stylised low-poly brass frog gauge on a post, belly dial with needle, hand-painted, under 800 triangles,
  0.5 m". Fallback: cylinder post + sphere + coloured disc.
- `kettle_bath_valve` (≤ 120 tris): a brass wheel valve on a pipe stub; 0.6 m. Fallback torus + cylinder.
- No generated images in this step.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all lines except "Frozen home island" (no home change) and "Precache" (unless a file is added to
  the site; then keep it).
- ss-40 appears in Field Notes once `ability:sling` holds; it has a journal entry, a two-part hint per step and a world
  mark (steam, bathers) that appears within 10 s of its last step.
- `js/content/kettle-baths.js` unit test: the start state reads cold, scalding, cold, scalding, lukewarm; the bible
  solution reads five "just right"; changing bath 2's valve changes bath 3's reading.
- Sitting in a bath after ss-40 refills hearts once per Kettle visit; a second sit in the same visit says
  `ke.ss40.soakley.full` "You're as soaked as you'll get, Pip. Come back another day. No rush." and heals nothing.
- Kettle's realm prints unchanged; content lint green; `tests/economy.test.js` passes with the new reward.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (new: `tests/kettle-baths.test.js`; extended `tests/content.test.js`, `tests/economy.test.js`).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side-kettle` (append case `ss-40`): from `ch-kettle-end.json`, set the five baths through the
  debug hook `G.debug.kettleBaths.set([...])` (add it: it drives the real prop handlers), assert `ss40.right` within 2 s
  of the last change and the props lock; sit, assert full hearts; sit again, assert no heal; leave Kettle and return,
  sit, assert a heal.
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- ss-39 (S-236a). Steam Hop and its bath towel gift (S-237b).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; S-236a is untouched. The bath props are appended `placeSideMarks` records (prints unchanged) and the
rewritable `ss40.*` settings flags are read by nothing else.

<a id="s-237a"></a>

### S-237a · feat: side story 41 Miso's Supplier and the Sling Gallery

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-232, S-030, S-191a · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-41](../bible/side-content.md#ss-41), [side-content#act-sling-gallery](../bible/side-content.md#act-sling-gallery),
[cast#miso](../bible/cast.md#miso), [cast#master-scoria](../bible/cast.md#master-scoria),
[charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person), [systems#tool-sling](../bible/systems.md#tool-sling)

INDEX's S-237 is split: this part ships ss-41 and the Sling Gallery; S-237b ships Steam Hop (see Index changes).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**. Miso has sold Scoria's swords for twenty
years without meeting him; meeting him reveals Kinako's secret kindness and opens the twins' peace (S-241). The Sling
Gallery is the Ember Sling's skill toy and pays one of the six activity heart petals.

#### What
1. **ss-41 quest** (`js/content/quests/kettle.js`, owner `miso`, n 141, unlock `['realm:kettle.visited',
   'quest:ss-03>=3']`, reward `gems:+30`, `set:ss41.done`, minutes 15). Steps:
   - `ask` · `ke.ss41.s1` "Talk to Miso at her shop" · done `flag:ss41.asked` on `TALK_END`.
   - `sail` · `ke.ss41.s2` "Sail Miso to Kettle Isle" · done `flag:ss41.arrived` on `REALM`. Set by the `quests`
     module when Pip enters `kettle` while ss-41 is at `sail` (any arrival: chart sailing, root-gate).
   - `meet` · `ke.ss41.s3` "Introduce Miso to Master Scoria" · done `flag:ss41.met` on `SCENE_END`.
2. **Miso aboard.** While ss-41 is at `meet`, every time Pip arrives on Kettle Isle a Miso NPC spawns 2 m beside Pip's
   arrival spawn and acts as a **chapter partner**: reuse the partner behaviour S-191 built for Digby (F27 path +
   [charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person): stays within 40° of the view direction and 2-8 m ahead
   while Pip moves toward the goal; waits, facing Pip, when Pip stops or turns away for more than 3 s). If that
   behaviour lives inside Digby's code, extract it into `js/partner.js` (`startPartner(npcId, goalAnchor)`,
   `stopPartner()`) in this PR with Digby using it unchanged (the ch13 chapter test is the regression net). Goal:
   `w:forge`. Miso never enters a fight: if an enemy targets Pip within 12 m she waits behind him; enemies never
   target her. On home Miso stays at her shop as always (only one realm is loaded, so she is never in two places).
3. **Scene `ss41-meet`** (20 s, `replay: true`) when the partner reaches 4 m from `w:forge`: Scoria at the anvil turns;
   lines in order: `ke.ss41.scoria.0` "...Good." (he looks Miso up and down) · `ke.ss41.miso.1` · `ke.ss41.scoria.1`
   · `ke.ss41.miso.2`; then `set:ss41.met`, the partner stops, Miso walks to the forge door and fades (she "sails home
   with Nell"). Within 10 s: the world mark.
4. **Lines** (`js/text/en/ke.js`):
   - `ke.ss41.miso.1` "Twenty years I've sold Scoria's swords and never met the bear~ Kinako always says he's 'very busy'. Mrrow."
   - `ke.ss41.scoria.1` "Your brother? Pays me full price. Sells to you at cost. Has done for twenty years. Told me not to tell. I'm terrible at not telling."
   - `ke.ss41.miso.2` "...At cost? Twenty years? ...That silly cat~ That silly, silly cat~"
   - `ke.ss41.miso.ask` (the start line at her shop) "Pip~ Next time you sail to Kettle Isle, take me? I want to meet the bear who makes my swords. Before I'm too old to be shy~"
   - Journal `.title` "Miso's Supplier"; `.open` "Miso has never met her supplier. I have never met my publisher. We have bonded."; `.done` "Kinako sold her swords at cost for twenty years. Miso said 'silly cat' nine times. I counted fondly."
   - Hints: `h1` ["Miso wants something. Unusual.", "Talk to Miso at her shop in Sprout Village."]; `h2` ["Miso doesn't sail alone.", "Sail to Kettle Isle from Nell's Jetty; Miso comes aboard."]; `h3` ["She'll lead; you follow.", "Walk with Miso to Master Scoria's forge in Forgefall."].
5. **World mark** (`ss41.done`): a stamped card "Forged at Forgefall ✿" (decal 0.3 × 0.2 m) on Miso's counter, attached
   as a child mesh of the shop group (no collider, home prints unchanged; it is not a generator item); in Scoria's forge
   interior a framed receipt on the wall, `[E] Look · Receipt`: `ke.ss41.look.receipt` "Sword no. 1, sold to Miso,
   twenty years ago. Paid in full. By Kinako." Miso's idle line after: `home.npc.miso.idle.ss41` "Scoria's forge smells
   of honey and coal. I didn't know a bear could be shy~ He is~".
6. **Sling Gallery activity** (`act-sling-gallery`, quest n 211, kind `activity`, owner `scoria`, unlock
   `ability:sling` and `realm:kettle.visited`; steps are its three tiers). Files: `js/activities/sling-gallery.js`
   (runtime) and `js/content/activities/sling-gallery.js` (pure data + scoring, unit-tested).
   - **Start**: a target board on a post in the forge yard nearest walkable to (−150, −50) (`placeSideMarks`):
     `[E] Use · Sling Gallery` shows best score and the three tiers, then a 3-2-1 chime; the realm's spawner pauses
     (spawn cap 0) for the round; Pip's position is held to a 3 m firing line (leaving it ends the round with no
     result).
   - **Targets** on the Forge Ledge cliff (anchor (−120, −55)) facing the firing line, all `bell_target`-style tin
     shapes hit by an ember (the Sling's existing hit test):
     - **Stars** (1 point): 6 rails at 10, 13, 16, 19, 22 and 25 m from the firing line, each 12 m long, carrying 2
       tin stars (0.6 m) ping-ponging at 1.5, 1.8, 2.1, 2.4, 2.7 and 3.0 m/s. A hit star swings down with a clang and
       stands back up after 3 s.
     - **Moons** (2 points): 8 moon slots on the cliff face; one moon pops up every 3.0 s for 1.5 s, in the fixed
       slot order 3, 7, 1, 5, 8, 2, 6, 4, repeating (20 pops per round). A pop-up is preceded by a 0.3 s rattle and a
       glint (sound has a visual twin).
     - **The golden bell** (5 points): crosses the top of the yard at 25 m once per round, from t 40 s to t 48 s,
       left to right.
   - **Round**: 60 s, timer top-centre (`m:ss.t`), score top-right. Missed shots cost nothing. Score = sum.
   - **Tiers** (each pays once, the first time): **25** → heart petal (`collect.petal['act-sling-gallery']`, the shared
     petal item-get); **40** → set `act.sling.t2`: Kinako's `quick_draw` costs **150** instead of 300 (her shop price
     reads `act.sling.t2`; if Kinako does not stock `quick_draw` yet, add the stock row from
     [systems#economy](../bible/systems.md#economy) in this PR); **55** → the sling-shot medal `give:nook_gift_slingmedal` (gift item, kind `furnishing`; listed in Items under Keepsakes until S-302a puts it on the Nook's trophy stand).
   - Best score: flag `act.sling.best` (registered kind `counter`, written as `max(old, new)`).
   - Lines: `ke.act.sling.scoria.1` "Stars, moons and the bell. The bell's hardest. I made it hard. I'm proud of
     that." · `.t1` "Good." · `.t2` "Again. ...No. Good. Tell Kinako I said half price." · `.t3` "Clang. That's all I
     have to say. Clang." · `.end` "{n} points. Again?" (the shared "Again?" wording from
     [charter#tone](../bible/charter.md#tone); never "failed").
7. **Economy**: ss-41 30 gems; the Sling Gallery pays no gems (its tiers are a petal and a discount); the `quick_draw`
   discount is recorded in the ledger as a reduced sink (300 → 150 when `act.sling.t2`).
8. **Debug**: `G.debug.gallery.start()`, `G.debug.gallery.score(n)` (awards tiers as if scored),
   `G.debug.gallery.bot(on)` (auto-aims at the highest-value live target with the real draw and cooldown).

#### Assets
- `kettle_gallery_targets` (code-built, one instanced type for stars and one for moons, ≤ 60 tris each; bell reuses
  `landmark_bell` from S-227 scaled 0.8): painted tin star/moon on a hinge. ART BRIEF: silhouette flat tin cut-outs on
  hinged stalks; 0.6 m; palette tin `#c8ccd4`, paint `#ffd36b` (stars) / `#f4d1a6` (moons), rail `#6b4a2e`; toon metal;
  60 / 300 tris; swings down 90° on a hit. Image prompt: preamble + `sheet-items` N = 3 + "a painted tin star target on
  a hinge, a painted tin crescent moon target, a little golden bell target, fairground shooting-gallery toys made by a
  bear blacksmith. Palette: #c8ccd4 #ffd36b #f4d1a6 #6b4a2e." 3D prompt: "stylised low-poly tin star and moon
  shooting-gallery targets on hinges, hand-painted, under 300 triangles each". Fallback: the code shapes.
- `kettle_gallery_board` (≤ 100 tris): start board on a post with a painted bullseye; fallback box + disc.
- `decal_forged_card` (generated `decal`, 256², ≤ 20 KB, `realm: home`): "a small cream card with a stamped round seal of
  a hammer and a flower, ink-stamped, for a shop counter. Palette: #f4d1a6 #e66f4f #3b3540 #8fc7a0." Process:
  white-to-alpha one-liner from [art-audio#pipeline](../bible/art-audio.md#pipeline), `export_sprites.py` at 256, webp
  q82. Fallback: a cream canvas card with a red circle.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all lines; "Frozen home island" holds because the counter card is a child mesh of the existing shop
  group (no generator list touched; `world-prints.json` unchanged).
- ss-41 completes from a Kettle-ready save: Miso walks ahead on Kettle Isle per the partner rule, the scene plays, the
  card appears on Miso's counter within 10 s of the scene ending; ss-03's step 4 condition (`ss41.done`) now holds.
- The ch13 chapter test still passes if the partner code was extracted.
- Sling Gallery: a 60 s round runs with the spawner paused; tiers pay once each; `act.sling.best` only rises; the bot
  scores ≥ 55 (so every tier is reachable); `quick_draw` costs 150 at Kinako's after tier 2.
- Unit test of the target schedule: 20 moon pops per round in the stated order, the bell crosses once, the maximum
  score with perfect play is ≥ 70.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (new `tests/sling-gallery.test.js` for the pure schedule and tier rules; content, economy and quest
  tests).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side-kettle` (extend S-236a's suite): ss-41 run from the Kettle fixture with
  `G.debug.realm('kettle', 'jetty')`, assert the partner stays 2-8 m ahead for ≥ 80 % of sampled frames while the bot
  walks toward `w:forge`; the scene; the mark. Gallery: `G.debug.gallery.bot(true)` round, assert score ≥ 55, petal
  given once, `act.sling.t2` set, a second round pays nothing.
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch13` (if the partner was extracted).
- `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (one new decal).

#### Out of scope
- Steam Hop (S-237b). ss-03 part 2 (S-241). Kinako's other stock.

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR. The partner extraction is the only refactor; if the ch13 test fails, keep Digby's original code and
duplicate the partner logic for Miso instead (note it in the PR).

<a id="s-237b"></a>

### S-237b · feat: Steam Hop, gliding up the bathhouse vents

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-237a, S-102 · Size: S (<200) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#act-steam-hop](../bible/side-content.md#act-steam-hop), [side-content#nook-gifts](../bible/side-content.md#nook-gifts),
[atlas-far#kettle-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-pois), [systems#tool-glider](../bible/systems.md#tool-glider)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**. A short timed glide course owned by Soakley,
paying a heart petal and the bath towel for Pip's Nook; it reuses the S-102 updraft gate.

#### What
1. **Course** (`placeSideMarks` on Kettle Isle): **8 steam vents** at the atlas anchors (−50, 110), (−35, 100),
   (−20, 92), (−5, 86), (10, 82), (25, 80), (40, 84), (52, 92) (nearest walkable within 4 m on the terrace there),
   a start sign at the bathhouse (`w:bathhouse`, nearest walkable 6 m south of it), and a **finish bell** on a post on the
   top terrace (y ≈ 22), nearest walkable to (45, 70), ≥ 4 m from the shoulder-tube mouth (40, 60).
2. **Vents** are the S-102 `updraft` gate object with a pulse: `pulse: { on: 2.0, off: 1.0, phase: 0.375 * i }`
   (vent i = 0..7). While puffing, a gliding Pip inside its r 1.5 m column rises at the updraft speed (6 m/s) to
   **5 m above the vent**; while off, the column is a soft wisp (no lift). A puff shows a white steam card and a
   "fssh" sound (twin). If the updraft gate has no pulse option, add it (`pulse` in the gate's data; the solver treats
   a pulsing updraft as an updraft).
3. **Run**: `[E] Use · Steam Hop` at the start sign: 3-2-1, timer top-centre; spawner paused; a fall below the terrace
   Pip last stood on for 1.0 s puts him back there (0.4 s fade, no damage); the timer keeps running. `[E] Ring · Steam
   bell` at the finish ends the run. Leaving the terraces region ends it with no result.
4. **Tiers** (quest `act-steam-hop`, n 212, owner `soakley`, unlock `ability:glide` and `realm:kettle.visited`):
   **< 90 s** heart petal (`collect.petal['act-steam-hop']`); **< 70 s** gift `nook_gift_towel` (item, kind
   `furnishing`, shown on the Nook's wall S peg (0.8, 4.6, 1.6) if the Nook shelf system exists, else listed in Items);
   **< 55 s** the towel ribbon `give:nook_gift_ribbon` (gift item, kind `furnishing`; listed in Items under Keepsakes until S-302a puts it on the Nook's trophy stand). Best time: flag `act.steam.best` in ms (register as **rewritable**, kind `record`, kept as
   `min(old, new)`; add the kind to the flag registry with a one-line doc; this is the one exception and the test
   asserts it never increases).
5. **Lines**: `ke.act.steam.soakley.1` "Ride the steam to the bell. Take your time. Or don't. The bell will wait.
   Bells are good at waiting." · `.t1` "Lovely. Here, a petal. No rush spending it." · `.t2` "Lovely, lovely. Have a
   towel. It's a good towel." · `.t3` "Faster than steam. I didn't know that was allowed. Lovely." · `.end` "{time}.
   One more go?".
6. **Debug**: `G.debug.steamHop.bot(on)` (glides vent to vent on the rhythm), `G.debug.steamHop.finish(ms)`.

#### Assets
- `kettle_steam_vent` (code-built, ≤ 120 tris): a ring of warm stones round a hole with a copper lip; puff = shared steam
  card. ART BRIEF: 1.6 m ring; palette stone `#3b3540`, copper `#c9873a`, steam `#f4f0ec`; image prompt: preamble +
  `card` + "a round ring of warm dark stones around a steaming hole with a copper lip, a puff of soft white steam rising.
  Palette: #3b3540 #c9873a #f4f0ec #8fc7a0."; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly steam vent ring of stones with copper lip,
  under 400 triangles". Fallback: torus + cylinder.
- `kettle_steam_bell`: reuse `landmark_bell` (S-227) on a 2 m post; fallback the same.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen home island".
- The bot completes the course in **≤ 50 s** (so every tier is reachable); a recorded `T_bot` is in the PR body. If it
  is slower, adjust vent phases (never the tier times).
- Tiers pay once; `act.steam.best` never increases; a fall returns Pip to his last terrace without damage.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (flag registry test for the `record` kind; content lint).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side-kettle`: bot run, assert completion ≤ 50 s, petal and towel given, a second slower run
  leaves `act.steam.best` unchanged; a forced fall (`G.debug.warp` off a terrace edge) restores the terrace within 1.5 s.
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Kettle's Dash course (S-234). Bath puzzle (S-236b).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the vents are a side-mark pass and an optional updraft `pulse` field defaulting to always-on.

<a id="s-238"></a>

### S-238 · feat: the Snowglobe rooms 1-10 under Frostcap: melting ice and sliding blocks

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-227, S-053, S-074 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm `snowglobe` hidden until S-239a · Bible:
[dungeons#o1](../bible/dungeons.md#o1), [dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities),
[dungeons#solver](../bible/dungeons.md#solver), [dungeons#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules),
[atlas-home#bell-targets](../bible/atlas-home.md#bell-targets), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table),
[art-audio#dungeon-textures](../bible/art-audio.md#dungeon-textures)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**. The Ember Sling's home find: a ten-room
optional dungeon under Frostcap whose signature is ice that slides until it bumps into something. It houses
Great-Aunt Flurry (S-239a).

#### What
1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/snowglobe.js`: copy the composite map, legend extension, room table, entities, chests,
   spawns and hints from [dungeons#o1](../bible/dungeons.md#o1) **verbatim** (the S-049 map test
   `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` compares the repo data with the bible's map). Fields: `id: 'snowglobe'`, `name: 'The
   Snowglobe'`, `kind: 'dungeon'`, `tile: 2`, `palette: 'snowglobe'`, `music: 'snowglobe'`, `light: { ambient: 0.75,
   torches: true, sun: false, tod: 0.5 }`, `parent: 'home'`, `door: 'snowglobe_wall'`, `needsOnEntry: ['sling',
   'lantern']`. Name card line 2: "beneath Frostcap Peaks".
2. **Registry** (`js/realms/index.js`): `snowglobe` with `hidden: true` (S-239a unhides it when Flurry lands), bundle
   `postgame` (the shared optional-dungeon bundle of [art-audio#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles); if S-194 used
   a different bundle id for the Undercap, use the same one).
3. **Portal** `home:snowglobe` (walk-in into the cave mouth behind the ice wall, `needs: 'flag:gate:home:icewall'`)
   on the ice-wall record of `placeBellTargets` (S-227). If S-227 named the flag `find:home:ice-wall`
   ([atlas-home#bell-targets](../bible/atlas-home.md#bell-targets) spelling), keep that name and alias it in the flag
   registry; do not set both. The pass's positions do not change (the portal is a record on the existing item).
   Exit `X` tiles return to the spawn `snowglobe_out` 2 m outside the melted arch.
4. **Ice walls (`A`)** inside o1 use the same rule as the overworld gate ([systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates)):
   **3 ember hits**, each shrinks it a third, melts in 1.5 s, for good (`S.dungeons.snowglobe.open['r3:A1']`). (This
   follows systems.md over dungeons.md's "one ember"; record it in the PR.) Before melting, Swing on it plays "Brr.
   Something warm might help."
5. **Push-ice (`Z`)** in `js/dungeon-mod.js`: a push (Pip walks into it 0.35 s) sends it sliding 1 tile per 0.12 s
   until the next tile is a wall, pillar, block, chest, closed door, another `Z` or the room's edge; it stops **on** a
   `P` hold plate (which then holds); sliding onto a `_` pit it drops in over 0.4 s and that pit tile becomes **ice
   floor** for good (`S.dungeons.snowglobe.state['r5:fill:<col>,<row>'] = true`); a reset lever `r` returns the
   room's `Z` blocks to their start tiles and un-fills pits that ice filled in that room (0.6 s fade). Push-ice is not
   pushable while a wave is live (rule shared with `b`). Positions save in `S.dungeons.snowglobe.blocks`.
6. **Solver** (`js/realms/dungeon-compile.js`): model `Z` as slide-until-blocked with stop-on-`P` and pit fill, plus
   ice walls needing `sling`. The solver must prove the bible's solver notes path:
   r1 → r2 (Z S) → r3 (`&`, `c1`, melt) → r4 (Z1 S; Z2 E, S; `k1`) → r5 (Z1 W; Z2 S, W; `%`) → unlock → r6 (melt)
   → r7 (Z S, E) → r8 (melt) → r9 (`K`, `c2`) → r8 → B → r10, and prove no soft-lock (every room with `Z` has its
   lever). Replay the path as a script test.
7. **Room 10** is built (arena floor, `+` pedestal hidden, `M` spawn) but **empty**: no boss and the petal does not
   appear until S-239a. The boss door `B` opens with the Root Key as normal. A sign in r10 is not needed; Flurry is a
   snoring quilt mound prop (code-built: a 5 m dome under a patchwork quilt, `nightGlow` 0) with `[E] Look · Quilt`
   `home.o1.look.quilt` "A mountain of quilt, snoring. Something very big and very fluffy is underneath. Not yet."
8. **Signs and lines** (`js/text/en/home.js`): `home.o1.sign.r1` "Snowglobe. Please shake gently. — F."
   (Frostbun's squiggly paw letters, the sign font) · hint pairs exactly from the bible:
   1. ["Ice doesn't stop till it bumps into something.", "Push the ice block and it slides all the way. Find the
   pillar or plate that will stop it where you need it."] 2. ["Pits don't mind a bit of ice.", "In the Frozen Pond,
   slide both ice blocks into the same row of the pit to make a path."] 3. ["Ice walls hate embers.", "Fling an ember
   at the blue ice wall to melt it."]
9. **Waves** per the map's `e` tiles: r3 Snowpuff ×3; r6 Snowpuff ×3; r8 Dozy Bat ×2; r9 Snowpuff ×2 (home species,
   KEEP stats). At most 4 alive per room.
10. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/snowglobe.json` written in this PR; S-239a may refresh it once.
11. **Music**: register the `snowglobe` variant `{ from: 'depths', transpose: 2, voices: { lead: 'glock' } }`
    ([art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)); snow particles drift **upward** and settle on the
    ceiling (the shared particle card with negative gravity, ≤ 1 draw).
12. **Completion**: the Snowglobe's 2 gem chests (`snowglobe:c1`, `snowglobe:c2`, 40 gems each) count in home's
    "Dungeon chests" row; regenerate `js/realms/stats.js` with `tools/realm-stats.mjs`.

#### Assets
- `tex_snowglobe` → use the dungeon-texture keys `wall_ice` and `wall_snowbrick`
  ([art-audio#dungeon-textures](../bible/art-audio.md#dungeon-textures)), 512 + 256 webp, ≤ 60 + 20 KB, bundle
  `postgame`. Prompts: preamble + `tile` + "Clear blue ice with trapped bubbles. Palette: #cfeaf7 #9fdcff #e9f6ff
  #5a7fa6." and "Rounded snow bricks. Palette: #cfeaf7 #e9f6ff #ffffff #5a7fa6." Process: `python3 -I
  tools/make_seamless.py <in> <out> --blend 64`, then resize to 512/256 and webp q82. Fallback: canvas noise
  `#cfeaf7` / brick grid `#e9f6ff` on `#cfeaf7`.
- `int_push_ice` (code-built, 120 tris): 1.9 m rounded ice cube, `#9fdcff` at 70 % opacity, white frosted rim, an
  etched snowflake decal inside. ART BRIEF per [dungeons#o1](../bible/dungeons.md#o1) "Art (o1)"; 3D prompt "Stylised
  low-poly rounded ice cube with frosted edges and a snowflake inside, hand-painted, under 480 triangles, 1.9 m".
  Fallback: the kit block tinted `#9fdcff`.
- `int_ice_wall` (code-built): 2 × 3 m glassy slab with frosted edges, melts in three steps to a puddle decal. Fallback:
  transparent blue box.
- `int_yarn_ball` (code-built, 900 tris): the 6 m sky-blue yarn ball with two needles over r8's pillars. ART BRIEF
  verbatim from the bible (silhouette, 6 m, `#8fd3ff` `#5a7fa6` needles `#c9a46b`, fuzzy matte, 2,000 tris painted,
  image prompt "<style line> A giant ball of sky-blue knitting wool with two wooden needles in a snowy cosy cave, tiny
  mittens hanging on strings.", 3D prompt "Stylised low-poly giant yarn ball with loose loops and two knitting needles,
  hand-painted, game-ready, under 2,000 triangles, 6 m."). Fallback: blue sphere.
- `int_quilt_mound` (code-built, ≤ 300 tris): 5 m dome under a patchwork quilt (`#e88a8a`, `#fff3e0`, `#9fdcff`
  squares as vertex colours), rising and falling 0.1 m at 0.25 Hz. Fallback: the dome.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints unchanged: the portal is a record on S-227's ice-wall item; `world.test.js`
  "pass changes nothing else" tests still pass.
- `bun test`: the compiler builds `snowglobe`; the map test equals the bible map; the solver proves exit, boss door,
  every chest (`&`, `%`, `K`, `c1`, `c2`) reachable and no soft-lock; the solver-notes path replays.
- In-page: melting the overworld ice wall (3 hits) shows the portal; entering shows the name card once; push-ice slides
  and stops as specified, fills the r5 pit, and the lever resets it; a dizzy Pip wakes at `@` with puzzle state kept.
- Realm suite: `snowglobe` entry: leaks, spawn walkable, ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles in every room view,
  generation ≤ 0.15 s.
- The realm stays `hidden: true`; nothing in r10 can be fought or collected yet.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (dungeon map test, solver test with push-ice and pit fill, prints, `stats.test.js`).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (or the suite S-059 created for dungeons; add a `snowglobe` case): melt the wall via
  `G.debug.give('sling')` + three ember hits at the anchor, walk in, run the solver path with
  `G.debug.dungeon.push(id, dir)` (add the hook if missing: it drives the real push), assert room states.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (the S-022 suite with the new realm row).
- `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Great-Aunt Flurry and the petal (S-239a); ss-12 (S-239b). The Undercap (S-194, shipped).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR. The realm is hidden; the portal shows only after the player melts the wall, and leads into a finished
dungeon with an empty last room (the quilt line explains it).

<a id="s-239a"></a>

### S-239a · feat: Great-Aunt Flurry, the Snowglobe's sleeping boss

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-238, S-045, S-074 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none (unhides `snowglobe`) · Bible:
[creatures#boss-flurry](../bible/creatures.md#boss-flurry), [dungeons#o1](../bible/dungeons.md#o1),
[cast#great-aunt-flurry](../bible/cast.md#great-aunt-flurry), [creatures#boss-framework](../bible/creatures.md#boss-framework),
[creatures#balance-targets](../bible/creatures.md#balance-targets), [systems#hearts](../bible/systems.md#hearts)

**Decisions** (bible files disagree; this step takes):
- Height **5.0 m** (creatures, art-audio brief index) over cast's 5.2 m: the fallback is `boss_frost` scaled
  5.0 / 3.4 = **1.47**.
- Look: cast's palette (rose-and-cream shawl `#e88a8a` / `#fff3e0`, frost-blue nightcap `#9fdcff` with a white pompom,
  lavender-grey ear tips `#b8b4d0`, needles `#c99a4a`); the long red-and-white scarf (`#e0574f` / `#f8fbff`) from
  creatures is the scarf she is knitting, hanging from her needles.
- Where she lives after the bout: asleep in r10 of the Snowglobe (creatures: "naps in the Snowglobe"; cast: "awake
  and talkable in the Snowglobe"). Her daytime Summit visit is ss-12's world mark and ships in S-239b.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**. The Snowglobe (S-238) gets its sleeper: an
optional bout with a kind reason (she kept the globe cold so the snowflakes keep) and one of the 24 heart petals
(`snowglobe:p1`). The realm is whole once she lands, so it is unhidden here. (Split from S-239: the boss, her model,
looks, lines and after spot here; side story 12 and its rewards in S-239b.)

#### What
1. **Boss def** `js/boss/flurry.js`, registered in `js/boss/defs.js` as `BOSS_DEFS['home.flurry']` with save key
   **`snowglobe:flurry`** ([systems#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids)):

   ```js
   { id: 'home.flurry', key: 'snowglobe:flurry', name: 'Great-Aunt Flurry',
     title: 'Snoozing Matriarch of the Snowglobe', kind: 'bout',
     model: 'boss_flurry', height: 5.0, r: 2.0, keepOut: 2.6, room: 3.6, talkAt: 2.8,
     hp: 90, enrageAt: 0.5, cool: [1.6, 2.6],
     arena: { anchor: 'snowglobe:M', r: 15, shape: 'round', seal: 'bossdoor', wallBonk: true },
     phases: [
       { below: 1.0, table: [['avalanche', 45], ['icicles', 40], ['shuffle', 15]] },
       { below: 0.5, table: [['avalanche', 35], ['icicles', 35], ['doubleroll', 30]] } ],
     moves: {
       avalanche: { telegraph: 'stripe', w: 3.2, wind: 0.9, lockAt: 0.5, speed: 7, recover: 0.4, open: 1.6, dmg: 1, threats: 1 },
       icicles:   { telegraph: 'circle', n: 5, nP2: 7, r: 1.0, wind: 1.0, act: 0.3, recover: 0.6, open: 0, dmg: 1, threats: 1 },
       shuffle:   { telegraph: 'none', act: 1.2, recover: 0.3, open: 0, dmg: 0, threats: 0 },  // she shuffles to face Pip
       doubleroll:{ telegraph: 'stripe', w: 3.2, wind: [0.9, 0.7], speed: 7, recover: 0.4, open: 1.6, dmg: 1, threats: 1 },
       nap:       { forcedAt: 0.5, once: true, act: 6.0, open: 6.0, shrug: false } },
     cross: { speedK: 1.4, cool: [1.0, 1.8] },
     handover: { lines: ['home.flurry.ho.1', 'home.flurry.ho.2', 'home.flurry.ho.3'],
                 reward: ['give:scarf', 'set:snowglobe.petalUp'], sit: 'dizzy' },
     after: { realm: 'snowglobe', anchor: 'snowglobe:M', pose: 'sleep', talk: ['home.flurry.after.1'] },
     plushie: null, dream: null,
     rematch: { hpK: 1.25, enrage: 0.75, gems: 60, verb: 'Wake' },   // round(1.25 × 90) = 113 HP
     bestiary: { card: 'card_boss_flurry', lore: 'home.bestiary.flurry.lore', tip: 'home.bestiary.flurry.tip' } }
   ```
   - `shuffle` is a 1.2 s non-damaging turn so the table always contains a quiet beat; the creatures move table has no
     third P1 move and the move runner needs one to keep cool times readable. It shows no telegraph and is not a threat.
   - **avalanche**: she tucks into a ball (whole-body squash to a sphere over 0.5 s, deep rumble `sfx boss_rumble`); a
     3.2 m stripe is drawn from her centre through Pip's position to the dome wall; the stripe's aim **locks 0.4 s
     before** the roll (`lockAt = wind − 0.4`); she rolls along it at 7 m/s and bonks the glass: she unrolls dazed for
     **1.6 s gold** (`boss.opening = true`, HUD "Now!").
   - **icicles**: a huge yawn (`pose: 'yawn'`, 0.4 s); 5 circles r 1.0 m (7 in phase 2) are drawn at Pip's position plus
     four (six) points at 2.5-6 m from him chosen by the boss RNG (`new RNG(seedOf('snowglobe') * 97 + 3)`, no two
     circles closer than 2.2 m, all inside r 14); icicles (cone 0.4 × 1.4 m, `#bfe3ff`) crack visibly at the dome
     8 m above each circle for the 1.0 s tell, then fall in 0.3 s. **The Sling:** an ember hit on an icicle during its
     tell melts it (it drips, its circle fades in 0.15 s and never damages). Hit test: sphere r 0.5 m at the icicle.
   - **nap**: forced once when HP first drops to ≤ 45: she sits, says `home.flurry.nap` "Five more minutes...", and
     snores for **6 s gold with no shrug** (every hit lands). Then she wakes **cross**: `speedK` 1.4, cool 1.0-1.8 s, the
     phase 2 table (this is the one escalation, rule 5).
   - **doubleroll** (phase 2): as avalanche; a second stripe is drawn the moment the first roll ends (wind 0.7), aimed
     at Pip then; after the second bonk, 1.6 s gold.
   - Live threats ≤ 2: icicles and a roll never overlap (the move runner holds the next move until the circles have
     fallen).
2. **Placement** (`js/realms/dungeons/snowglobe.js`, S-238's file): r10's `M` (10, 30) spawns the boss asleep under
   the S-238 quilt mound. The quilt line now reads `home.o1.look.quilt` "A mountain of quilt, snoring. Something very
   big and very fluffy is underneath." and the prompt becomes `[E] Wake · Quilt`; waking her starts the intro (2.4 s,
   name card "Great-Aunt Flurry" / "Snoozing Matriarch of the Snowglobe"; the boss door `B` drops). Dizzy rule: Pip wakes
   at `@` with puzzle state kept; she naps again at full HP.
3. **The petal.** On the handover the `+` snow pedestal at (10, 38) rises 0.8 m over 1.2 s with the heart petal on it
   (walk over: `collect:petal:snowglobe:p1`, the shared petal toast). The pedestal stays up; a collected petal never
   reappears.
4. **Text** (`js/text/en/home.js`):
   - Handover: `home.flurry.ho.1` "Mmf. Who's poking me? ...Oh. A small one." · `.ho.2` "Frostbun sent you? That dear
     bun. I've been asleep since the big snow, keeping the globe cold so the snowflakes keep." · `.ho.3` "Here. I knitted
     while I dreamt. It's a scarf. It may be rather long."
   - `home.flurry.nap` "Five more minutes..." · `home.flurry.after.1` "Zzz... purl one... zzz." · `home.flurry.after.2`
     (awake after a rematch) "Rrr. Visitors. In MY day visitors brought biscuits. ...where was I? Ah. Biscuits."
   - Bestiary: `home.bestiary.flurry.lore` "Great-Aunt Flurry: the source of all snow, according to Frostbun. I am not
     arguing." · `.tip` "She naps halfway. Free bonks! Then she's cross."
   - Item: `scarf` "Flurry's Scarf" desc "Very long. Very warm. Smells faintly of peppermint." (kind `key`, beat true,
     icon `icon_scarf`). (`trim_snowdrift` ships in S-239b.)
5. **Unhide**: `REALMS.snowglobe.hidden = false`. The portal `home:snowglobe` already exists (S-238).
6. **Completion / economy**: the Snowglobe petal counts in the Hearts row (`snowglobe:p1`); `snowglobe:flurry` counts in
   home's Rematches row on its first rematch win (60 gems once); regenerate `js/realms/stats.js`.
7. **Debug**: `G.debug.actors.boss('flurry')` fires named moves; `G.debug.killBoss('snowglobe:flurry')`.

#### Assets
- **`boss_flurry`** (code-built in `js/boss/flurry.js` via `js/models.js`): the `boss_frost` GLB clone scaled 1.47 with
  a `#f0f4ff` tint uniform, plus `extra` code meshes: nightcap (cone r 0.55 × 1.6 m, `#9fdcff`, bent 30° back, pompom
  sphere r 0.22 `#fbfdff`), shawl (a lathe collar r 1.4, `#e88a8a` with `#fff3e0` vertex-colour stripes), two needles
  (cylinders 0.9 m `#c99a4a`), the scarf (a ribbon strip 3 m, `#e0574f` / `#f8fbff` stripes). Extra tris ≤ 1,000. If
  the `boss_frost` GLB fails to load, the shipped `bossDef('frost')` code model takes its place, scaled the same.
  Poses: tuck-and-roll (uniform squash to a sphere 0.5 s), yawn, nap (rise and fall 0.1 m at 0.25 Hz), cross wake (a
  0.3 s shake). ART BRIEF (batch 12): silhouette a huge soft dome with two tall ears and a long drooping nightcap; 5.0
  m; palette fur `#fbfdff`, shade `#dfe9f5`, ear tips `#b8b4d0`, nose `#f5a3b5`, nightcap `#9fdcff` with pom `#fbfdff`,
  shawl `#e88a8a` / `#fff3e0`, scarf `#e0574f` / `#f8fbff`, needles `#c99a4a`; materials toon fur + knit; 1,000 extra
  code / 12,000 GLB; poses sit-upright and curled-nap (or one GLB with a `head` node). Image prompt (concept sheet,
  docs/art/ref only): PREAMBLE + `sheet` + "an enormous elderly white yeti-bunny great-aunt with
  lavender-grey ear tips, a rose-and-cream knitted shawl with knitting needles, a long frost-blue nightcap with a white
  pompom, a very long red-and-white striped scarf, sleepy grand expression." 3D prompt: "a cute chunky enormous fluffy
  white yeti-bunny grandmother, lavender ear tips, rose knitted shawl, long blue nightcap, knitting needles and a long
  striped scarf, sleepy face, hand-painted toon texture, low-poly under 12k triangles, 5.0 m."
- **`card_boss_flurry`** (generated `card-beast`, 384² webp ≤ 45 KB, `realm: home` core per creatures asset index): PREAMBLE +
  `card-beast` + "an enormous fluffy white yeti-bunny grandmother in a long blue nightcap, dozing with knitting needles and a
  very long red-and-white scarf, inside a giant snow globe. Palette: #fbfdff #9fdcff #e88a8a #e0574f." Process
  `python3 -I tools/export_sprites.py <in> --size 384 --webp 82`. Fallback: the tinted blob card.
- **`portrait_flurry`** (generated `portrait`, 3-frame strip 1536 × 512 → sliced 512² frames neutral / dizzy / smiling,
  ≤ 120 KB, `realm: home`): subject from [cast#great-aunt-flurry](../bible/cast.md#great-aunt-flurry). Process
  `python3 -I tools/process_strip.py <in> --frames 3` then `export_sprites.py --size 512 --webp 82`. Fallback: a circle
  `#fbfdff` with "F".
- **`icon_scarf`** (128 px into the icon atlas): "STYLE. A long red-and-white striped knitted scarf as a round game icon
  on a cream disc, soft shadow". Fallback: canvas stripes.
- Icicle (cone, 12 tris, `#bfe3ff` at 80 % opacity, instanced, ≤ 7 live) and the glow card reuse the shared fx card.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints unchanged. `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/snowglobe.json` may be refreshed once in
  this PR (S-238's rule) and is then frozen.
- `tests/creatures.test.js`: `home.flurry` passes the boss grammar (every damaging move `wind ≥ 0.55`, `threats ≤ 2`, an
  `open > 0` move without a tool); HP 90; rematch HP 113 and 60 gems; `plushie` and `dream` are null.
- In-page `bosses` suite: intro card text; each move fires through `G.debug.actors.boss('flurry')`; an avalanche bonk sets
  `boss.opening` for 1.6 s; the nap fires once at ≤ 50 % with no shrug; the cross wake sets `speedK` 1.4; an ember hit
  on an icicle during its tell removes that circle; the handover plays its three lines and gives `scarf`; the petal
  pedestal rises; the Wake prompt after leaving gives a 113 HP rematch.
- Balance (`tests/balance` bots, S-047 harness): careful bot with 8 hearts, Shiny Sword and the Sling wins ≥ 90 % in
  60-120 s losing ≤ 3 hearts; careful bot with the wooden sword wins in ≤ 180 s; never-dodging bot ≤ 50 % and never
  downed before 15 s.
- `snowglobe` is no longer hidden; the Hearts row counts `snowglobe:p1`; content lint green (no line > 220 characters,
  every flag registered, every text id present).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h` first. `bun test` (creatures, content, stats, dungeon map/solver tests).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses` (new case `flurry`: from fixture `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-kettle-end.json`, give `sling`,
  `G.debug.realm('snowglobe', 'r10')`, wake, run each move, bot fight, handover, petal).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (snowglobe row unchanged budgets: ≤ 120 calls per room view with the boss present).
- `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (new card, portrait, icon).

#### Out of scope
- Side story 12, Flurry's trim Talk, the news scene, the Summit visit, the glow and the ear-warmer (S-239b). Wearing the
  Snowdrift trim (S-303). Flurry's Hundred-Year Night spot (S-288). ss-10 (S-240).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR: `snowglobe` goes back to hidden with S-238's empty r10. The boss is optional and gates nothing; the
petal is one of 24 and Completion reads totals from `stats.js`, which the revert restores.

<a id="s-239b"></a>

### S-239b · feat: side story 12 Frostbun's Great-Aunt and the Snowdrift trim

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-239a, S-170, S-078 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-12](../bible/side-content.md#ss-12), [cast#great-aunt-flurry](../bible/cast.md#great-aunt-flurry),
[systems#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests)

**Decisions** (bible files disagree; this step takes):
- Frostbun is **he** (LOCKED, Digby's shipped line; cast decision 6). The bible's ss-12 lines "Is she still little?"
  and "beside her" are written here as "Is he still little? ... He'll always be little." and "beside him".
- ss-12's world mark ("two buns sit on Frostbun's Summit, the huge one knitting") is a daytime visit: 10:00-16:00
  she sits on the Summit beside the napping Frostbun; the rest of the day she is in r10 (S-239a's after spot).
- The Snowdrift trim's colour is `#dfeaf6` (the bible names the trim but gives no hex). The trim *item* ships here;
  wearing it (the hood tint) arrives with the trim system in S-303. Until then the Items tab lists it with "Ready to
  wear once Miso's mirror arrives." (a placeholder line S-303 replaces).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**. A side story owned by a Catcher, so Frostbun
has a life after Act I: he sends Pip to wake his great-aunt, she knits Pip a hood trim, and the two buns sit together on
the Summit by day. (Split from S-239; the boss and her model are S-239a.)

#### What
1. **ss-12 Frostbun's Great-Aunt** (`js/content/quests/home.js`, id `ss-12`, n 112, owner `frostbun`, unlock
   `ability:sling`, reward `gems:+40`, `set:ss12.done`, minutes 15):

   | Step | Text id → text | Done | On |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `ask` | `home.ss12.s1` "Frostbun is worried about Great-Aunt Flurry" | `flag:ss12.asked` | `TALK_END` |
   | `melt` | `home.ss12.s2` "Melt the Snowglobe's ice wall" | `flag:gate:home:icewall` (the S-238 spelling) | `GATE` |
   | `reach` | `home.ss12.s3` "Find Great-Aunt Flurry in the Snowglobe" | `flag:snowglobe.r10` (set by `dungeon-mod` on entering r10) | `REALM` / room enter |
   | `calm` | `home.ss12.s4` "Calm Great-Aunt Flurry" | `boss:snowglobe:flurry` | `BOSS_DEFEATED` |
   | `news` | `home.ss12.s5` "Tell Frostbun" | `flag:ss12.news` | `SCENE_END` |

   - Steps already true when the quest starts complete at once (a player who melted the wall first skips `melt`).
   - **Start**: Frostbun's priority-40 entry `home.frostbun.ss12.start`, `when: ['ability:sling', '!quest:ss-12.active',
     '!quest:ss-12=done']`, wherever he is talkable (the plaza edge 18:00-21:00 from `moteNamed`, S-170); lines
     `home.ss12.frostbun.1` "Aunt Flurry. Sleeping. Too long. Under the snow. ...Wake her? Gently. She's grumpy before
     tea."; effects `quest:ss-12.start`, `set:ss12.asked`.
   - **Flurry's Talk after the handover** (priority 40, `when: ['boss:snowglobe:flurry', '!item:trim_snowdrift']`, in
     r10): `home.ss12.flurry.1` "Little Frostbun sent you? Little Frostbun! Is he still little? Don't answer. He'll
     always be little." · `home.ss12.flurry.2` "You've got a cold neck, dear. Hold still. ...There. Knitted it while I
     was dozing." with effect `give:trim_snowdrift` (item-get beat after the line).
   - **News**: scene `ss12-news` (12 s, `replay: true`). Trigger A: Talk with Frostbun at the plaza edge 18:00-21:00
     while ss-12 is at `news`. Trigger B: entering r 12 m of the Summit anchor (`W.arenas.peaks`) 07:00-18:00 while at
     `news`: Frostbun sits up from his nap. Steps: `shot orbit` on Frostbun (and Flurry, who is `spawn`ed beside him at
     the Summit for trigger B) · `say frostbun home.ss12.frostbun.2` "Aunt Flurry. Here. Awake. ...She called me a medium
     bun. I'm a LARGE bun. ...Thank you." · `flag set:ss12.news`.
   - Journal: `home.ss12.title` "Frostbun's Great-Aunt" · `.open` "Frostbun has an aunt. The aunt is larger than
     Frostbun. I did not think that was possible." · `.done` "Flurry is awake and knitting. She knitted me a warmer for
     my spectacles. It is too warm. I wear it anyway."
   - Hints (two-part, `home.ss12.h<n>.n` / `.a`): 1 ["Frostbun is awake in the evenings.", "Talk to Frostbun at the
     plaza edge between 18:00 and 21:00."] 2 ["Ice doesn't like embers.", "Fling three embers at the blue ice wall high
     on Frostcap."] 3 ["She's at the very bottom.", "Solve the Snowglobe down to the last room, behind the Root Key
     door."] 4 ["She naps halfway through.", "Watch for her roll; she's dizzy after bonking the glass. Melt icicles with
     embers."] 5 ["Frostbun will want to know.", "Talk to Frostbun: on the Summit by day, at the plaza in the evening."]
2. **World marks** (on `ss12.done`):
   - **The Summit visit**: a schedule for NPC `flurry` (S-174): `[[10, 'home.summit_flurry', 'knit'], [16,
     'snowglobe:M', 'sleep']]`. `home.summit_flurry` = nearest walkable to the Summit anchor at bearing 120°, 4.5 m from
     Frostbun's nap spot, ≥ 3 m from the arena ring props; it is a schedule spot record (no generator change, home prints
     unchanged). On the Summit she sits knitting (needles bob 1 Hz, the long scarf trails 2 m on the snow); Talk:
     `home.flurry.summit.1` "Rrr. My nephew snores like a kettle. ...where was I? Ah. Purl one." While she is at the
     Summit, r10 shows the quilt mound with a note: `[E] Read · Note` `home.flurry.note` "Gone up to see my nephew. Back
     for my nap. — F." and no Wake prompt.
   - **The glow**: a pale-blue additive glow card (r 4 m, `#bfe3ff`, `nightGlow` only, 1 draw, `userData.cull = { dist:
     320 }`) 6 m above the melted ice-wall anchor, from `boss:snowglobe:flurry`. It is a child of the existing ice-wall
     item group (no new generator list).
   - **The ear-warmer**: when `ss12.done` and `ss25.done` both hold, Frostbun's GLB gets a knitted ear-warmer extra mesh
     (a `tube` cuff 0.25 m on the left ear, `#e88a8a` with white stripes `#fff3e0`) where the sock was; his idle line
     `home.frostbun.idle.warmer` "Aunt Flurry knitted it. Warm. ...Don't tell Pinch I like it."
3. **Item** (`js/text/en/home.js`): `trim_snowdrift` "Snowdrift" desc "A frost-pale hood trim, knitted while dozing."
   (kind `trim`).
4. **Economy**: ss-12's 40 gems join the side-story ledger in `js/content/economy.js`.

#### Assets
- **`icon_trim_snowdrift`**: a hood-shaped swatch in `#dfeaf6`
  drawn on canvas (no generation; S-303 replaces all trim icons).
- Flurry's Summit pose reuses S-239a's `boss_flurry` (sit-upright, needles bobbing); the glow card reuses the shared fx
  card; the ear-warmer is a code `tube` (≤ 80 tris). No generated images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints unchanged (the Summit spot is a schedule record; the glow and ear-warmer are child
  meshes of existing items).
- ss-12 runs start to finish; steps already true complete at once; Talk with Flurry gives `trim_snowdrift` once; the
  news scene plays from both triggers; the Summit visit and the note in r10 follow the schedule; Frostbun's ear-warmer
  appears only with `ss25.done`; 40 gems once.
- Content lint green (Frostbun is "he" in every ss-12 line; no line > 220 characters; every flag registered);
  economy test green.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h` first. `bun test` (content, quests, economy).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (new case `ss-12`: start at the plaza at 19:00 via `G.debug.tod(19)`, melt the wall with
  three ember hits, warp into r10, `G.debug.killBoss('snowglobe:flurry')`, Talk for the trim, news scene at the Summit
  with `G.debug.tod(11)`; assert gems +40 once; `G.debug.tod(17)` → Flurry back in r10, no note).
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- The boss, petal and unhide (S-239a). Wearing the Snowdrift trim (S-303). Flurry's Hundred-Year Night spot (S-288).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; S-239a is untouched. The trim item stays in saves that got it (nothing earned is taken back) and the
Items tab placeholder line still reads.
