<a id="s-240"></a>

### S-240 · feat: side story 10 The Parcel With No Name and the Chime Bell

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-232, S-170, S-161c, S-203 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-10](../bible/side-content.md#ss-10), [systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools),
[systems#completion](../bible/systems.md#completion),
[cast#postmistress-pell](../bible/cast.md#postmistress-pell), [cast#catchers](../bible/cast.md#catchers),
[systems#brief-ui](../bible/systems.md#brief-ui)

**Decisions:** the key item id is **`parcel`** (systems' key-item table) and its kind is `delivery` so `take:parcel` is
legal ([systems#conditions](../bible/systems.md#conditions)); side-content's `parcel_noname` is not used. Captain
Pinch's line is written with Frostbun as "he" (LOCKED).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**. A forty-minute walk through twelve people
the player already loves, ending with the passive finder every collector wants at this point of a 100-hour game: the
Chime Bell. It also gives every Catcher something to say at the plaza.

#### What
1. **Quest** `ss-10` (`js/content/quests/glassway.js`, n 110, owner `pell`, unlock `['flag:moteNamed',
   'realm:kettle.visited']`, reward `['gems:+20']`, `set: ['set:ss10.done']`, minutes 35):

   | Step | Text | Done | On |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `parcel` | `gw.ss10.s1` "Ask about the parcel with no name" | `item:parcel` | `ITEM_GET` |
   | `chain` | `gw.ss10.s2` "Find who the parcel belongs to ({n}/12): ask {next}" | `flag:ss10.n>=12` | `TALK_END` |
   | `open` | `gw.ss10.s3` "Open the parcel" | `item:chime_bell` | `ITEM_GET` |

   - `{n}` is `flag:ss10.n`; `{next}` is the display name of holder `n + 1` from the table below (the journal row, the
     compass target and the objective line all follow the next holder's current scheduled spot).
   - Flags (registry): `ss10.n` (`counter`, 0-12), `ss10.done` (`story`), `ss10.after.pell`, `ss10.after.frostbun`
     (`seen`-like story flags set by the after-lines).
2. **The chain.** Pell's start entry (priority 40, `when: ['flag:moteNamed', 'realm:kettle.visited', '!quest:ss-10.active',
   '!quest:ss-10=done']`) plays `gw.ss10.pell.parcel` and applies `quest:ss-10.start`, `give:parcel`, `set:ss10.n=1`.
   For holders 2-12 one dialogue entry each, priority 40, `when: ['quest:ss-10.active', 'item:parcel',
   'flag:ss10.n=<k−1>']`, lines `<short>.ss10.<npc>.parcel`, effects `inc:ss10.n`. A holder talked to out of order
   says their idle line as usual (no special "wrong person" line: the journal names who is next). Holder table (the
   lines are the bible's, verbatim except holder 10):

   | k | Holder (npc id) | Where (spot) | Text id → line |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | 1 | Postmistress Pell (`pell`) | Puffin Post counter | `gw.ss10.pell.parcel` "No name, no stamp, no return address. A hundred years on my shelf. Stamps required, normally. I made an exception." |
   | 2 | Granny Wick (`granny_wick`) | Wickhaven | `cc.ss10.granny_wick.parcel` "Lamplighter twine. Not my knot, though. That's a turtle's knot. Slow and careful." |
   | 3 | Elder Shellby (`shellby`) | the pedestal | `home.ss10.shellby.parcel` "My knot? Goodness. I tied a lot of parcels when I was small. Not this one, I think. Ribbit keeps the village post." |
   | 4 | Mayor Ribbit (`ribbit`) | the plaza | `home.ss10.ribbit.parcel` "No stamp! Highly irregular. Croak. The paper is from Miso's counter, though. I'd know that paper anywhere." |
   | 5 | Miso (`miso`) | Miso's Shop | `home.ss10.miso.parcel` "Mrrow~ My paper. From before I was born, so not my parcel~ Clover borrows my string. Ask her~" |
   | 6 | Clover (`clover`) | the Shores path | `home.ss10.clover.parcel` "It RATTLES! No, it CHIMES! Hoot will know what chimes. Hoot knows everything. Except when he doesn't." |
   | 7 | Professor Hoot (`hoot`) | the Woods path | `home.ss10.hoot.parcel` "It chimes at approximately... I have no idea. Digby hears better than any of us. Ask Digby." |
   | 8 | Digby (`digby`) | the windmill | `home.ss10.digby.parcel` "That's a bell. A little one. It wants to find something, by the sound. Grumble collects bells, doesn't he?" |
   | 9 | Grumble Shroom (`grumble`) | plaza edge, 18:00-21:00 | `home.ss10.grumble.parcel` "Hmph. Not mine. I collect MOSS. Ask the crab." |
   | 10 | Captain Pinch (`pinch`) | plaza edge, 18:00-21:00 | `home.ss10.pinch.parcel` "Aye, it's no ship's chime. Ask the bun, lad. He sleeps on everything; he'll know a bell by its warmth." |
   | 11 | Frostbun (`frostbun`) | plaza edge, 18:00-21:00 | `home.ss10.frostbun.parcel` "Warm. It wants to be opened. ...By the one who finds things. Forge mark on the clasp. Bear's mark." |
   | 12 | Master Scoria (`scoria`) | Forgefall forge | `ke.ss10.scoria.parcel` "That's my granny's mark. An order for 'whoever goes looking', a hundred years back. Never collected. ...Open it, little one." |

   - Catchers 9-11: from `moteNamed` they are at the plaza edge 18:00-21:00 (S-170). Outside those hours the journal
     row adds "(evenings, at the plaza)" (text `gw.ss10.evening`), and the bench Dusk tune (S-208) is the shortcut.
3. **Opening.** After holder 12, Scoria's anvil shows `[E] Open · Parcel` (an interactable on the anvil anchor
   `ke:w:anvil` placed by Kettle's `placeSideMarks` stream from S-236a, nearest walkable 1.2 m south of the anvil). Use:
   scene `ss10-open` (8 s, `replay: true`): close FP shot on the anvil; the twine bow is pulled; the paper unfolds; a
   little golden bell rises, chiming once (`sfx chime_bell`, visual: the HUD bell icon swings); effects `take:parcel`,
   `give:chime_bell` (item-get beat: "The Chime Bell! It chimes faster near anything still waiting to be found within
   30 m."), then `say scoria ke.ss10.scoria.2` "'For the one who finds things.' Well. Who else has been finding
   things all over the Wide Blue?".
4. **The Chime Bell** is a second row in S-161c's finder module `js/finder.js` (no new module, no second interval
   helper; [systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools) "The finder"):
   - `FINDERS` gains `{ id: 'bell', item: 'chime_bell', radius: 30, kinds: ['chest', 'keepsake', 'acorn', 'bottle',
     'log'] }`. `gather` gains four kinds, each "not yet found" in the current realm: **`chest`** = every chest anchor
     whose `<realm>:<id>` key is not in `S.chests` (buried chests, home caches and dungeon chests included);
     **`keepsake`** = `W.secrets` entries not in `S.secrets`; **`bottle`** = `W.bottles` entries whose key is not in
     `S.collect.bottle`; **`log`** = `W.logs` entries whose key is not in `S.collect.log` (Lamp Logs handed over in
     scenes have no anchor and are never targets). `acorn` already exists (S-161c).
   - Interval: S-161c's `chimeInterval(d, 30)` (2.0 s at 30 m, 0.25 s at ≤ 2 m, linear); silence rules as S-161c
     (towns, scenes, fights, modals). With the Ear also owned, one chime stream plays for the nearer target.
   - Each chime: `sfx chime_tick` (a soft two-partial bell, 0.12 s, pitched up 1 semitone per 5 m closer) **and** the HUD
     finder icon switches to `icon_chime_bell` while the nearest target is the Bell's (the Ear's icon otherwise) and
     swings ±18° for 0.3 s (deaf-friendly visual twin; [charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person)). The
     "Finder chimes" toggle (`settings.chime`, S-161c) mutes both.
   - **Where the last ones are, game-wide.** `completionV2`'s `missingByRegion` (S-161c) extends to the game-wide
     **Bottles** and **Lamp Logs** rows: at `done / total >= 0.75` and not full, one sub-line per land and region still
     holding one, "{Land} · {Region}: {n} bottle(s) still bobbing" / "... Lamp Log(s) still unread" (text ids
     `ui.comp.left.bottle`, `ui.comp.left.log`); `tools/realm-stats.mjs` already writes `where` for every anchored id
     (bottles and logs included once their finds steps placed them; regenerate `js/realms/stats.js` if a list moved).
     Moonmere's bottle 11 (kept by Mote) is listed as "Moonmere" with no region.
5. **After-lines** (priority 40, `once: true`, `when: 'quest:ss-10=done'`): Pell `gw.ss10.pell.2` "Delivered! A hundred
   years late and exactly on time. Stamp, please. ...Fine. Exception." · Frostbun (plaza) `home.ss10.frostbun.2` "Told
   you. Warm. ...You find things. Everyone says."
6. **World marks** (on `ss10.done`, within 10 s where loaded): Pell's dead-letter shelf (S-203's prop) shows empty and a
   sign `gw.ss10.sign` "All letters delivered ✿" (`[E] Look · Shelf`); gift `nook_gift_twine` (kind `furnishing`)
   appears tied in a bow on Pip's Nook shelf slot reserved for gifts (S-056/S-302 gift slot 1; if the gift-slot system
   is not shipped yet, the item shows in the Items tab and S-302 places it).
7. **Journal**: `gw.ss10.title` "The Parcel With No Name" · `.open` "A parcel with no name. Twelve people declined to
   open it. I declined twice." · `.done` "It was for the one who finds things. That is Pip. We all agreed, which has never
   happened before." · Hints: 1 ["Pell keeps a dead-letter shelf.", "Talk to Postmistress Pell at Puffin Post in the
   Glassway."] 2 ["Each holder knows who's next.", "Ask the person named in the journal; the Catchers are at the plaza
   edge in the evenings."] 3 ["The bear knows his granny's mark.", "Open the parcel at Master Scoria's anvil in
   Forgefall."].
8. **Economy**: ss-10 20 gems in the side-story table.
9. **Debug**: S-161c's `G.debug.finder.nearest()` (now also reports bell kinds); `G.debug.flags.set('ss10.n', k)`.

#### Assets
- **`icon_chime_bell`** (generated, 128² cell in the UI icon atlas, core): PREAMBLE + `icon` + "a little golden hand bell
  icon with a leaf ribbon" ([systems#brief-ui](../bible/systems.md#brief-ui)). Process: white-to-alpha one-liner, then
  `export_sprites.py --size 128`; packed by `tools/pack_atlas.py` (S-037). Fallback: canvas bell (`#f2c45a` dome, `#8fc7a0`
  ribbon).
- **`icon_parcel`** (128²): "STYLE. A small brown-paper parcel tied with pale twine as a round game icon on a cream disc,
  soft shadow". Fallback: canvas box `#c9a46b` with a cross of `#f4ead8`.
- **`prop_parcel`** (code-built, 40 tris): a 0.3 × 0.2 × 0.15 m box `#c9a46b` with twine strips `#f4ead8` and a brass
  clasp `#c99a4a`; it rests on Scoria's anvil in the scene. ART BRIEF: silhouette a small brown-paper parcel with a twine
  bow and a forge clasp; 0.3 m; toon paper; 40 / 300 tris; 3D prompt "small brown paper parcel tied with twine, brass
  clasp with a bear-paw mark, hand-painted, under 300 triangles".
- **`tool_chime_bell`** (code-built, 60 tris, not held: the bell hangs from Pip's belt in third person only): a 0.08 m
  golden bell `#f2c45a` with a green ribbon `#8fc7a0`. ART BRIEF (batch 12): "a little golden hand bell with a leaf
  ribbon, hand-painted, under 200 triangles, 0.08 m".

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen realms" lines that do not apply (no realm generated); home prints unchanged.
- `tests/finder.test.js` (extend): `chimeInterval(30, 30) = null`, `(29.99, 30) ≈ 2.0`, `(1, 30) = 0.25`; `gather`
  returns chests, keepsakes, acorns, bottles and logs not yet found and skips found ones; with Ear and Bell owned and an
  acorn at 15 m plus a chest at 5 m, the chest's interval is used and the HUD shows the bell icon.
- `tests/completion-v2.test.js` (extend): Bottles at 9/12 list exactly the missing bottles' land and region lines; at 8/12
  none.
- The chain only advances in order; holder k's line plays once per playthrough at `ss10.n = k − 1`; the journal names
  the next holder; all twelve holders are reachable at some hour (Catchers 18-21).
- Opening gives `chime_bell` once and takes `parcel`; 20 gems paid once; Pell's shelf sign and the Nook gift appear.
- The HUD bell swings with every chime; muting in settings silences both sound and swing.
- Content lint: `~` only in Miso's line; Pip has no line; all ≤ 220 characters.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (`tests/finder.test.js`, `tests/completion-v2.test.js`; content, quests, economy).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (new case `ss-10`): from the Kettle fixture with `moteNamed`, walk the chain with
  `G.debug.warpTo('<npc>')` + Talk for k = 1..12 (setting `G.debug.tod(19)` for the Catchers), open at the anvil, then
  `G.debug.realm('saltglass')` is not used; instead on home warp 10 m from an unopened home cache and assert
  `G.debug.finder.nearest().interval` < 1.0 with `finder === 'bell'` and that the HUD icon's rotation changes within
  2 s; warp 10 m from an unread home Lamp Log (if the home old-beacon logs are shipped) and assert `kind === 'log'`;
  warp into the plaza and assert silence.
- `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (two icons).

#### Out of scope
- The Nook gift-slot system (S-302). Other Catcher plaza lines (S-170, shipped).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR. The Chime Bell is a `FINDERS` row behind `item:chime_bell` plus four `gather` kinds; the Acorn Ear
keeps working without it.

<a id="s-241"></a>

### S-241 · feat: side story 3 Nothing Is Refundable, part 2: the twins' joint sign

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-134, S-237a · Size: S (<200) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-03](../bible/side-content.md#ss-03), [cast#miso](../bible/cast.md#miso), [cast#kinako](../bible/cast.md#kinako),
[cast#granny-mochi](../bible/cast.md#granny-mochi), [atlas-home#interiors](../bible/atlas-home.md#interiors)

**Decisions:** the board the twins painted reads **"MISO & KINAKO"**; it was sawn through the middle of the ampersand, so
Miso's half reads "MISO &" and Kinako's "& KINAKO" (side-content's halves). Cast's joint-sign wording is kept as a small
hand-lettered strip under the board: "Nothing is refundable." The LOCKED "Miso's Shop" sign is not touched (atlas-home
decision 24). Kinako's bazaar keeps its daily land route ([cast#kinako](../bible/cast.md#kinako)) and, from `ss03.done`,
moors at Nell's Jetty 18:00-21:00 every evening.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**. Closes the twenty-year sulk opened in M16
(S-134) now that ss-41 revealed Kinako sold Miso her swords at cost. A shop the player has visited a hundred times
changes, permanently.

#### What
1. **Quest data** (`js/content/quests/home.js`, extend `ss-03` from S-134): steps 4-5 appended; the part-1 "To be
   continued ✿" stop is now the `needs` on step 4 (S-236a's per-step `needs`):

   | Step | Text | Needs | Done | On |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | `halves` | `home.ss03.s4` "Bring Kinako's half of the sign to Miso" | `quest:ss-41=done` | `flag:ss03.halves` | `TALK_END` |
   | `sign` | `home.ss03.s5` "Watch the twins hang the sign" | - | `flag:ss03.sign` | `SCENE_END` |

   Reward on done: `gems:+25` (part 1 paid 15 at step 3), `set:ss03.done`.
2. **Kinako gives his half.** Kinako's priority-40 entry, `when: ['quest:ss-03>=4', '!item:sign_kinako',
   '!flag:ss03.halves']`, wherever his bazaar is moored: `home.ss03.kinako.4` "Take this to her!! My half!! It's the
   BETTER half!! ...It's the same size. Don't tell her." → `give:sign_kinako` (key item, kind `delivery`, name "Half a
   sign", desc "'& KINAKO', in a kitten's careful paint."). While ss-03 is at `halves` without `sign_kinako`, the bazaar
   **stays moored** where it is (the bazaar route skips its daily move; [cast#kinako](../bible/cast.md#kinako)
   decision 12) and the journal map marks it.
3. **Miso.** Miso's priority-40 entry with `item:sign_kinako`: `home.ss03.miso.2` "He kept his half too? ...Silly cat~
   Silly, silly cat." → `take:sign_kinako`, `set:ss03.halves`; she bends under the counter (a 1 s `pose: 'bow'`).
4. **Scene `ss03-sign`** (15 s, `replay: true`), trigger `{ on: 'tod', band: 'dusk', realm: 'home', area: [shop.x,
   shop.z, 30] }` once `ss03.halves` (the next dusk with Pip within 30 m of the shop; Continue at dusk also fires it).
   Actors: Miso, Kinako (`spawn` at the shop door), Granny Mochi (`spawn`, sits on a stool prop 3 m east of the counter).
   Steps: `shot fixed` framing the shop front (FP-safe: camera at Pip's eye height 1.1 m, 6 m back) · `move kinako` and
   `move miso` to either end of the board · `fx leaf_fall` · the board prop rises into place under the LOCKED sign
   (0.8 s) · `say miso home.ss03.miso.3` "Fine~ He can have half the sign. The smaller half~" · `say kinako
   home.ss03.kinako.3` "It's the SAME size!! It's ALWAYS been the same size!!" · `say mochi home.ss03.mochi.3` "There. Now
   I can nap." · `pose mochi sleep` · `flag set:ss03.sign`.
5. **World mark** (child meshes of the shop group, no generator lists; home prints unchanged): the joined board (1.6 ×
   0.35 m, `decal_twin_sign` on a plank) hangs 0.5 m under the shop's LOCKED sign; a hand-lettered strip under it reads
   "Nothing is refundable."; `[E] Look · Sign` `home.ss03.look.sign` "'MISO & KINAKO'. The ampersand has a saw line
   through it, very neatly mended." From `ss03.done`: Kinako's bazaar moors at Nell's Jetty 18:00-21:00 every evening
   (a row in the bazaar route table: `{ evening: 'home:jetty_bazaar', from: 18, to: 21, when: 'flag:ss03.done' }`;
   the berth anchor is the bazaar pier spot S-092 used for home); Miso's idle after `home.miso.idle.ss03` "Mrrow~ He
   comes for supper now. He brings dessert. It's never shiny enough~".
6. **Journal**: `.done` "The twins share a sign. Granny Mochi napped immediately. Case closed, and also the shop, at six."
   Hints: 4 ["Kinako kept something too.", "Find Kinako's Floating Bazaar (the journal map shows where it is moored) and
   talk to him."] 5 ["Sign-hanging is an evening job.", "Be near Miso's Shop at dusk."].

#### Assets
- **`decal_twin_sign`** (generated `decal`, 512 × 128, alpha, ≤ 40 KB, core): PREAMBLE + `decal` + "a wooden shop board
  painted by two kittens long ago, the words 'MISO & KINAKO' in wobbly cream and pink letters with little paw prints,
  sawn in half through the ampersand and neatly mended with a brass strip. Palette: #f4d1a6 #e88a8a #6b4a2e #c99a4a."
  Generated text is unreliable: if the letters come out wrong, generate the board without words and draw the text on a
  canvas over it at build time (`tools/export_sprites.py` then a canvas text pass in `js/props.js`); the canvas text
  fallback is always used when the key is missing. Process: `export_sprites.py --size 512x128 --webp 82`.
- Stool (reuse the cottage stool prop from S-134).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen realms" (no realm generated) and "Budgets" lines (no new view).
- Step 4 is hidden until ss-41 is done; Kinako's bazaar does not move while ss-03 waits on his half; the scene fires at
  the next dusk near the shop (and on Continue at dusk); 25 gems paid once; the board hangs under the unchanged LOCKED
  sign; the evening jetty berth appears from `ss03.done`.
- Lint: `~` only in Miso's lines, `!!` only in Kinako's; all ≤ 220 characters.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (quests, content, economy).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (new case `ss-03b`): from the M27 fixture with `quest:ss-41=done` and ss-03 at step 4,
  warp to the bazaar, Talk, warp to Miso, Talk, `G.debug.tod(18.5)`, assert the scene plays, the board mesh exists under
  the shop sign, `ss03.done`, +25 gems; `G.debug.tod(19)` and assert the bazaar actor is at the jetty berth.
- `bun run test:smoke`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
- Kinako's other stock and spoons (ss-18, shipped). The Nook (S-302).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; ss-03 returns to its part-1 stop ("To be continued ✿").

Milestone M27 · Depends on: S-236a, S-236b, S-237a, S-237b, S-238, S-239a, S-239b, S-240, S-241, S-001, S-030, S-191a · Size: S (<200) · Save: none · Flag: none ·

[art-audio#brief-index](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-index)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M27 · Kettle stories, the Snowglobe and the parcel chain**: the close-out records what landed, runs the
full suite once, and hands the owner the twelfth batch of GLB briefs for Kettle Isle and the Snowglobe.

#### What
Follow [charter#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone) steps 1-10 in order. Specifically:
1. **Suite** (after `free -h` shows ≥ 6 GB available, one job at a time): `bun test` · `node tools/build-pages.mjs` +
   `bun run test:pwa` · `bun run test:smoke` (and `?noassets=all`) · `bun run test:opening` · `bun run test:playthrough`
   · every in-page suite in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (including `realm`, `bosses`, `side`, `side-kettle`, `dungeons`,
   `activities`) · `node tests/chapter.mjs <id>` for every shipped chapter in story order (through `ch-kettle`).
2. **DESIGN.md**: side stories table rows for ss-03 (complete), ss-10, ss-12, ss-39, ss-40, ss-41; activities rows for the
   Sling Gallery and Steam Hop (tiers, rewards); optional-dungeon row for the Snowglobe (10 rooms, push-ice rule, 2 gem
   chests, petal); optional-boss row for Great-Aunt Flurry (90 HP, moves, rematch 113 HP); the Chime Bell (30 m, 2.0 →
   0.25 s, silent in towns, scenes and fights; HUD twin).
3. **ARCHITECTURE.md**: the Chime Bell row of `js/finder.js` (S-161c); `js/partner.js` (if S-237a extracted it); the `needs` field on quest steps;
   the flag kinds added in M27 (`record` from S-237b, the rewritable bath settings from S-236b); Kettle's `placeSideMarks`
   stream (`40261 * 107 + 19`) and what it places (ember pot, bath props, gallery board and targets, vents, anvil
   interactable).
4. **`docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-12.md`**: one brief per code-built model of M26-M27 still without an owner GLB, each in
   the [brief template](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-template) (silhouette, size m, palette hexes, materials, poly budget,
   pose, image prompt, 3D prompt, manifest key, the fallback it replaces). The list (from
   [art-audio#brief-index](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-index) rows for batch 12 plus M27's own):
   `npc_scoria`, `npc_pitch`, `npc_soakley` ([cast#master-scoria](../bible/cast.md#master-scoria), `#pitch`, `#soakley`);
   `enemy_soot_snuffle`, `enemy_steam_puff`, `enemy_ash_moth`; `boss_fumarole`; `boss_flurry`; `tool_sling`;
   `tool_chime_bell`; the Kettle kit (`kettle_forge_house`, `kettle_basin_rim`, `kettle_vent`, `kettle_brazier`,
   `kettle_pipe`, `kettle_towel_line`, `kettle_black_rock`, `kettle_fern`, `kettle_tube_mouth`, `kettle_sword_rack`,
   `kettle_deckchair`, `kettle_spout`, `kettle_handle`, `kettle_hammerwheel`, `kettle_bathhouse`, `kettle_teacup`, from
   [atlas-far#kettle-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#kettle-kit)); M27's props `kettle_bath_gauge`, `kettle_bath_valve`,
   `kettle_star_pendant`, `kettle_gallery_targets`, `kettle_steam_vent`, `prop_parcel`; the Snowglobe pieces
   `int_push_ice`, `int_ice_wall`, `int_yarn_ball`, `int_quilt_mound`. Copy each brief from the step PR that built it;
   where the step's brief is missing a field, fill it from the bible file named in the index. Add commented-out
   `models` slots in `assets/manifest.js` for each GLB key that has none yet (art-audio decision 6).
5. **progress.md** entry from the [template](../bible/charter.md#progress-template): steps S-236 - S-242 with issues and
   PRs; splits S-236a/b, S-237a/b, S-239a/b; hours +4.5 h (cumulative 67.0 h) vs a careful run (the chapter tests' scripted time is the
   floor); the Snowglobe realm's genMs and its worst room's calls and tris; save size of `ch-kettle-end` + M27 state.
6. **Hours check**: if a careful run of M27's content is more than 25 % off +4.5 h, record it in the entry and the vision
   Log (close-out step 7).
7. **Release state**: `saltglass`, `driftholm`, `starmeadow`, `crown` and every unbuilt dungeon still `hidden: true`;
   `snowglobe` unhidden; New Game → `ch-kettle` → Continue chain passes.
8. `bun test tests/docs.test.js` passes.

#### Assets

#### Acceptance criteria

- `docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-12.md` holds a complete brief for every key in the list above (a test-free check: the PR
  body pastes `grep -c '^### ' docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-12.md` and it equals the list length, 36).

<a id="s-243"></a>

### S-243 · feat: Saltglass dunes recipe: dune ridges, salt pans and ruins (hidden)

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-222 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm `saltglass` `hidden: true` · Bible:
[atlas-far#saltglass-card](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-card), [atlas-far#saltglass-shape](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-shape),
[atlas-far#saltglass-regions](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-regions), [atlas-far#saltglass-arrival](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-arrival),
[atlas-far#saltglass-anchors](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-anchors), [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes),
[systems#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table),
[art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes), [charter#budgets-realm](../bible/charter.md#budgets-realm)

**Decision (dune numbers):** the atlas asks for crests 45 m apart, 4-14 m high, lee faces ≤ 32°. A single 45 m wave
cannot rise 14 m and keep both faces under 32° (a cosine face of height H needs a run of at least 2.5 H). The recipe
therefore builds the height from two layers: a slow **swell** (0-8 m, wavelength 160 m) and the **ridges** (0-6 m,
wavelength 45 m, asymmetric). Crest heights above the pans run 4-14 m as the atlas says, and every face stays ≤ 32°.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. The biggest far land, built hidden first so its ground,
regions and prints are frozen before anything is placed on it (ENGINE §2, "Frozen realms").

#### What
1. **Recipe** `js/realms/recipes/dunes.js`: `export class DunesWorld extends RealmWorld` (the S-018 base), overriding
   `layout`, `baseTerrain`, `regions`, `splat`, `scatterTables`, `places`. Stage RNG streams (the shared far table,
   [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes)): terrain `new RNG(40277 * 7 + 3)`, landmarks/stamps
   `new RNG(40277 * 13 + 5)`, scatter `new RNG(40277 * 17 + 3)`, gem trails `new RNG(40277 * 31 + 17)`. Grid 640 m
   (HALF 320). Coordinates x east, z south, bearings clockwise from north (`x = r sin θ`, `z = −r cos θ`).
2. **Terrain, in stage order** (all heights y in metres; sea floor −3):
   1. **Island**: ellipse centre (0, 0), rx 280, rz 230; edge radius scaled by `1 + 0.08 · n(θ)` where `n` is 1-D value
      noise on the perimeter with a wavelength of 90 m of arc. Inside the edge, base land y **1.0**; a **beach ring 12
      m** wide falls from 1.0 to −0.2 at the edge (smoothstep), pale-sand splat.
   2. **Academy Hill**: `y += 13 · (1 + cos(π d / 90)) / 2` for `d = dist((x, z), (−20, 40)) < 90` → hilltop y 14.0.
      A **tower pad**: the 9 × 9 m square centred (−20, 40) is flattened to y 14.0 (the tower in S-244 stands on it).
   3. **The Drift ramp**: a 4 m wide strip on x ∈ [−22, −18] from z 24 (hill height there, ≈ 13.6) rising linearly to
      y 18.0 at z 32, then a flat landing z ∈ [32, 36.5] at y 18.0 against the tower's north face (z 36.5). Sides fall
      to the hill at ≤ 40°. Gradient 0.55 (walkable, < 0.84).
   4. **The Glass Dunes**: cells whose bearing from (0, 0) is in [200°, 360°) ∪ [0°, 30°] and outside the hill
      (`d > 90`) and the camp (`dc > 70`): `y = 1.0 + w · (swell + ridge)`, where `w` blends 0 → 1 over 20 m from the
      sector edges and the hill/camp borders; `swell = 8 · s(x/160, z/160)` with `s` smooth value noise in [0, 1];
      `ridge = H · f(p)` with `H = 2 + 4 · s(x/120 + 7, z/120 + 3)` (2-6 m), `p = frac((x + 12 · s(x/90, z/90) · 2 − 1)
      / 45)` (crests run north-south; the wind is from the west), and the asymmetric profile `f(p) = (1 − cos(π p /
      0.55)) / 2` for `p < 0.55` (windward, gentle) and `(1 + cos(π (p − 0.55) / 0.45)) / 2` otherwise (lee). Max face
      gradient = `(π/2) · 6 / (0.45 · 45)` ≈ 0.47 (25°). **The Organ dune**: add a smooth bump `6 · (1 + cos(π d/25)) /
      2` at (−200, −60) so the crest there reaches y ≥ 14 (cap the cell at 15).
   5. **The Salt Pans**: bearings [30°, 200°) outside hill and camp: y `1.0 + 0.1 · s(x/40, z/40) − 0.05`; **salt
      ridges**: a hexagonal lattice of cell size 30 m (pointy-top), each edge a 2 m wide ridge raised 0.3 m (walkable).
      Ridge cells get splat `snow` slot weight 0.4 (S-245 maps the slot to `saltglass_salt`).
   6. **Sablecamp shelf**: within 70 m of (110, −160): y 3.0 flat, blended 15 m into the surroundings. **Trenches**
      carved 1.0 m deep with 1:2 ramps at both ends: T1 8 × 2 m centred (92, −142) running east-west; T2 6 × 2 m at
      (100, −130) east-west; T3 6 × 2 m at (125, −140) north-south.
   7. **The Mirage Well**: a pool r 9 m at (120, 40): floor −0.8, water level 0.6 (the realm's pond list, the shipped
      pond water shader), rim 1.5 m of damp splat (`forest` slot → `saltglass_oasis`).
   8. **The Lamp Walk** (authored path, `pathD` stamped like the shipped paths, width 3 m, flagstone splat `dirt`
      slot): the polyline jetty (100, −215) → (110, −160) → (70, −110) → (40, −70) → the Academy gate (20, −35),
      Catmull-Rom through the points. The path's 12 lamp anchors every 17 m of arc length from (95, −195) are saved as
      `W.anchors['saltglass:lamp-1..12']` (posts placed in S-244).
   9. **Shore**: the jetty cell (100, −222) faces 22°; carve a 6 × 14 m shallow slot (y −1.2) north of the beach so
      S-244's jetty kit stands in water.
   10. **Ruin stamps**: 34 stamps by Poisson discs (min spacing 14 m, the stamp RNG) on Academy Hill (`d < 88`) and the
       dune foot within 40 m of the hill, rejecting the tower pad + 6 m, the Drift ramp + 4 m, the Lamp Walk + 4 m and
       Dormitory Row's line + 6 m. Kinds by draw: `saltglass_ruin_roof` 40 %, `saltglass_ruin_wall` 35 %,
       `saltglass_column` 25 %; yaw random. Each is a generator prop with a static collider (roof 6 × 3 × 2.2 m, wall
       5 × 0.8 × 3 m, column r 0.6 h 2.5 m) and a fallback mesh (box / prism / cylinder in `#e8d9b8` / `#b55d4c`);
       S-244 gives them their code models.
   11. **Dormitory Row**: eight anchors evenly spaced on the line (−85, 85) → (−40, 60), stored as
       `W.anchors['saltglass:dorm-1..8']` (west to east); the terrain under each is flattened to a 5 × 4 m pad at the
       local height. Cottages arrive in S-244.
3. **Regions** (`W.regions`, the S-028 shape): id, card name, subtitle, extent rule as above, music layer, ambience,
   enemy table **empty in this step** (S-247 fills it), fog row from [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes):

   | id | Card / subtitle | Fog `[near, far, colour, density]` | Music layer · ambience |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `sablecamp` | Sablecamp / "Brush, don't dig" | `[36, 262, #f8f0e0, .10]` | `camp` · `saltglass_camp` |
   | `dunes` | The Glass Dunes / "Wind-polished" | `[34, 262, #f6ecd8, .15]` | `dunes` · `saltglass_dunes` |
   | `pans` | The Salt Pans / "Very bright" | `[40, 270, #fbf7f0, .20]` | `pans` · `saltglass_dunes` |
   | `academy` | Academy Hill / "Mind the bell" | `[32, 250, #f2e8d6, .12]` | `academy` · `saltglass_camp` |

   Precedence: `academy` (d < 90 from (−20, 40)) > `sablecamp` (< 70 from (110, −160), plus the jetty within 25 m of
   (100, −215)) > `dunes` / `pans` by bearing. `sablecamp.town = true`.
4. **Registry** (`js/realms/index.js`):

   ```js
   saltglass: { id: 'saltglass', name: 'Saltglass Flats', kind: 'overworld', size: 640, seed: 40277, recipe: 'dunes',
     palette: 'saltglass', music: 'saltglass', ambience: 'saltglass_camp', hidden: true, bundle: 'saltglass',
     spawns: { jetty: { x: 100, z: -215, facing: 202 }, drift: { x: -20, z: 24, facing: 180 },
               gate: { x: -40, z: 118, facing: 0 } },
     views: [ { id: 'dense', at: [-95, 95], look: [-20, 40] },        // Dormitory Row up to the tower
              { id: 'landmark', at: [20, -35], look: [-20, 40] },     // the gate and the bell tower
              { id: 'town', at: [100, -215], look: [115, -160] },     // Sablecamp from the jetty
              { id: 'vista', at: [-20, 40, 36], look: [-200, -60] } ] } // the belfry over the dunes
   ```
   Until S-245 the palette entry is a stub copied from `kettle`'s with the Saltglass swatch (`#c9a46b` `#efe3c8`
   `#b55d4c` `#8ec9d8`) and the terrain layer keys fall back to home textures (the S-029 fallback rule).
5. **Arrival**: the Glassway's `glassway:buoy_saltglass` portal (S-200 stub) now targets `{ realm: 'saltglass', spawn:
   'jetty' }`, keeping S-200's `locked: { needs: '!hidden:saltglass', say: "Nell won't risk the fog yet." }` (use
   exactly the lock S-222 gave the Kettle buoy before S-232; if S-222 used another form, copy it). The chart keeps
   Saltglass as a fog bank.
6. **Anchors**: add the named anchors that exist after this step: `w:belltower` (−20, 40) (pad centre), `w:driftDoor`
   (−20, 32) y 18, `w:academyGate` (20, −35), `w:glassOrgan` (−200, −60), `w:practiceBeacon` (150, 110),
   `w:windcatcher` (125, −150), `w:findsTable` (112, −158), `w:mirageWell` (120, 40), `w:dorms` (−60, 72), plus the lamp
   and dorm anchors above. Each is nearest-walkable within 12 m.
7. **Prints**: write `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/saltglass.json` (the S-018 `placementPrint` + `fingerprint` of the
   realm). S-244 and S-249a may refresh it (same milestone); after S-254 only post-passes add content.

#### Assets
None generated. Ruin stamps use the fallback primitives listed in What 2.10 until S-244.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Precache" (no assets) and the home-island line is trivially kept (no home change).
- `tests/realms/saltglass.test.js` (new): prints equal the fixture; `W.genMs ≤ 1200` on this machine (logged in the PR);
  `W.unreachable` empty; the three spawns walkable and reachable; hilltop y ∈ [13.8, 14.2]; the Drift landing y ∈
  [17.9, 18.1] and walkable; pan cells off the ridges y ∈ [0.9, 1.1]; ≥ 99 % of dune cells have gradient ≤ 0.625 and all
  ≤ 0.84; the Organ crest cell y ≥ 14; every region id is assigned to at least 15,000 cells; 34 ruin stamps with
  pairwise spacing ≥ 14 m; the 12 lamp anchors are 17 ± 0.5 m apart along the Lamp Walk.
- The same recipe generates without error and with ≥ 80 % of land reachable for seeds 1-20.
- `REALMS.saltglass.hidden === true`; the buoy refuses with "Nell won't risk the fog yet."; `?realm=saltglass` and
  `G.debug.realm('saltglass', 'jetty')` load it.
- Realm suite (`realm`): enter/leave/re-enter leak check; Pip walkable at each spawn; 4 views within budget in both
  cameras (record calls and tris in the PR body).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test tests/realms/saltglass.test.js` then `bun test`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (saltglass row; swap Glassway → Saltglass via `G.debug.realm` timed ≤ 1.5 s).
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
Props (S-244), art and music (S-245), people (S-246), enemies (S-247), finds (S-249a/b).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR. The realm is hidden and nothing links to it except a locked buoy.

### S-244 · feat: Saltglass props: the Academy bell tower, glass sculptures and dig camp

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-243, S-082 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[atlas-far#saltglass-kit](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-kit), [atlas-far#saltglass-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-pois),
[atlas-far#saltglass-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-states), [atlas-isles#shared](../bible/atlas-isles.md#shared),
[art-audio#brief-template](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-template)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. The landmarks that make the Flats readable in first person:
the 26 m bell tower seen from every cell, the singing Glass Organ, the crooked Practice Beacon, the striped
Windcatcher over the camp.

#### What
1. **Kit module** `js/props-saltglass.js` (registered for realm `saltglass`; every builder returns a toon mesh and
   honours its manifest key first). Instanced types (11 new + shared from S-082; the realm total must stay ≤ 24):
   `saltglass_ruin_roof`, `saltglass_ruin_wall`, `saltglass_column`, `saltglass_dorm`, `saltglass_glass`,
   `saltglass_tent`, `saltglass_trench`, `saltglass_lamp_post`, `saltglass_salt_block`, `saltglass_prickle`,
   `saltglass_nameplate`. Single meshes: `saltglass_belltower`, `saltglass_glass_organ`, `saltglass_practice_beacon`,
   `saltglass_windcatcher`, `saltglass_finds_table`, `saltglass_mirage_well`, `saltglass_acacia`. New materials (6 of
   12): `saltCrust`, `sunStone`, `windGlass` (fresnel rim + 0.85 opacity, no refraction pass), `canvas`, `brass`,
   `petrifiedWood`.
2. **Placements** (a `places` stage of the recipe, landmark stream `40277 * 13 + 5`; S-243's prints are refreshed once):

   | Thing | Anchor | Size | Notes |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | Bell tower | pad (−20, 40), base y 14 | 7 × 7 m, 26 m tall (top y 40) | box collider h 26; **belfry deck** 7 × 7 m at y 36 (a bridge-deck record; open arches on four sides, bell hangs centre, silent); **Drift Door** on the north face at the landing (y 18), closed door mesh, no portal yet (S-250); `w:belltower.bell` y 36 |
   | Glass Organ | (−200, −60), on the dune crest | 14 pipes, tallest 18 m, cluster 10 m | colliders per pipe (r 0.6-1.0); a **ledge deck** 3 × 2 m at y 18 on its east side (the glide launch for c07, reached by S-249b's ivy) |
   | Practice Beacon | (150, 110) | 9 salt-block tiers, 16 m | stepped collider; **top ledge deck** 4 × 4 m at y 16 (c10 and the vista, Hook in S-249b); the receiver cradle mesh on top (unlit) |
   | Windcatcher | (125, −150) | 10 m mast, 4 m scoop | collider r 0.5; scoop yaws 6°/s toward the wind bearing 270° ± 20° (sine, 40 s) |
   | Glass sculptures (16) | spiral round the Organ: `r_i = 25 + 65 · i / 15`, `θ_i = 40° + 137.5° · i`, nearest walkable within 6 m | 2-5 m | `saltglass_glass` kinds by `i mod 4`: tall (5 m), short (2 m), leaning (4 m, 15° lean), cracked (3 m, a visible crack line); note per `i`: D4 E4 F4 G4 A4 B4 C5 D5 E5 F5 G5 A5 B5 C6 D6 E6; record `{ i, kind, note }` in `W.sculptures` (S-249b makes them strike-able) |
   | Dormitory Row | `saltglass:dorm-1..8` | 4 × 3 m each, buried to the eaves | door faces the row's south side; a blank `saltglass_nameplate` on each door |
   | Tents (5) | (118, −172), (130, −158), (98, −158), (122, −132), (106, −176) | 4 × 3 m | yaw toward (112, −158); colliders |
   | Trench frames | T1-T3 from S-243 | 6-8 × 2 m | plank edges and string, no collider |
   | Finds table | (112, −158) | 3 m | tarp over one end; labelled pots, rocks (decor) |
   | Stewpot | (130, −165) | 0.8 m | steaming (shared steam card), no NPC (cast names two crew) |
   | Lamp posts (12) | `saltglass:lamp-1..12` | 2.2 m | unlit; collider r 0.2 |
   | Salt blocks | 6 around (140, 95) | 1 m | decor beside the Practice Beacon |
   | Prickle patch | 7 at (190, 120) r 6 | 0.8 m | decor (curio 25); never enemies |
   | Mirage Well | (120, 40) | rim r 9.5 | stone rim + two date palms (`saltglass_acacia` family palette, a simple palm build) |
   | Acacia Root | (−40, 125) | 9 m | petrified acacia, collider r 0.8; root-gate knot comes in S-249a |
   | Jetty | (100, −222), facing 22° | the S-082 jetty kit | 12 m long |
3. **States now**: the bell hangs still and silent; ruin windows are dark at night; lamp posts unlit. The healed states
   are S-254's.
4. **LOD and cull**: ruins, dorms, tents and sculptures get the shipped `lodBand` proxies (≤ 180 tris) past 38-48 m;
   lamp posts, salt blocks and prickles `cull: { dist: 90 }`; the tower, Organ and Practice Beacon never cull and get
   `landmark: true` (fog cap 0.45).
5. **Prints**: refresh `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/saltglass.json` (same milestone). Record the realm's instanced type
   count and material count in the PR.

#### Assets
Every model is code-built now, with the ART BRIEFs below copied into `docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-13.md` at S-261.
Briefs (each: silhouette · size · palette · materials · tris code/GLB · pose · fallback · image prompt · 3D prompt):
- **`saltglass_belltower`**: a square sandstone bell tower with a pointed copper cap, an arched open belfry with a great
  bronze bell, a moth-and-lamp emblem, spirals round the cornice, a door 4 m up on a sand drift, ivy on the south face ·
  26 m above the hilltop, 7 × 7 m · stone `#e8d9b8`, trim `#b55d4c`, cap `#8ec9d8`, bell `#b8894a` · sunStone + brass ·
  1,500 / 10 k · bell swings on rings · fallback a box shaft, a pyramid cap and a box belfry with a cylinder bell ·
  image (concept, docs/art/ref): PREAMBLE + `card` + "a half-buried old school bell tower standing out of a pale salt
  desert, sandstone with a verdigris copper cap, a big bronze bell, a moth-and-lamp emblem, a sand drift reaching a door
  halfway up. Palette: #c9a46b #efe3c8 #b55d4c #8ec9d8." · 3D: "toon sandstone bell tower 26 m with verdigris cap, open
  belfry, bronze bell, door 4 m up".
- **`saltglass_glass_organ`**: a cluster of wind-polished glass pipes of many heights on a dune crest · 18 m tallest,
  10 m wide · glass `#8ec9d8`, base `#c9a46b` · windGlass · 900 / 6 k · a slow glint sweeps · fallback 14 cylinders of
  varied heights · image: PREAMBLE + `card` + "a cluster of tall wind-polished glass pipes like an organ on a desert
  dune, pale blue-green, singing in the wind. Palette: #8ec9d8 #c9a46b #efe3c8 #ffffff." · 3D: "toon glass pipe cluster
  18 m".
- **`saltglass_practice_beacon`**: a slightly crooked tower of square salt blocks, a stone lamp cradle on top with a
  receiver, chalk marks · 16 m · salt `#efe3c8`, shadow `#d8c8a8`, cradle `#b55d4c`, receiver `#ffcf7a` · saltCrust ·
  600 / 4 k · fallback 9 stacked boxes each offset 0.15 m · 3D: "toon tower of stacked salt blocks 16 m".
- **`saltglass_windcatcher`**: a timber mast with a striped canvas scoop · 10 m · canvas `#b55d4c` / `#efe3c8`, timber
  `#8a6a4a` · canvas · 400 / 2.5 k · scoop yaws · fallback cylinder mast + curved quad.
- **`saltglass_glass`**: a wind-polished glass pillar in four shapes · 2-5 m · `#8ec9d8`, inner `#cfeef5` · windGlass ·
  80 / 500 · glow pulse when struck · fallback a capsule.
- **`saltglass_ruin_roof` / `_ruin_wall` / `saltglass_column` / `saltglass_dorm`**: roof ridge with chimney (6 m,
  `#b55d4c`, 200 tris), wall top with an arched window (5 m, `#e8d9b8`, 160 tris; window glows `#ffcf7a` at night after
  the stone), spiral-carved broken column (4 m, 120), tiny dormitory cottage buried to its eaves (4 × 3 m, 260, a door
  with a nameplate) · sunStone · fallback boxes, prisms, cylinders · image (one sheet): PREAMBLE + `sheet-items` N = 4 +
  "ruins of a gentle old school half buried in pale sand: a roof ridge with a chimney, a wall top with an arched window,
  a spiral-carved column, a tiny dormitory cottage. Palette: #e8d9b8 #b55d4c #c9a46b #8ec9d8."
- **`saltglass_tent` / `saltglass_trench` / `saltglass_finds_table`**: striped tent (4 × 3 m, `#efe3c8` / `#b55d4c`,
  160), trench frame of planks and string (6 × 2 m, 60), trestle table of labelled finds under a tarp (3 m, 300) · canvas
  + brass · fallback prisms and boxes.
- **`saltglass_lamp_post`**: slim stone post with a brass lamp and a plate · 2.2 m · `#e8d9b8`, `#b8894a`, glass
  `#ffcf7a` · 100 / 600 · fallback cylinder + emissive box.
- **`saltglass_salt_block` / `saltglass_prickle` / `saltglass_nameplate`**: cut salt block (1 m, 12), round prickly plant
  (0.8 m, 120, `#7fae4b`, flowers `#ff9f8f`), brass door plate (0.4 m, 8, decal `decal_nameplate` from S-245, blank
  until then) · fallback box, sphere with cone spikes, plane.
- **`saltglass_mirage_well`**, **`saltglass_acacia`**: stone ring r 9.5 with two date palms (palms 6 m, trunk `#8a6a3c`,
  fronds `#7aa86a`); a petrified acacia, stone from root to twig (9 m, `#b5a58a`, 500 tris), its big root diving into
  the ground · fallback ring of boxes + two cone palms; a branching cylinder tree.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Precache" (no image files). Realm totals: instanced types ≤ 24, new material programs ≤
  12 (logged by the realm suite's warm-up count).
- Unit (`tests/realms/saltglass.test.js` extended): 16 sculptures, each within 6 m of its spiral anchor, kinds cycling
  tall/short/leaning/cracked, notes as listed; 8 dorms; 12 lamp posts; the belfry deck at y 36 and the Practice Beacon
  deck at y 16 exist as deck records; the bell tower top y 40 ± 0.2; nothing placed blocks the Lamp Walk, the Drift ramp
  or a spawn (reachability unchanged from S-243 except the colliders' own cells).
- Realm suite: the 4 views within budget in both cameras; the tower is in line of sight from the jetty spawn and from
  each other view at eye height.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test tests/realms/saltglass.test.js`; `bun test`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (saltglass; record calls/tris per view and the material count).
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
Art textures and music (S-245); the bell's healed states and windows (S-254); strike-able sculptures, lamps that kindle,
receivers, ivy (S-249b).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; S-243's terrain and fallback stamps remain, realm hidden.

<a id="s-245"></a>

### S-245 · art: Saltglass look, loading card, map art and music

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-243, S-029b · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[art-audio#saltglass](../bible/art-audio.md#saltglass), [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes),
[art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data), [art-audio#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience),
[art-audio#pipeline](../bible/art-audio.md#pipeline), [art-audio#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Gives the Flats their bright, still, old look and their
music: a pale salt desert that is never dark, with a land that is "dim" by sound until the Academy bell rings again.

#### What
1. **Palette** `palettes.saltglass` (replace the S-243 stub) from [art-audio#palettes](../bible/art-audio.md#palettes):
   swatch `#c9a46b #efe3c8 #b55d4c #8ec9d8`; sky rows day top/horizon `#6fb0e0`/`#f6efe0`, sun `#ffd9a0`, dusk
   `#6d6aa8`/`#f0a888`, night `#1e2444`/`#3e4470`; clouds `#ffffff` / `#ffd0b8` / `#7480b8`; water deep/shallow/foam
   `#3a8fa8` / `#8ec9d8` / `#ffffff`; terrain layer keys (slots 0-6) `saltglass_saltbush`, `saltglass_oasis`,
   `saltglass_bloom`, `saltglass_dune`, `saltglass_salt`, `saltglass_hardpan`, `saltglass_sandstone`, cliff
   `saltglass_cliff`. Region fog rows are S-243's.
2. **Terrain textures** (8, `tile` kind, 1024 generate → `_hd` 1024 ≤ 110 KB + `_lo` 512 ≤ 40 KB webp, `realm:
   saltglass`): subjects verbatim from [art-audio#saltglass](../bible/art-audio.md#saltglass) (saltbush, oasis, bloom,
   dune, salt, hardpan, sandstone, cliff). Process each: `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py raw.png out.png --blend 64`,
   then `cwebp`-equivalent through `tools/export_sprites.py --size 1024 --webp 82` and `--size 512 --webp 80`. Fallback:
   the home texture of the same slot (S-029 rule).
3. **Billboard cards** (`realm: saltglass`): `saltglass_card_ground` (`sheet-items` N = 4, 1024 × 512 → four cells
   `saltbush`, `desertflower`, `glassshard`, `datepalmsprout`, sliced with `tools/slice_sheet.py --cols 4 --rows 1`),
   used by the scatter tables (saltbush and flowers on `grass`/`meadow`, glass shards on `sand`, sprouts by the Well);
   `saltglass_card_shimmer` (1024 × 256, alpha: a band drawn at 0.4 m over the pans in day only, additive 0.25, `cull:
   { dist: 120 }`); `saltglass_card_sandflow` (512² 6-frame strip via `tools/process_strip.py --frames 6`; used by S-250
   and on dune crests as wind-blown sand); `saltglass_card_glassglint` (128² 4-frame: the visual twin of the singing
   dunes, shown on sculptures and on 1 in 40 dune glass shards); `decal_nameplate` (`decal`, 512 × 128). Subjects
   verbatim from the bible table. Fallbacks: tinted quads in the palette colours.
4. **Loading card** `load_saltglass` (`loading`, 1600 × 900 ≤ 200 KB + 960 × 540 lo) and **map** `map_saltglass`
   (`map`, 1024² ≤ 200 KB), subjects verbatim from the bible. **Sketches** `sketch_saltglass_1..3` (`sketch`, 512 × 288
   ≤ 45 KB) for the three vistas: 1 "The Flats Shine" (the salt pans from the top of the stacked salt-block beacon, white
   hexagons to the horizon), 2 "Wind Music" (glass pipes on a dune crest at golden hour), 3 "The Whole School" (the
   desert from a belfry, ruins poking out of the sand like hats). Loading tips are S-308's.
5. **Music** `js/music/tracks.js` (or the S-028 realm module): add `saltglass` exactly as in
   [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data): `bpm: 60, bpb: 4, sp: 8, swing: 0, rev: 0.55, delay: {
   beats: 1.5, fb: 0.4, mix: 0.35 }`, chords `'Dm C Bb C Dm F G Am Dm C Bb F Gm Am Dm Dm'`, the 16-bar melody as listed,
   `lead: 'flute', leadVol: 0.9, lead2: 'bell', lead2Vol: 0.35, layers: { lead2: 1 }, startLayer: 0`, bass `'r . . . . .
   . .'` `bassSoft` 0.55, comp `'1 . . . 3 . . .'` `harp` 0.25 oct 1, pad `pad` 0.7, sparkle false. **Dim rule**
   ([art-audio](../bible/art-audio.md#saltglass) dim row): until `stone:saltglass` the track plays at layer 0 (no bell
   `lead2`); from `stone:saltglass` (or `stoneHeld:saltglass`) at layer 1. Region layers `camp`, `dunes`, `pans`,
   `academy` map to the same track (the S-028 region-layer hook; a missing layer name is ignored).
6. **Ambience** beds from [art-audio#ambience](../bible/art-audio.md#ambience): `saltglass_camp` `{ wind: [900, 1.0, 0.05,
   0.04], ev: [['ambPage', 0.04, 0], ['ambCricket', 0.25, 1]] }`; `saltglass_dunes` `{ wind: [1100, 1.2, 0.08, 0.06], ev:
   [['ambGlassChime', 0.12, -1]], dim: { ev: 0 } }` (the dunes sing only once the stone is set). Add the one-shot
   `ambGlassChime` from art-audio if not yet shipped: `function (t) { var n = [1318.5, 1568, 1975.5, 2349]; T({ t: t, f:
   n[(rnd() * 4) | 0], d: 2.0, a: 0.3, v: 0.03, vib: [3, 4], rev: 0.5 }); return 2.1; }`. Each glass chime shows a
   `saltglass_card_glassglint` on the nearest sculpture within 40 m (visual twin).
7. **Manifest**: every key above with `realm: 'saltglass'`; `tools/build-pages.mjs` puts them in the `saltglass` bundle.
   Log every prompt and command in `docs/art/prompts.md`. Raw generations are not committed.

#### Assets
As listed in What 2-4 (all generated in this step by a GPT Sol subagent with `image_gen.imagegen` or a Gemini model,
each prompt = [preamble](../bible/art-audio.md#preamble) + kind suffix + the bible subject). Budget (art-audio bundle
table): terrain ≈ 1,050 KB, cards 5 × ≤ 90 KB, load 260 KB, map 180 KB, sketches 3 × 45 KB: about 2.1 MB of the 2.5 MB
bundle, leaving room for S-246/S-247's portrait and cards.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home-island line (no home change). `node tools/build-pages.mjs` reports the
  `saltglass` bundle ≤ 2.5 MB and core unchanged.
- `?noassets=all&realm=saltglass` renders (fallbacks); with assets the realm suite's palette check sees the Saltglass fog
  colour per region.
- `tests/assets.test.js` (S-029): every new key has a fallback, a `realm` tag and meets its size limit.
- Music unit test: `saltglass` bars sum to `bpb` per bar; `layer` is 0 without the stone and 1 with `stone:saltglass`.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test` (assets, music data). `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (bundle partition,
  offline replay of `?realm=saltglass`). `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (saltglass palette, views).
- Attach to the PR a headless screenshot of each of the 4 views in third person (`G.debug.perf.views('saltglass')` with
  the capture flag) for the owner.

#### Out of scope
Portraits and bestiary cards (S-246, S-247, S-252); `exam` and `buried_academy` music (S-252, S-250).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the realm falls back to home textures and the stub palette.

<a id="s-246"></a>

### S-246 · feat: Sablecamp's people: Sable and the dig crew

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-244, S-068a · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[cast#sable](../bible/cast.md#sable), [atlas-far#saltglass-people](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-people),
[atlas-far#saltglass-interiors](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-interiors), [systems#dialogue](../bible/systems.md#dialogue),
[systems#schedules](../bible/systems.md#schedules)

**Decisions:** the dig crew is the two characters cast.md names, **Grit** (tortoise) and **Quill** (porcupine). The
atlas's third crew spot and the cook spot are props (a sieve on a stool in T3, the stewpot), not people. Sable's spots
use cast's ids and atlas's coordinates: `saltglass.sable_tent` = the finds table (112, −162); `saltglass.dig_site` =
trench T1 (92, −142) until ss-42 moves it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Sablecamp is the Flats' town (no enemies) and Sable is the
first face Pip meets there: the archaeologist who knows the Academy is under the hill.

#### What
1. **Looks** (`js/content/looks.js`, `NPC_LOOKS`; read by the existing `npcDef` cases):
   - `sable`: `rodent` rig, h 0.7 (1.0 with ears), `ears: 'long'` (0.3 m, inner `#f4b3b0`), `tail: 'tuft'` (0.5 m,
     `#3b3540` tip `#ffffff`), long hind-leg capsules; fur `#e2c79a`, belly `#fff8e6`; acc: pith helmet (`#efe3c8`, band
     `#b55d4c`), khaki shirt with pockets (`#c9a46b`), brush in the right paw (`#8a5a30`), notebook (`#8ec9d8`).
   - `grit`: `turtle` rig without the beard, h 0.6, shell `#8a7a5a`, skin `#b5a58a`, sun hat `#efe3c8`; a sieve in both
     hands (pose `work` = slow side-to-side sift, 0.4 Hz).
   - `quill`: `rodent` rig with `spines` (back cone cluster `#6b5a4a`, tips `#efe3c8`), h 0.7, spectacles `#3b3540`, a
     clipboard; pose `work` = writing (head nod 1 Hz).
2. **Spots and schedules** (`js/content/schedules/saltglass.js`, S-174 format):
   - Sable `[[6, 'saltglass.dig_site', 'work'], [12, 'saltglass.windcatcher_nap', 'sleep'], [16, 'saltglass.dig_site',
     'work'], [20, 'saltglass.sable_tent', 'sit']]` where `windcatcher_nap` is 2 m east of the Windcatcher's foot (atlas:
     napping under it 12:00-16:00). Until the library interior opens (S-258a) "indoors" is the tent spot.
   - Grit `[[6, 'saltglass.crew-1', 'work'], [19, 'saltglass.tent_2', 'sleep']]`; Quill `[[7, 'saltglass.crew-2',
     'work'], [19, 'saltglass.tent_3', 'sit']]`. Spots: `crew-1` (90, −145) in T1, `crew-2` (100, −130) in T2,
     `tent_2` (130, −158), `tent_3` (98, −158); each nearest walkable within 3 m, ≥ 3 m from every other spot.
   - The **Library Dig doorway**: a timber-framed hole in the hill's east slope at (30, 50), facing east, with a sign
     `[E] Look · Doorway` `sg.look.librarydig` "A doorway dug into the hill, propped with planks. A sign: 'LIBRARY
     (probably). Do not lean on the planks.'" No portal until S-258a.
3. **Dialogue** (`js/content/dialogue/saltglass.js`, text `js/text/en/sg.js`). Sable's tic is the find-then-deflate;
   she calls Pip "colleague". Grit speaks slowly with "...". Quill reads from notes.
   - Sable greeting (priority 20, `once`, `set: ['set:met:sable']`): `sg.sable.meet.1` "A VISITOR! A real one! Not a
     pot! ...Unless you're a pot. You're not a pot. Hello, colleague. I'm Sable." · `sg.sable.meet.2` "This whole hill is
     a school. Buried to the bell tower. I've been brushing at it for three summers. Brush, don't dig. Digging is rude to
     history."
   - Sable idle (10, rotation by day index): [`sg.sable.idle.1` "A POT! ...Oh. A rock. A lovely rock. I'll label it."],
     [`sg.sable.idle.2` "Every rock is a pot until proven otherwise. That's science. I think."], [`sg.sable.idle.3` "The
     Windcatcher is the only cool spot for miles. I'm not napping under it. I'm observing the shade."].
   - Sable after the stone (20, `when: 'flag:stone:saltglass'`): `sg.sable.stone.1` "The bell RANG. A real bell! In a
     real tower! ...I knew it wasn't a pot. I always knew."
   - Grit idle: [`sg.grit.idle.1` "Sifting... sifting... found a pebble. ...Lovely pebble."], [`sg.grit.idle.2` "Sable
     is very quick. ...I am very careful. ...Between us we are a whole archaeologist."].
   - Quill idle: [`sg.quill.idle.1` "Item four hundred and six: a rock. Labelled 'pot'. I have added a question mark."],
     [`sg.quill.idle.2` "I keep the logbook. Somebody has to write down which rocks are pots."].
   - These are place/idle lines only; Sable's main-quest lines ship with the quest in S-254.
4. **Portrait** `portrait_sable` (`portrait`, 512² ≤ 60 KB, `realm: saltglass`): the cast prompt verbatim ("A tiny,
   over-excited sand-coloured jerboa archaeologist with huge pink ears, a pith helmet with a red band, a khaki shirt with
   many pockets, holding a dusting brush and a cracked pottery shard, eyes shining.") + "Palette: #e2c79a #f4b3b0 #c9a46b
   #b55d4c." Process `export_sprites.py --size 512 --webp 82`. Fallback: circle `#e2c79a` with "S". Grit and Quill use
   the initial-circle fallback (no generated portraits; they are minor cast).
5. **NPC voice blips** (art-audio SFX): Sable `rodent` blip pitched +4 semitones, quick; Grit `turtle` blip −5, slow;
   Quill `rodent` blip +1 with a paper rustle.
6. **Sound cue twin**: Sable's brush swish (`sfx brushSwish`, 0.2 s, every 1.5 s while `work`) shows a puff of sand
   (shared dust card) at her brush.

#### Assets
- `portrait_sable` as above.
- Code-built NPCs via the rig families (no new rig). ART BRIEFs (batch 13): **`npc_sable`**: silhouette a tiny
  big-eared jerboa on long hind legs in a pith helmet; 1.0 m with ears; palette as above; materials toon fur + canvas;
  ≤ 4,000 tris; pose standing with brush, brushing, napping; 3D prompt "[3D preamble] a tiny over-excited jerboa
  archaeologist with huge pink ears, pith helmet with a red band, khaki shirt with many pockets, dusting brush, long
  tufted tail, 1.0 m". **`npc_dig_crew`** (one brief, two looks): a slow tortoise with a sun hat and a sieve (0.6 m) and
  a bespectacled porcupine with a clipboard (0.7 m); ≤ 2,500 tris each; 3D prompts "[3D preamble] a gentle old tortoise
  archaeologist in a straw sun hat holding a sieve, 0.6 m" / "[3D preamble] a small porcupine scholar in round
  spectacles holding a clipboard, 0.7 m".

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home-island line.
- Sablecamp spawns no enemies (region `max 0`); the three NPCs stand at their spots by the schedule (`G.debug.tod`
  6, 13, 21); Talk gives the greeting once, then idle lines that change only with the day index.
- Content lint: all lines ≤ 220 characters, no `~` or `!!`, every text id exists; the portrait key has a fallback and
  `realm: saltglass`.
- Realm suite: the town view within budget with the three NPCs drawn.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (content, schedules). `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (town view). `node tests/inpage.mjs side` is not
  needed; add a small `saltglass` case to the `npcs` checks (S-174's schedule test) asserting the spots at three hours.
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
Sable's quest lines (S-254) and ss-42 (S-258a). The library interior (S-258a).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the camp is empty again, realm hidden.

<a id="s-247"></a>

### S-247 · feat: Saltglass enemies: Sandsnoot, Prickle Pear, Sun Beetle and Dust Puff

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-041, S-244 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[creatures#enemy-sandsnoot](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-sandsnoot), [creatures#enemy-prickle-pear](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-prickle-pear),
[creatures#enemy-sun-beetle](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-sun-beetle), [creatures#enemy-dust-puff](../bible/creatures.md#enemy-dust-puff),
[creatures#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables), [creatures#tiers](../bible/creatures.md#tiers),
[creatures#telegraphs](../bible/creatures.md#telegraphs), [atlas-far#saltglass-enemies](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-enemies)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Four Act III species, all tier 2 patterns (harder by
pattern, never by bigger hits), each with a tell drawn ≥ 0.55 s ahead and a gentle poof.

#### What
1. **Species** (`js/content/enemies/saltglass.js`, entries in `ENEMY`), copied from creatures.md:

   | id | Archetype | HP | r | Move | Emerge | Gems | Poof | Params |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | `sandsnoot` | burrower | 6 | 0.46 | under sand | `sand` | 2 | `#e8b88a` | `{ moundSpeed: 3.2, ringR: 1.3, tell: 0.75, lock: 0.25, exposed: 1.4, dive: 0.4, reach: 8, tier: 2, pops: 2 }` |
   | `prickle_pear` | turret | 5 | 0.46 | stationary | `still` | 2 | `#f08fb8` | `{ turn: 90, aim: 0.80, lock: 0.30, shot: 10, burst: 2, burstGap: 0.35, reload: 1.2, range: 13, tier: 2 }` |
   | `sun_beetle` | shield | 7 | 0.50 | ground | `sand` | 2 | `#f2c14e` | `{ turn: 110, bashWind: 0.65, bash: 1.8, guardArc: 60, stagger: 1.0, tier: 2, secondWind: 0.55 }` |
   | `dust_puff` | kiter | 5 | 0.45 | floats 0.6 m | `sand` | 2 | `#efe3c8` | `{ band: [6, 10], retreat: 3.2, throwWind: 0.60, lob: true, volley: 2, cornerT: 0.5, dizzy: 1.6, tier: 2 }` |

   - **Sandsnoot**: as the Sand Snapper (S-121) but `pops: 2`: after the first pop it dives 0.4 s and a second ring (r
     1.3, its own 0.75 s tell, a sniffing snout poking up) appears where Pip stands when the first pop ends; exposed
     1.4 s gold after the second.
   - **Prickle Pear**: its flower opens and a coral stripe draws for 0.80 s (aim locks 0.30 s before the shot); two soft
     prickles 0.35 s apart down the stripe at 10 m/s; reload 1.2 s gold (flower closed).
   - **Sun Beetle**: polishes its sun-disc shield, flash 0.65 s, bash 1.8 m forward; a second bash after its own 0.55 s
     flash. Guard ±60° (front hits clink); the C chop breaks the guard (stagger 1.0 s).
   - **Dust Puff**: spins faster with a whoosh for 0.60 s, lobs two sand clods onto reticles (each reticle drawn for the
     full wind-up); keeps 6-10 m; cornered for 0.5 s → dizzy 1.6 s gold.
   - Damage: every hit is 1 HP (half a heart). The attack-token rule (≤ 2 winding up or attacking) applies.
2. **Spawn tables** (S-243's regions, exactly [creatures#spawn-tables](../bible/creatures.md#spawn-tables)):
   Sablecamp none, `max 0`; The Glass Dunes `[['sandsnoot', 3], ['dust_puff', 3]]` max 6; The Salt Pans
   `[['sun_beetle', 3], ['dust_puff', 2]]` max 6 + placed `prickle_pear` × 4; Academy Hill `[['sun_beetle', 2],
   ['sandsnoot', 2]]` max 6 + placed `prickle_pear` × 6.
3. **Placed Prickle Pears** (a `placeTurrets` stage on the landmark stream, refresh prints): pans (95, 120), (170, 80),
   (60, 150), (200, 120); hill (−60, 0), (30, 30), (−80, 60), (0, 95), (−40, 85), (25, 70); each nearest walkable within
   6 m and ≥ 10 m from the Lamp Walk, the Drift ramp, every dorm anchor and every spawn. A poofed turret returns on the
   next realm entry (the shipped respawn rule for placed enemies).
4. **Models** (`js/models.js` `enemyDef` cases, toon, code-built): Sandsnoot (round sandy body r 0.3, long soft snout
   cone 0.25 m, big ears with brick-red insides; sniff, pop, dive); Prickle Pear (round pad r 0.35 with two stubby pad
   arms, a pink flower crown that opens, cream prickle dots as a point texture; flower open, recoil); Sun Beetle (amber
   scarab body 0.5 m, a round gold disc held in front with a specular toon band; polish, flash, bash); Dust Puff (three
   stacked tori spinning at 2 turns/s, two eyes, a crown of three blue pebbles orbiting; spin, wobble, dizzy). Wind-up
   poses lean/swell (KEEP) and each wind-up has a sound (`sfx sniff`, `sfx petalOpen`, `sfx shieldFlash`, `sfx whoosh`)
   with the drawn ground shape as its visual twin.
5. **Bestiary** (S-046 shape): entries with `card`, `lore` (Hoot) and `tip` (Clover), text from creatures.md verbatim:
   - `sandsnoot`: "The Sandsnoot can smell a biscuit through ten metres of sand." / "It pops up twice! Keep moving."
   - `prickle_pear`: "Prickle Pears are very huggable, from a distance." / "It reloads after two. Run in!"
   - `sun_beetle`: "The Sun Beetle polishes its shield every morning. Vanity or hygiene? Further study needed." / "Go
     behind it. It bashes twice!"
   - `dust_puff`: "Dust Puffs are tidying the desert, one corner at a time." / "Chase it into a wall. It gets dizzy."
6. **Dungeon use**: the same ids serve d9's waves (S-250/S-251).

#### Assets
- Bestiary cards (`card-beast`, 384² ≤ 45 KB, `realm: saltglass`), prompts = preamble + `card-beast` + the creatures.md
  card line + palette: `card_sandsnoot` "a round sandy desert creature with a long soft snout and big ears popping up out
  of pale dunes, sniffing the air. Palette: #c9a46b #e8b88a #b55d4c #2a2440." · `card_prickle_pear` "a round friendly
  cactus with two stubby pad arms and a pink flower on its head, in a pale salt desert. Palette: #8fae6a #6b8a4a #f08fb8
  #fff3d8." · `card_sun_beetle` "an amber scarab beetle proudly holding up a polished round golden disc like a shield,
  standing on bright salt flats. Palette: #c9873a #f2c14e #5a3a2a #fff3d8." · `card_dust_puff` "a tiny friendly dust
  devil of pale sand with two round eyes and three blue pebbles circling its top, whirling over glassy dunes. Palette:
  #efe3c8 #c9a46b #8ec9d8 #2a2440." Process `export_sprites.py --size 384 --webp 82`. Fallback: tinted blob card.
- ART BRIEFs (batch 13), from creatures.md: **`enemy_sandsnoot`** round desert critter with a long snout and big ears ·
  0.6 m · fur `#c9a46b`, snout `#e8b88a`, inner ears `#b55d4c`, eyes `#2a2440` · 900 / 3,000 · sniff, pop, dive.
  **`enemy_prickle_pear`** round cactus with a flower crown · 0.9 m · pad `#8fae6a`, shade `#6b8a4a`, flower `#f08fb8`,
  prickles `#fff3d8` · 900 / 3,000 · flower open, recoil. **`enemy_sun_beetle`** scarab holding up a round sun disc · 0.8
  m · shell `#c9873a`, disc `#f2c14e`, body `#5a3a2a` · 1,000 / 3,500 · polish, flash, bash. **`enemy_dust_puff`** small
  spinning dust devil with eyes · 0.8 m · sand `#efe3c8`, swirl `#c9a46b`, pebbles `#8ec9d8` · 600 / 2,000 · spin,
  wobble, dizzy. 3D prompt pattern: "[3D preamble] <silhouette line>, hand-painted toon texture, low-poly under <GLB
  tris> triangles, <size> m".

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home-island line.
- `tests/creatures.test.js`: the four entries name registered archetypes; HP in the Act III band (4-7); params within
  each archetype's allowed range; computed lead (first warning to first damage) ≥ 0.55 s for every attack, including
  the Sandsnoot's second pop and the Sun Beetle's second bash; every species has `card`, `lore`, `tip`, `realm`; spawn
  tables equal creatures.md (Sablecamp `max 0`; every `max ≤ 7`).
- In-page `species` suite, one case per species in a test arena (`G.debug.actors.spawn(id, x, z)`): the telegraph is
  drawn ≥ 0.55 s before the first damage frame; poof at 0 HP; bestiary count +1; the Sun Beetle's front hit clinks and
  the C chop staggers; the Dust Puff dizzies when cornered.
- Ten Prickle Pears stand at their anchors with the clearances; Sablecamp never spawns.

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (creatures, content). `node tests/inpage.mjs species` (four new cases). `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`
  (saltglass views with enemies live; still within budget).
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (four cards in the bundle).

#### Out of scope
d9 wave placement (S-250/S-251). Scarabelle (S-252).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; regions go back to empty tables.

<a id="s-248a"></a>

### S-248a · feat: the Ivy Gloves: a climb state on ivy lattices

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-048, S-243 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: `ability:climb` (debug-granted until
S-251 places the Gloves) · Bible: [systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools),
[systems#tool-gloves](../bible/systems.md#tool-gloves), [systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates),
[systems#abilities](../bible/systems.md#abilities), [systems#brief-tools](../bible/systems.md#brief-tools),
[charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person), [charter#verbs-new](../bible/charter.md#verbs-new)

INDEX's S-248 is split: this part ships the ability, the `ivy` gate object and their tests; S-248b ships the three
home climbs (see Index changes).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. The ninth tool: Pip climbs anything with ivy on it. It is
the reward of the Buried Academy and the key to the Starstair's ivy route (S-264), so it lands first as a tested
movement state that nothing in the shipped game can reach yet.

#### What
1. **Item and ability**: `ITEMS.gloves = { name: 'Ivy Gloves', kind: 'tool', icon: 'icon_gloves', desc: 'Knitted
   gloves with leafy grip pads. Walk into ivy to climb it.', stack: 1, beat: true, passive: true }`; getting it applies
   `ability:climb` ([systems#abilities](../bible/systems.md#abilities)). Passive: not in the tool ring; no HUD slot.
   Item-get line (used by S-251): "You got the Ivy Gloves! Walk into an ivy lattice to climb it."
2. **Gate object `ivy`** (`js/gates.js`): `{ type: 'ivy', needs: 'ability:climb', faces: [{ x, z, rot, w, y0, y1, top
   }] }` where a face is a vertical rectangle `w` wide from `y0` to `y1` on a wall plane with outward normal `rot`;
   `top` is `{ kind: 'deck' | 'ground', id }`, the walkable surface Pip mantles onto. Look: the `gate_ivy` lattice
   texture on a quad 0.04 m proud of the wall. **Without the Gloves**, walking into it for 0.2 s plays a slide-down
   wobble and a toast "Pip slides off. The ivy is too thin to grip." (once per 10 s). **First-person cue**: a leaf rustle
   (`sfx ivyRustle`) within 6 m, with leaves trembling on the lattice as its visual twin.
3. **Climb state** (`js/climb.js`, a module with `beforeStep`/`afterStep`, plus a minimal branch in `js/player.js`):
   - Enter: Pip grounded or airborne, `ability:climb`, moving into a face (input dot normal < −0.5) within 0.35 m of it
     for **0.2 s** → `player.state = 'climb'` with `player.mods.climb = { face, u, v }` (u across the face, v up). The
     player's `step` for `'climb'` is one `case` that defers to `player.mods.climb.step(dt, input)` (systems: movement
     tools work through `player.mods`; the only `player.js` change is this dispatch and the state name).
   - Move: **1.5 m/s** up, down and sideways (stick/WASD mapped to the face: forward = up, back = down, left/right =
     across); no stamina; clamp u to [0.3, w − 0.3]; gravity off.
   - **Hop** jumps off backwards **2 m** (vy of a normal hop, horizontal 4 m/s along the normal for 0.5 s).
   - **Mantle**: reaching `y1 − 0.2` while pushing up → 0.4 s mantle onto `top` (lerp over the lip to 0.6 m inside the
     deck or ground), state `normal`.
   - **Bottom**: moving down to within 0.1 m of the ground under the face → state `normal`.
   - Attack, roll, potion and tool are ignored while climbing; getting hurt (only a projectile can reach Pip there)
     drops him off backwards like a hop with the usual hurt i-frames.
   - Camera: third person keeps the spring arm (it already slides off walls); boom pitch biased +8° so the face does not
     fill the view. First person: the eye stays 0.30 m off the face; pitch is free; the viewmodel shows both arms reaching
     up alternately at the climb rhythm with the gloves (`tool_gloves`, rounded boxes `#4f8a3a` with pads `#7fbf5a`).
     Gloves are shown only while climbing ([charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person): a worn reward is
     visible in third person and in Pip's shadow; the gloves show on Pip's hands in third person while climbing).
   - Pill: `Climb` on the targeted face is shown with no key ([charter#verbs-new](../bible/charter.md#verbs-new)).
4. **Solver** (`js/progress-graph.js`): the `climb` ability model is "ivy any height on a lattice face"; a deck whose
   only way up is an `ivy` face needs `ability:climb`. Add a fixture graph case.
5. **Test lattice**: the debug sandbox realm (S-019) gains one 6 m tall, 2 m wide lattice on a 6 m wall with a 3 × 3 m
   deck on top at y 6 (sandbox only; it never ships to players). Debug: `G.debug.ability('climb')`,
   `G.debug.climb.state()` → `{ on, face, u, v }`.
6. **Glove art**: ART BRIEF **`tool_gloves`** (batch 13): knitted green gloves with leaf-shaped grip pads · 0.2 m · knit
   `#4f8a3a`, pads `#7fbf5a` · hands reach up alternately · image "a pair of knitted green gloves with leaf-shaped grip
   pads on the palms, isolated, transparent background" · 3D "low-poly toon knitted glove with leaf pads, 400 tris" ·
   fallback rounded boxes on the hands.

#### Assets
- **`tex_ivy_lattice`** (generated `tile`-style with alpha, 512² webp ≤ 60 KB, core: ivy appears on home and in several
  lands): PREAMBLE + `card` + "a seamless tile of a wooden garden lattice covered in thick green ivy leaves, front view,
  flat lighting, transparent gaps. Palette: #9b7a52 #4f8a3a #7fbf5a #ffffff." Process: white-to-alpha one-liner, then
  `python3 -I tools/make_seamless.py <in> <out> --vertical`; export 512 webp q82. Fallback: a green quad with a canvas
  lattice pattern (`#9b7a52` diagonal grid over `#4f8a3a`).
- **`icon_gloves`** (128² cell, core): PREAMBLE + `icon` + "a pair of knitted green gloves with leaf-shaped grip pads".
  Fallback: canvas glove shape `#4f8a3a`.
- `tool_gloves` code-built (above).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except "Frozen realms" (no realm generated; the sandbox is debug-only) and the home line is kept
  (no home change).
- `tests/climb.test.js` (pure helpers): face mapping of input; clamp; the mantle target lies on the deck; the bottom exit.
- In-page `tools` suite case `climb` in the sandbox: without the ability, walking into the lattice shows the slide-off
  toast and Pip stays grounded; with it, Pip enters the climb after 0.2 s, rises 6 m in 4.0 ± 0.3 s, mantles onto the
  deck, can climb down to the ground, and Hop throws him 2 ± 0.3 m off the face; first and third person both work; no
  `modErrs`.
- The solver fixture: a deck behind ivy is unreachable without `climb` and reachable with it.
- `keys.test.js` and `padbinds.test.js` unchanged (no new action).

#### Verification plan
- `bun test` (climb, progress-graph, items). `node tests/inpage.mjs tools` (climb case). `bun run test:smoke`.
- `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa` (one texture, one icon in core).

#### Out of scope
Home climbs (S-248b); Saltglass lattices (S-249b); the Gloves chest (S-251); the Starstair ivy (S-264).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR. The state is entered only with `ability:climb`, which no player can have yet.

<a id="s-248b"></a>

### S-248b · feat: three home climbs: the Great Tree to the Crown Balcony, the windmill and the lighthouse

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-248a, S-078 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[atlas-home#ivy-finds](../bible/atlas-home.md#ivy-finds), [atlas-home#great-tree](../bible/atlas-home.md#great-tree),
[atlas-home#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes), [atlas-home#vistas](../bible/atlas-home.md#vistas),
[systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Pacing rule 5: every tool opens at least one find on home.
The Gloves open three, including the best view of the isle from Wenna's own crown.

#### What
1. **Post-pass** `placeIvyFinds` (home pass row 23, `new RNG(7151 * 173 + 103)`, [atlas-home#passes](../bible/atlas-home.md#passes)),
   run after `placeThornglass`, guarded by `o.placeIvyFinds !== false`, writing only `W.ivyFinds`, restoring `W.walk /
   W.reach`, following the `placeShells` rules (nearest valid around a named anchor, 160 tries then the 0.5 m scan).
   Solids and decks go in each record's `solid` / `deck` and are attached by `attachPost(W)` after generation (rule 7).
   The pass needs almost no randomness: the stream only breaks ties in the scan.
2. **The three finds** (ids in this order):

   | id | Lattice face | Top (deck) | Reward |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `home:ivy-tree` | 1.4 m wide on the Great Tree's west face at tree-local `az` −1.55, from the root-flare top (tree-local y 0.6) to 10.5 m, laid on the trunk radius `TW.trunkR(y)` (a curved strip of 1 m quads following the radius) | the **Crown Balcony**: a round deck nest on the west side, `az` −1.9 to −1.2, 2.4 m deep, deck top at **10.5 m** (Y ≈ 12.7), at least 1.0 m below the lowest foliage on that bearing on both the GLB and the fallback tree (measure in the PR; lower in 0.5 m steps until true, and move the lattice top with it) | the vista post for **`home:v4`** "From Wenna's Crown" (data from S-076; the post placed here; looks west over the Meadow to the sea, pitch −10°) |
   | `home:ivy-windmill` | up the windmill tower's north face (away from the sails), base to 9.0 m | a 1.6 × 1.2 m ledge deck at base + 9.0 m (Y ≈ 12.8), view west over the sea | a 25-gem pouch (`S.chests['home:ivy-windmill']`) |
   | `home:ivy-lighthouse` | up the lighthouse's south face beside the portholes, from the sand to the gallery | a porthole ledge 1.2 × 1.0 m at the upper porthole (Y ≈ 8.3); the lattice continues to Pinch's Lookout (S-102) as a second way up | a tin box: 25 gems and a note (`[E] Read · Note`) `home.ivy.note` "Pinch's emergency biscuits. Do NOT eat. — P." (the biscuits stay in the box) |

   - Crown Balcony look: planks on root brackets with a root railing, open on the lattice side; `occ: true` materials so
     the third-person camera dithers it.
   - The windmill lattice must stay ≥ 1.5 m clear of the sail sweep; the lighthouse lattice ≥ 0.6 m from the door and
     the shipped portholes' glow quads.
3. **Gate records**: each lattice is an `ivy` gate face (S-248a) with `top: { kind: 'deck', id }`. Pouch and tin are
   containers in `S.chests` (home caches, counted in home's chest row and the "Tool finds" row). The solver gains the
   three finds (pacing rule 5: `climb` opens ≥ 1 home find).
4. **Economy**: +50 gems to the home tool-finds ledger (atlas-home's 615 total includes ivy 50); `tests/economy.test.js`.
5. **Completion**: home "Tool finds" row +2 containers (the vista counts in Vistas); regenerate `js/realms/stats.js`.

#### Assets
- The Crown Balcony (code-built, ≤ 400 tris): planks `#9b7a52`, root brackets `#6f5a44`, railing of bent roots `#7a5a3a`.
  ART BRIEF (batch 13): "a round wooden balcony nest built into a giant tree's trunk, on root brackets, a railing of bent
  roots, under the canopy" · 2.4 m deep · 400 / 1,500 tris · 3D prompt "low-poly toon round wooden tree balcony on root
  brackets with a root railing, 1,500 tris". Fallback: a cylinder slice deck.
- Ledges (windmill, porthole): planks on two brackets, 60 tris. Tin box: reuse the cache-box prop with a `#c8ccd4` tint.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. `tests/fixtures/world-prints.json` byte-identical.
- `tests/world.test.js` (SLOW, seeds 1-40): `generateWorld(seed)` and `generateWorld(seed, { placeIvyFinds: false })`
  have equal `placementPrint`, `fingerprint`, `walk`, `reach`, `unreachable`; `W.ivyFinds` has the three ids in order;
  each deck is unreachable on foot and reachable with `climb` in the post-solid walk grid (rule 9d); the Crown Balcony
  deck is ≥ 1.0 m below foliage on that bearing (both tree models).
- In-page `tools` suite case `ivy-home` (seed 7151): climb each lattice from its foot, mantle onto its deck, open the
  pouch / tin (+25 each, once), sketch `home:v4` from the balcony; third and first person.
- Realm suite home views unchanged within ±2 draw calls.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test` (world SLOW group, economy, stats). `node tests/inpage.mjs tools`. `node tests/inpage.mjs wp9`
  (home world module still green). `bun run test:smoke`; `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I untouched).

#### Out of scope
The Starstair ivy route (S-264). Saltglass lattices (S-249b).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the pass and its post-decks disappear and home prints were never touched.
