<a id="s-249a"></a>

### S-249a · feat: Saltglass finds: chests, keepsakes, acorns, vistas, six Lamp Logs, Hollow doors, benches

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-244, S-074 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[atlas-far#saltglass-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-pois), [atlas-far#saltglass-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-keepsakes),
[atlas-far#saltglass-curios](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-curios), [atlas-far#saltglass-arrival](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-arrival),
[atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes), [lore#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [lore#bottles](../bible/lore.md#bottles),
[systems#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids), [systems#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles)

INDEX's S-249 is split: this part places the plain finds (`placeFinds`); S-249b places the tool-gated finds and the
two puzzles and runs the coverage test (see Index changes).

**Decisions:**
- **Ids** follow [systems#ids](../bible/systems.md#ids) (`c<n>`, `k<n>`, `a<n>`, `v<n>`, `b<n>`), mapped from the atlas
  labels: chests `c01..c16` → `saltglass:c1..c16`; keepsakes badge, fork, paper, bookmark → `saltglass:k1..k4`; acorns
  `acorn-1..11` → `saltglass:a1..a11`; vistas shine, wind, school → `saltglass:v1..v3`; benches camp, walk, gate, organ,
  well, dorms → `saltglass:b1..b6`.
- **Lamp Logs**: the atlas places six slots and lore numbers them (atlas-far decision 9). Lore puts logs 29 and 30 as
  twin niches in the bell tower's porch, so the slot mapping is: atlas `log-5` (Sable's finds table, "PAPER? (Ask an
  expert)") → **25** *The Academy* (lore: "niche at Sablecamp"); `log-2` Dormitory Row → **26** *The Starstair*
  (Academy Hill); `log-4` the Glass Organ's base → **27** *A Step of Sky* (the Dunes); `log-3` the Practice Beacon's foot
  → **28** *Given Light* (the Pans); `log-6` the Drift Door's lintel → **29** *The Answering Star*; and atlas `log-1`
  moves from the Lamp Walk's first stone to the twin niche beside it on the Drift landing → **30** *The Last Page*. Keys
  `saltglass:25..30` ([systems#collectibles](../bible/systems.md#collectibles)).
- **Bottle**: atlas `bottle-1` (60, −228) holds global bottle **6** (Tuppence, the dormouse family's letter;
  [lore#bottles](../bible/lore.md#bottles)), key `saltglass:6`.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. The Flats must take hours to explore: every 40 × 40 m cell
holds a find, a view or a path to one.

#### What
1. **Pass** `placeFinds` on `new RNG(40277 * 71 + 3)` (far table), in `js/realms/finds/saltglass.js`, run as the realm's
   post-pass (after prints are taken; it restores `W.walk / W.reach` and adds to no generator list). Each item is
   placed nearest-walkable within 12 m of its anchor (6 m where the atlas says "on" a prop or deck), on reachable ground
   unless its gate says otherwise.
2. **Chests** (`saltglass:c1..c16`, 30 gems each, the shared chest prop). Place all 16 containers now; gates that need
   S-249b's objects are recorded on the item and stay inert (closed and unreachable) until S-249b:

   | id | Anchor | Placed as | Gate (S-249b unless "now") |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | c1 | (115, −165) | under the finds table's tarp | now: open |
   | c2 | (95, −140) | buried in trench T1 | Paws (`placeBuried`, S-249b) |
   | c3 | (25, −40) | hidden under the Academy gate's sand; rises when all 12 practice lamps burn at night | S-249b puzzle |
   | c4 | (−195, −45) | hidden at the Organ's foot; rises when the Dune Chord is struck | S-249b puzzle |
   | c5 | (−150, −150) | a dune crest in the north | now |
   | c6 | (−230, 60) | a wind-scooped hollow | now |
   | c7 | (−240, −110), y 10 | on a glass shelf deck 3 × 2 m at y 10 (placed here, `deck` record), reached by gliding 50 m from the Organ ledge (y 18) | Glider (no object) |
   | c8 | (120, 50) | the Mirage Well's south rim | now |
   | c9 | (60, 170) | a salt cave mouth in a pan edge (a 3 × 3 m alcove of salt blocks) | now |
   | c10 | (150, 110), y 16 | on the Practice Beacon's top deck | Hook (hookbloom, S-249b) |
   | c11 | (200, 60) | behind a salt-block door (2 × 2.4 m) | Sunglass (receiver, S-249b) |
   | c12 | (−85, 85) | in the last cottage of Dormitory Row (dorm-1's doorway) | now |
   | c13 | (−10, 75) | the old exam garden behind the tower | now |
   | c14 | (−20, 40), y 36 | on the belfry deck | Ivy Gloves (tower south face, S-249b) |
   | c15 | (−45, 105) | behind a cellar door sealed with a wax plug | Ember Sling (1 hit, S-249b) |
   | c16 | (−265, 10) | behind a star door in the far west dunes | Starsong at night (`placeNightFinds`, S-249b) |
3. **Keepsakes** (`saltglass:k1..k4`, the shipped keepsake pattern: twinkle within 6 m + two-note shimmer, `[E] Look`,
   toast "Secret found n/44"): k1 First-year badge (138, −160), label "Something dusty"; k2 Brass tuning fork (−215, −65),
   "Something singing"; k3 Singed exam paper (160, 122), "Something scorched"; k4 Moth-wing bookmark (−20, 52) on the
   tower's south face (reachable at the foot), "Something stitched". Texts verbatim from
   [atlas-far#saltglass-keepsakes](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-keepsakes) (`sg.keep.k<n>.1` / `.2`), and the fifth
   line on finding all four: `sg.keep.fifth` "✿ Saltglass's keepsakes: the Academy's last students were young once. One of
   them still keeps a window lit." Each joins the Keepsake Hollow shelf for Saltglass in Pip's Nook (S-056 shelf order).
4. **Sleeping Acorns** `saltglass:a1..a11` at (130, −175), (70, −105), (−120, −100), (−210, −150), (−250, 40),
   (−160, 120), (100, 80), (180, 150), (40, 190), (−60, 20), (10, 95); the shared snore cue (10 m) and `[T] Wake ·
   Sleeping Acorn` (Sunglass, 1.5 s); a sapling grows where woken (S-161 rule).
5. **Vistas** (Hoot's posts, `[E] Sketch · <view>`): `saltglass:v1` "The Flats Shine" on the Practice Beacon deck (150,
   110) y 16 (Hook, reached in S-249b), sketch `sketch_saltglass_1`; `v2` "Wind Music" on the Organ's dune (−170, −70) y 14
   (open), `sketch_saltglass_2`; `v3` "The Whole School" in the belfry (−20, 40) y 36 (Ivy, S-249b), `sketch_saltglass_3`.
   Hoot's captions (`sg.vista.v<n>.caption`): v1 "Salt, salt, salt, and a very proud beacon. Note: bring a hat." · v2
   "The dunes hum. I hummed back. We have agreed on nothing." · v3 "From up here the school looks like it is waiting for
   the bell. It is." (Captions are new text; ≤ 100 characters each.)
6. **Lamp Logs** (the shared log niche prop, `[E] Read · Lamp Log`, `letter` frame, texts from
   [lore#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs) numbers 25-30, toast "✿ Lamp Log {number} (n/30)"): 25 at Sable's finds
   table (112, −158) with Sable's label card "PAPER? (Ask an expert)"; 26 Dormitory Row (−60, 70); 27 the Glass Organ's base
   (−190, −55); 28 the Practice Beacon's foot (145, 100); 29 and 30 twin niches either side of the Drift Door on the
   landing (−21.5, 36) and (−18.5, 36), y 18 (reached by the ramp; open). Each read joins Granny Wick's library bookcase
   (S-088's 30 slots).
7. **Bottle** `saltglass:6` on the north shore (60, −228): bob and glint, `[E] Open · A bottle`, `letter` frame with
   Tuppence's hand style from lore, toast "✿ A letter in a bottle! (n/12)".
8. **Hollow doors** (the S-082 spiral door, portals `saltglass:hollow1..3` → `hollow_saltglass_1..3`, built in S-255;
   the doors show "The spiral is cold." until then): `hollow_saltglass_1` The Dune Hollow, a dune cave mouth at (−240,
   −120); `hollow_saltglass_2` The Trellis Hollow, a ruined classroom roof doorway at (−95, 30); `hollow_saltglass_3`
   The Sun-Dial Hollow, a salt-block hut at (200, 150) whose door is receiver-gated (S-249b).
9. **Benches** (the S-082 bench, `[E] Sit · Bench`, travel keys `travel:saltglass:b<n>`): b1 camp (105, −150), b2 walk
   (55, −90), b3 gate (15, −25), b4 organ (−185, −40), b5 well (125, 30), b6 dorms (−70, 90). Lamplighter benches face
   Starseed Isle's bearing from Saltglass (bearing 22°; lore: every bench faces home).
10. **Root-gate** `saltglass:gate`: the shared root-gate knot at the Acacia Root's foot (−40, 125), sealed; `[T] Dig ·
    Soft earth` opens it from this side (Paws; `travel:saltglass:gate`), joining the Rootdeep hub (S-182 menu row).
11. **Fish spot** (`placeFishSpots`, `new RNG(40277 * 83 + 9)`): the Mirage Well (118, 45), water type "spring" (S-207's
    fish table rows for Saltglass).
12. **Curios** (28, `[E] Look · <thing>`, one line, no reward; ids `saltglass:q1..q28`, text `sg.curio.<n>`): anchors and
    lines verbatim from [atlas-far#saltglass-curios](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-curios) rows 1-28.
13. **Counts test** and **Completion**: regenerate `js/realms/stats.js` (`tools/realm-stats.mjs`) with Saltglass's
    group: 16 chests (+ d9's 4 dungeon chests in S-250/S-251), 4 keepsakes, 11 acorns, 3 vistas, 6 logs, 1 bottle, 3
    Hollows, 6 benches. The group stays hidden in Completion while the realm is hidden.

**Prize chests** (CANON §10.4; rows already in `js/content/prizes.js`, opened by S-086a's chest path): this land's
three prize chests give their item instead of 30 gems: `c13` Saltglass Hourglass and `c03` Dune Sand trim (placed with the Lamp Walk in S-249b), `c04` "Academy Bell" sticker (placed with the Dune Chord in S-249b). Add one unit test that each listed chest resolves to its
prize, and keep `STATS` chest counts unchanged.

#### Assets
- Glass shelf (c7, code-built, 3 × 2 m slab of `windGlass`, 40 tris), salt cave alcove (salt blocks reused), salt-block
  door (c11, 2 × 2.4 m `saltCrust`, 24 tris), cellar door (c15, a slanted wooden hatch in the hill, 60 tris). No generated
  images (sketches came with S-245).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line; the realm prints file is unchanged by this pass (the pass runs after
  prints).
- `tests/realms/saltglass.test.js`: counts 16 / 4 / 11 / 3 / 6 / 1 / 3 / 6 / 1 / 1 / 28 (chests, keepsakes, acorns,
  vistas, logs, bottle, Hollow doors, benches, root-gate, fish spot, curios); each item within its radius of its anchor;
  every ungated item reachable; c7 / c10 / c14 decks unreachable on foot; the pass is deterministic and changes no other
  list (run with and without it).
- `tests/stats.test.js` equals regeneration; content lint green (keepsake texts ≤ 220, captions ≤ 100, ids unique).
- In-page `realm` suite: Saltglass views within budget with the finds drawn.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test`. `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`. `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
Gate objects, puzzles and the coverage test (S-249b). Hollows' interiors (S-255).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the realm is hidden and the pass is additive.

<a id="s-249b"></a>

### S-249b · feat: Saltglass gate finds, the Lamp Walk and the Dune Chord, and the coverage test

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-249a, S-248a, S-078, S-086b, S-180 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[atlas-far#saltglass-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-pois), [atlas-far#saltglass-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-states),
[atlas-far#saltglass-coverage](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-coverage), [atlas-far#poi-kinds](../bible/atlas-far.md#poi-kinds),
[systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates), [systems#coverage-test](../bible/systems.md#coverage-test),
[systems#abilities](../bible/systems.md#abilities)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Pacing rule 5 (every land re-opens finds for later tools)
and the coverage rule: the Flats' tool-gated corners, the two little puzzles that reward looking closely (a potsherd's
four pillars; twelve lamps at night), and the proof that no cell is empty.

#### What
1. **Gate objects** (each on its own far stream, [atlas-far#far-passes](../bible/atlas-far.md#far-passes)):
   - `placeBuried` (`new RNG(40277 * 73 + 5)`): a `soft_earth` mound on c2 in trench T1 (95, −140) (`[T] Dig · Soft
     earth`, 1.0 s, the chest rises).
   - `placeThornglass` (`new RNG(40277 * 79 + 7)`): five `thornglass` thickets at (−130, −40), (−260, 100), (90, 130),
     (−90, 120), (160, −120), each walling a small glass grotto (3 × 3 m, the c7 glass material) that holds a **15-gem
     pouch** `saltglass:tg1..tg5` (Starsteel; 3 hits). The pouches are containers in `S.chests` counted in Saltglass's
     chest row like home caches. Thornglass guards optional ground only ([systems#gates](../bible/systems.md#gates));
     atlas-far leaves the reward to systems, which names none, so this step adds **75 gems** to the ledger as a new row
     "Far thornglass pouches" in `js/content/economy.js` (sources only; the ≥ 105 % rule only gets easier).
   - `placeNightFinds` (`new RNG(40277 * 89 + 11)`): a `star_door` at (−265, 10) facing east before c16 (opens to *Dusk*
     at night within 4 m).
   - Hookbloom on the Practice Beacon's east face, head at y 17, pad 1.0 m from the top deck edge; from the pans at
     ≤ 14 m (c10 and vista v1).
   - Wax plug on c15's cellar door (`bell_target`-style hit box, 1 ember: it melts, the hatch opens).
2. **Receivers** (`receiver` gate, 1.0 s of beam): (a) c11's salt-block door (the door slides 2 m sideways); (b) the
   Practice Beacon's crown cradle (lights its lamp for good, `gate:saltglass:beaconcrown`; a view-only reward: the
   beacon glows at night); (c) the Sun-Dial Hollow hut's door (opens the door for S-255's portal); (d-f) three ruin
   walls on Academy Hill nearest (−40, 10), (0, 80), (−70, 40): each lights its arched window for good and reveals a
   painted line inside (curio-style Look, `sg.window.1..3`): "A classroom, desks in rows, a chalk drawing of a lamp with
   wings." · "A slate on the wall: 'Light is not lost when it is given. It is only somewhere else.'" · "Coat hooks, each
   with a name, too faded to read. One hook still has a scarf on it." No gems (the ledger is not changed).
3. **Ivy lattices** (`ivy` gate faces, S-248a): (1) the bell tower's south face from the hilltop to the belfry deck (y
   14 → 36; c14 and v3); (2) dorm-4's chimney (a 2 m face to the roof ridge, where a curio `sg.curio.chimney` reads "A
   tiny weathervane shaped like a moth sits on the chimney pot. Whatever the wind does, it points north-east. Home."); (3) and (4) two ruin walls
   on the hill's west slope nearest (−95, 25) and (−60, −5), each topped by a small deck with a curio (`sg.curio.wall1`
   "From the wall top you can see the shape of the old school under the sand: three wings and a courtyard." ·
   `sg.curio.wall2` "Someone carved 'P. + W. were here' in the wall top. The P is much bigger."); (5) the Windcatcher's
   mast (to a lookout ring at y 8: acorn a1's view; no reward, a curio
   line `sg.curio.mast` "From the mast the camp looks like spilled sweets. Sable is the red one.") ; (6) the Glass
   Organ's back (y 14 dune → the y 18 ledge, the glide launch for c7).
4. **The Lamp Walk (c3)**: the 12 lamp posts from S-244 become `[E] Kindle · Lamp` targets (Lantern lit, at night only:
   by day "The lamp wants dusk." is shown on Use). Each kindled lamp burns until dawn (`S.dungeons`-style runtime state
   kept in flag `saltglass.lamps` as a 12-bit number, rewritable, cleared at dawn). When all 12 burn on one night: the
   chest c3 rises from the sand at the Academy gate (1.2 s, `sfx chestRise`) and the flag `gate:saltglass:lampwalk` is
   set; from then on the 12 lamps light every night by themselves (atlas world state "c03 solved"). Hint text for the
   journal's land page: none (it is a find, not a quest); curio 6 names the rule ("Trim, then flame").
5. **The Dune Chord (c4)**: the 16 sculptures (S-244 `W.sculptures`) become strike targets: a Swing or an ember hit
   plays its note (bell voice, 1.6 s decay) and a `saltglass_card_glassglint` flash (visual twin). The four nearest the
   Organ are `i = 0..3` (tall, short, leaning, cracked). Curio 3 (the potsherd) shows the order "tall, short, leaning,
   cracked". Striking those four in that order with ≤ 6 s between strikes raises c4 at the Organ's foot (1.2 s); any
   other strike resets the sequence silently. `gate:saltglass:chord` saved.
6. **Solver**: every placement above becomes a find node with its `needs` (Paws, Starsteel `sword3`, Starsong + night,
   Hook, Sling, Sunglass, `climb`, Glider for c7 from the Organ ledge (which itself needs `climb`), Lantern + night for
   c3). Pacing rule 5 report lines for Saltglass (each Act II and III tool opens ≥ 1 find here).
7. **Coverage test** (`tests/coverage.test.js`, the shipped module `js/realms/coverage.js`): add `saltglass` with
   S-086's cell rule. Atlas expectation: land cells **140-156**; additionally assert that **≥ 70 %** of land cells pass by
   an anchor or a path cell (not by line of sight alone; [atlas-far#saltglass-coverage](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-coverage)).
   **Density**: count POI anchors per [atlas-far#poi-kinds](../bible/atlas-far.md#poi-kinds) (curios count; gem trails and
   enemies do not). The atlas budget is 160 at 200,000 m² of land, of which 12 brush spots arrive with ss-42 (S-258a),
   so this step asserts **≥ 148** and the measured land area / 1,250 minus 12; S-258a raises it to the full rule. If a
   cell fails, add a curio near it (atlas-far decision 6) and list it in the PR.

#### Assets
- All gate objects reuse the shared looks (`gate_soft_earth`, `gate_thornglass`, `gate_star_door`, `gate_hookbloom`,
  `gate_receiver`, `gate_ivy`). Wax plug: a 0.5 m dome of cream wax `#f4ead8` on the hatch, 40 tris. Lit lamp: the
  S-244 lamp post with its glass `nightGlow` 1.0 and the shared lamp-pool sprite (no point light; the 4-light pool picks
  the nearest).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line.
- Unit: five thornglass + caches, one mound, one star door, one hookbloom, one wax plug, six receivers, six lattices,
  12 kindle-able lamps and 16 strike-able sculptures are placed within radius of their anchors; each pass changes no
  other list; the solver proves every find reachable with the full toolset and the pacing-rule-5 lines print.
- `bun test` coverage: Saltglass land cells within 140-156, no failing cell, ≥ 70 % pass by anchor or path, POI count ≥
  148 (raised in S-258a).
- In-page `tools` suite case `saltglass-finds`: kindle the 12 lamps at night via `G.debug.tod(22)` and the Lantern;
  assert c3 rises, and that the next night the lamps relight themselves; strike sculptures 0-3 in order and assert c4
  rises; a wrong order raises nothing; climb the tower to the belfry and open c14.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test` (coverage, solver, saltglass). `node tests/inpage.mjs tools`. `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`.
- `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
The bell's healed states (S-254); Hollow interiors (S-255); brush spots and nameplates (S-258a/b).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; S-249a's plain finds stay.

<a id="s-250"></a>

### S-250 · feat: the Buried Academy rooms 1-7: sand-flow tiles and nameplate doors

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-248a, S-053 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm `buried_academy` hidden (the
parent `saltglass` is hidden) · Bible: [dungeons#d9](../bible/dungeons.md#d9), [dungeons#format](../bible/dungeons.md#format),
[dungeons#entities](../bible/dungeons.md#entities), [dungeons#solver](../bible/dungeons.md#solver),
[dungeons#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table),
[art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)

**Decision (the entrance):** dungeons.md says the Academy is entered "through the half-buried bell tower's top window";
atlas-far places the door, the **Drift Door** at y 18 on the tower's north face; r1 "The Belfry Stair" climbs out of the
tower. They describe the same opening: the portal is the Drift Door (S-244's door mesh).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Dungeon 9's first half: sand rivers that carry Pip and
blocks, and doors that want a student's name instead of a key.

#### What
1. **Data** `js/realms/dungeons/buried_academy.js`: the composite map `buried_academy L1`, the legend extension and the
   room table copied **verbatim** from [dungeons#d9](../bible/dungeons.md#d9) (the S-049 map test compares the two),
   with rooms r8-r14 present in the map but **sealed**: the two openings from the first half into the second, the
   nameplate door `N` (10,12) (r7 → r8) and the door (35,16) (r5 ↔ r12), compile as closed walls flagged `sealedUntil:
   'S-251'` (a data flag the compiler honours; S-251 deletes it). Fields: `id:
   'buried_academy'`, `name: 'The Buried Academy'`, `kind: 'dungeon'`, `tile: 2`, `palette: 'academy'`, `music:
   'buried_academy'`, `light: { ambient: 0.65, torches: true, sun: false, tod: 0.5, windows: true }`, `parent:
   'saltglass'`, `door: 'drift_door'`, `needsOnEntry: ['lantern', 'sunglass']`, grid 102 m (41 × 43 tiles of 2 m + margin).
   Name card line 2: "beneath Academy Hill".
   Palette `academy`: stone `#efe3c8`, trim `#c9a46b`, accent `#8ec9d8`, brick `#b55d4c`, ivy `#5f8a4a`, dark tint
   `#3a2f22`. "Windows": sand-choked high windows are additive light cards on north walls (warm `#ffe2a8`, 0.35), and
   sand trickles fall beneath them (shared particle card, ≤ 1 draw per room).
2. **Registry**: `buried_academy` (`hidden: true` until S-254), bundle `saltglass`. **Portal** `saltglass:academy`
   ([systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table)): on the Drift Door, `how: 'use'`, `[E] Enter · The Buried
   Academy`, `needs: 'quest:mq-saltglass>=3'` (from S-254; until then debug only); `to: { realm: 'buried_academy', spawn:
   'entry' }`; the dungeon's r1 `X` (5,0) returns to spawn `drift` on the landing.
3. **Sand-flow tiles** (`<` `>` `{` `}`; `js/dungeon-mod.js`): while Pip's feet are on a flow tile he moves with it at
   **6 m/s** plus his own input **across** it (input along the flow adds nothing; against it is cancelled); a hop keeps
   the flow's velocity; leaving the flow restores normal control at once. Visual: the floor uses `tex_sandflow`
   UV-scrolled at 3 m/s in the flow direction, with `saltglass_card_sandflow` streaks (S-245) over it; sound `sfx
   sandFlow` loop within 6 m (the scrolling floor is its twin). A **block** pushed onto a flow is carried at 6 m/s until it
   leaves the flow onto the next non-flow tile; it stops there if the tile is free, else on the last flow tile. Pits at
   the end of a flow: the shipped pit rule (Pip is put back on his last safe tile, no damage).
4. **Nameplates and nameplate doors** (`y`, `N`): a nameplate is a dungeon item (`S.dungeons.buried_academy.plates[name]
   = 'held' | 'placed'`), picked up with `[E] Pick up · Nameplate` (the item-get toast names it: "A brass nameplate: 'A.
   Wick'"). An `N` door shows its expected name engraved faintly above an empty slot; `[E] Place · Nameplate` with the
   matching plate places it (the plate decal appears, the door swings open 90° in 0.8 s, open for good:
   `S.dungeons.buried_academy.open['r3:N']`). With the wrong plate or none: "The slot wants a different name." The
   three: **"A. Wick"** `r3:y1` (25,3) → N (30,4); **"Pelagia"** `r5:y1` (39,15) → N (30,10); **"F. Ashmoth"** `r7:y1`
   (15,12), appears on the reading desk when r7's wave is cleared → N (10,12) (sealed toward r8 in this step, opens in
   S-251).
5. **Rooms r1-r7** exactly as the bible's room-by-room notes: r1 sign `?`(3,3) `sg.d9.sign.r1` "LAMPLIGHTER ACADEMY. Walk
   in the halls. Sand: also walks in the halls."; r2 the south-flowing river (cols 14-16) into a pit, map chest `&`
   (19,7); r3 the one-way lanes and the island desk with A. Wick; r4 wave Sun Beetle ×2 (33,2), (37,2) + Dust Puff ×1
   (35,5), Chime Stone chest `%` (39,7), door (35,8) `clear`; r5 four alternating lanes, Prickle Pear ×2 at (33,9), (37,9),
   Pelagia at (39,15); r6 push block `r6:b1` (27,10) south ×2 onto the west-flowing lane → it rides to hold plate `r6:P1`
   (21,12) which holds door (20,14) open; reset lever `r6:r1` (28,15); gem chest `r6:c1` (21,9); r7 four bookcase pillars,
   Dust Puff ×3 at (13,10), (17,10), (16,14), then F. Ashmoth on the desk. Waves respawn only when Pip re-enters the
   dungeon from outside; at most 4 alive per room.
6. **Solver** (`js/realms/dungeon-compile.js`): flow tiles are forced moves; a lane crossing drifts ⌈1.36 × lanes⌉
   tiles; blocks ride flows; nameplates are keys bound to one door each. For this step the solver proves, with r8-r14
   sealed: entry → r2 `&` → r3 (A. Wick) → N (30,4) → r4 `%` → r5 (Pelagia) → N (30,10) → r6 `c1` (block rides to the
   plate) → r7 (F. Ashmoth), no soft-lock (r6's lever resets the block; every flow exit lands on safe floor or a pit
   with the respawn rule). Replay the path as a script test.
7. **Ask the Tree hints** (`sg.d9.hint.<n>.n` / `.a`), from the bible: 1 ["Sand rivers carry you. Start upstream.",
   "Step into the sand river at its very top and walk across. It carries you down to the far bank before the pit."]; 2
   ["Doors here want names, not keys.", "Each brass door has a nameplate slot. Find the student's nameplate in this wing
   and place it."]; 3 ["Sand carries blocks, too.", "In the Sand Pump Room, push the block south onto the sand lane. It
   rides all the way to the square plate."].
8. **Music** variant `buried_academy` = `{ from: 'depths', transpose: -2, voices: { lead: 'flute' }, add: { perc: [{
   pat: 't . t . t . t .', vol: 0.3 }] } }` ([art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)).
9. **Prints** `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/buried_academy.json` written here; S-251 refreshes it once.
10. **Completion**: d9's chests (`buried_academy:r6:c1` here; `r9:c2`, `r11:c3`, `r13:c4` in S-251, 40 gems each) roll up
    into Saltglass's "Dungeon chests" row; regenerate `stats.js`.

#### Assets
- **`tex_academy`** (generated `tile`, 1024 `_hd` ≤ 110 KB + 512 `_lo`, `realm: saltglass`): PREAMBLE + `tile` + "Pale
  sandstone school walls with faded blue tile trim and drifts of fine sand along the bottom. Palette: #efe3c8 #c9a46b
  #8ec9d8 #b55d4c." Process `make_seamless.py --blend 64`, export 1024/512 webp. Fallback `#efe3c8` with a `#8ec9d8` band.
- **`tex_sandflow`** (512² `tile`): "Rippled pale sand, ripples across one axis only, tileable along the flow. Palette:
  #e4d3a6 #c9a46b #efe3c8 #f6ecd2." `make_seamless.py --axis y`. Fallback `#e4d3a6` stripes.
- **`int_door_nameplate`** (code-built, 120 tris): a tall rounded-top wooden door with brass studs and an empty brass
  plate slot at eye height; the placed plate shows its name as a canvas-text decal on `decal_nameplate` (S-245). ART
  BRIEF from the bible: 2.0 × 2.8 m · wood `#7a5232`, brass `#c9a24a`, plate `#e9d8b0` · painted wood, brass sheen ·
  120 / 500 · swings open 90° in 0.8 s · image "A tall old school door with brass studs and an empty brass nameplate slot,
  sand drifted at its foot, warm light, cosy." · 3D "Stylised low-poly tall wooden school door with brass studs and a
  nameplate slot, hand-painted, game-ready, under 500 triangles, 2 × 2.8 m." Fallback: the kit door + a brass rectangle.
- **`icon_nameplate`** (128² cell): PREAMBLE + `icon` + "a small brass plate with engraved letters". Fallback a brass
  rectangle glyph.
- **`int_desk`** (code-built, 0.8 m school desk with an inkwell and a slate, 80 tris, instanced; fallback a small box).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line.
- `bun test`: the compiler builds `buried_academy`; the map test equals the bible map (r8-r14 tiles present; sealed
  doors are data, not map changes); the solver proves the r1-r7 path above and no soft-lock; the replay script passes.
- In-page `dungeons` suite case `buried_academy_a`: enter via `G.debug.realm('buried_academy', 'entry')`; cross r2's
  river from its top and land on the east bank; ride r3's lanes to the island and pick up A. Wick; place it; push r6's
  block and watch it ride to the plate; clear r7 and take F. Ashmoth; a dizzy Pip wakes at `@` with plates and doors
  kept.
- Realm suite: every room view ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles; generation ≤ 0.15 s.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test` (dungeon maps, solver, prints, stats). `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`. `node tests/inpage.mjs
  realm`. `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
r8-r14, the Gloves chest and the exam hall (S-251); the exam (S-252/S-253).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the dungeon is hidden and its portal needs a quest step that does not exist yet.

### S-251 · feat: the Buried Academy rooms 8-14: ivy between floors and the exam hall

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-250, S-001 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[dungeons#d9](../bible/dungeons.md#d9), [creatures#boss-scarabelle](../bible/creatures.md#boss-scarabelle),
[systems#items-tools](../bible/systems.md#items-tools), [dungeons#solver](../bible/dungeons.md#solver)

**Decision (the exam hall):** creatures.md owns arena geometry (its decision 15: "dungeons.md's boss rooms must match").
Its Great Hall has a 6 × 6 grid of 4 m tiles and **eight** wall receivers, two per wall at 1.5 m and 3.5 m; dungeons.md's
r14 draws four floor receivers and a 3 × 3 plate grid. This step **edits r14 in `docs/roadmap/bible/dungeons.md`** (the
repo copy of the bible) to the creatures geometry, in the same PR as the data, so the S-049 map test keeps comparing
like with like. The ivy "gallery" creatures mentions is decor (ivy on the hall's north wall, no second level).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. The second half of dungeon 9: the Ivy Gloves in r8 and the
climbs they open between floor heights, the Root Key, and the hall where the exam is sat.

#### What
1. **Unseal** r8-r14: delete S-250's `sealedUntil` flags. Rooms exactly per the bible notes:
   - **r8 The Gloves Hall**: treasure chest `$` (5,11) gives `gloves` (item-get "You got the Ivy Gloves! Walk into an ivy
     lattice to climb it.", `ability:climb`, the S-248a beat); the south half is a terrace (digit 9 = 2.7 m) behind a low
     wall with ivy `i` (3-5,13): climb it to door (5,16).
   - **r9 The Ivy Court**: arrive on the terrace, drop to the court; Sandsnoot ×2 at (3,21), (7,21); ivy (4,22), (4,23)
     to a corner ledge: gem chest `r9:c2` (1,23).
   - **r10 The Exam Corridor**: ivy (17,18) to a ledge: Root Key chest `K` (19,17); Sun Beetle ×2 at (13,21), (17,21).
   - **r11 The Old Gymnasium** (ceiling 7): ivy (22,19) to a ledge: gem chest `r11:c3` (22,17); Dust Puff ×1 (25,21),
     Prickle Pear ×2 (23,22), (27,22).
   - **r12 The Prefects' Room**: switch `r12:s1` (35,17) opens door (35,16) to r5 for good (shortcut); Prickle Pear ×2
     (33,19), (37,21).
   - **r13 The Bell-Weight Hall**: the empty bell-weight cradle (decor), gem chest `r13:c4` (38,30), boss door `B`
     (30,28) (Root Key).
   - **r14 The Exam Hall**: the new layout below; Scarabelle's lectern `M` (20,27); her desk and a 2 m hourglass prop
     at (23,26); rows of tiny desks (`int_desk`) along the west and east walls (decor, colliders); `X` (20,42) leads out
     to Academy Hill at a new Saltglass spawn **`examgarden`** (−10, 66), facing south, in the old exam garden behind the
     tower (add it to `REALMS.saltglass.spawns`; registry data, not prints). Until S-252 the hall is empty
     and the lectern shows `[E] Look · Lectern` `sg.d9.look.lectern` "A lectern with a register on it. Every name is
     ticked 'absent'. The ink is very old. The ticks are very new."
2. **Ivy tiles** `i` compile to `ivy` gate faces (S-248a) on the wall between the floor and the terrace or ledge; the
   compiler gives each face `y0` = lower floor height, `y1` = upper floor height, `top` = the upper floor tile.
3. **The new r14 rows** (columns 10-30 of rows 24-42; every other character of those rows is unchanged; the room keeps
   its box [10, 24, 21, 19]):

   ```
   row 25  #t...R.........R...t#
   row 26  #...................#
   row 27  #.........M.........#
   row 28  #...................B
   row 29  #...................#
   row 30  #...................#
   row 31  #R.................R#
   row 32  #...................#
   row 33  #...................#
   row 34  #...................#
   row 35  #...................#
   row 36  #R.................R#
   row 37  #...................#
   row 38  #...................#
   row 39  #...................#
   row 40  #...................#
   row 41  #t...R.........R...t#
   row 42  ##########X##########
   ```
   Room table row for r14 becomes: `t`(11,25) `R`(15,25) `R`(25,25) `t`(29,25) `M`(20,27) `R`(11,31) `R`(29,31)
   `R`(11,36) `R`(29,36) `t`(11,41) `R`(15,41) `R`(25,41) `t`(29,41). Each `R` is a wall-mounted receiver on the wall
   behind its tile; heights (creatures: one low and one high per wall): north (15,25) 1.5 m, (25,25) 3.5 m; east (29,31)
   1.5 m, (29,36) 3.5 m; south (25,41) 1.5 m, (15,41) 3.5 m; west (11,36) 1.5 m, (11,31) 3.5 m. Receivers start with
   their bronze **shutters closed** (S-252 opens them). The **exam grid** is the 24 × 24 m square over tiles cols 14-25,
   rows 28-39: column A = tiles 14-15 … F = 24-25, row 1 = tiles 28-29 … row 6 = 38-39; the start mat `@` is D6 (tiles
   20-21, 38-39). The grid is plain floor in the map; S-252/S-253 draw and animate it.
   Update the bible's d9 room-by-room note for r14 to say the same, and add one line to its decisions list: "r14 follows
   creatures.md#boss-scarabelle (8 wall receivers, a 6 × 6 grid of 4 m tiles); changed in S-251."
4. **Solver**: the full path from the bible's solver notes now proves end to end: … r7 (F. Ashmoth) → N (10,12) → r8 `$`
   (Gloves) → climb → r9 `c2` → r10 climb, `K` → r11 climb, `c3` → r12 `s1` → r13 `c4` → B → r14. Ivy edges need
   `climb`, which is obtained inside (no soft-lock: the r8 terrace is the only climb before the Gloves, and the chest is
   on the floor below it). Every chest (`&`, `%`, `K`, `$`, `c1`-`c4`) reachable.
5. **Prints** refreshed (same milestone). **Completion**: 4 gem chests (160 gems) for Saltglass's dungeon row.

#### Assets
- **`int_ivy`** reuses `tex_ivy_lattice` (S-248a) on wall quads. **`int_hourglass`** (code-built, 2 m, two lathe glass
  bulbs `#d8eef2` at 0.6 opacity in a brass frame `#c99a4a` with a sand cone `#c9a46b` that shrinks over 45 s and
  refills when turned; 200 tris). ART BRIEF: "a tall brass-framed hourglass on a stand, sand running" · 2 m · 200 / 800 ·
  3D "low-poly toon brass hourglass on a stand, 2 m, 800 tris". Fallback: two cones and four cylinders.
- Receivers reuse `gate_receiver` plus a bronze shutter (a hinged disc 0.9 m, `#9a6a3a`, 24 tris).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line.
- `bun test`: the map test equals the edited bible map; the solver proves the full path, every chest and no soft-lock;
  the r14 grid helper maps A1..F6 to the tile ranges above.
- In-page `dungeons` case `buried_academy_b`: from r7 with F. Ashmoth, open the r8 door, take the Gloves (ability set),
  climb to r9-r11's ledges and open their chests, take the Root Key, flip r12's switch (r5's south door opens), open the
  boss door and reach r14; the lectern Look line shows; a dizzy wakes at `@` with state kept.
- Realm suite: every room view ≤ 120 draw calls (r14 with 8 receivers and its desks included).
- `bun test tests/docs.test.js` passes (the bible edit keeps every anchor).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test`. `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`. `node tests/inpage.mjs realm`. `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
The exam papers (S-252, S-253); Saltglass's ivy lattices outside (S-249b, shipped).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR (code and the bible edit together); S-250's sealed half-dungeon remains.

<a id="s-251b"></a>

### S-251b · feat: The Buried Academy grows the Pump Gallery and the Head Prefect's Study: the sand changes its mind (depth rule R14)

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-251, S-060b, S-104b (shared annex pieces and the latch) · Size: M (200-500; most of it map data and entity tables) · Save: none · Flag: the dungeon realm stays `hidden: true` · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#d9-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d9-depth), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-common), [../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts](../bible/dungeons.md#depth-counts), [../bible/dungeons.md#rules](../bible/dungeons.md#rules), [../bible/dungeons.md#d9](../bible/dungeons.md#d9)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md R14, CANON §11.1 A2; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after
S-251 and before S-252, which now depends on it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Every story dungeon had about one simple beat per room and almost no room where two
mechanics meet, so The Buried Academy played in well under its budget and taught one idea without building on it. Rule R14
asks for ≥ 1.6 beats per counted room, ≥ 4 compound rooms, one mid-dungeon state flip that sends the player back
through rooms already solved, and a last room before the boss that uses everything. This step builds
The Buried Academy's annex, flip, latch and synthesis from the bible, so the dungeon meets the rule before its boss lands.

#### What
1. **Annex levels.** Copy the map blocks of [dungeons.md#d9-depth](../bible/dungeons.md#d9-depth) verbatim
   into the dungeon's data module (`js/realms/dungeons/<id>.js`, the file its room steps created): `buried_academy LA` (`at` [43,0]): r15 The Pump Gallery; `buried_academy LB` (`at` [43,10]): r16 The Head Prefect's Study; extent 54 × 43, realm grid 124 m. Room
   table rows, entity rows and the room-by-room conditions exactly as the bible gives them. Annex levels join the §5
   rooms only through stair warps.
2. **Stairs.** r15 `^`(1,7) ↔ r13 (35,26) on a small gallery reached by the new ivy; r16 `^`(1,7) ↔ r6 (27,14), sealed until the flip.
3. **Tile edits to the §5 map** (apply to the bible's map block and to the data in this PR; the map test compares
   against the edited block): r13 (34,26) and (36,26) `.` → `9`; r13 (35,26) `.` → `^`; r13 (35,27) `.` → `i`; r13 (32,30) `.` → `b`; r13 (33,30)-(36,30) `.` → `>`; r13 (37,30) `.` → `P`; r6 (27,14) `.` → `^`. Update the bible's room table, keys-and-chests ledger and solver notes to
   match, in the same PR.
4. **The flip.** **the Sand Pump** `r15:s1` (9,1), flag `ba.pump`, line `dg.ba.flip` "The pump groans. Through the walls, the sand changes its mind.". From then on: **Q = r5** and **P = r6**: every sand-flow tile in r5 and r6 reverses (`<` ↔ `>`, `{` ↔ `}`); in r5 the lane crossing now drifts east, so the nameplate door `N` (30,10) is reached from another column; in r6 the block rides the reversed row-12 flow off its plate and must be pushed round onto it again; the stair (27,14) unseals. Saved as `S.dungeons[<id>].state.flip = true` (v8
   container, no bump); the dungeon compiler applies the listed entity-state changes when it builds Q and P; the first
   entry into each after the flip plays the 0.4 s result cue (R7).
5. **The latch.** `B` (30,28) opens on `key:K && latch:r16:s1 && clear:r13`. Hitting the latch plays the toast `dg.latch` and a positional clunk at `B`.
6. **Synthesis in the room before the boss** (R14 item 4): r13: push the new block `r13:b1` (32,30) onto the new east flow; it rides onto the new hold plate (37,30), which opens the cage at the foot of the new ivy (35,27); climb to the gallery stair; the Sandsnoot wave is `clear` for `B`.
7. **Ask the Tree** gains two hint pairs for this dungeon (systems#hints): "The Bell-Weight Hall's sand will carry a block for you." / "Push the block onto the moving sand so it rides onto the plate, climb the ivy, take the stair, and pull the Sand Pump." · "The sand runs backwards now in two rooms." /
   "Cross the Lecture Hall lanes from the other side, then push the Sand Pump Room's block back onto its plate and take the stair." (text ids `dg.buried_academy.hint.4`, `.5`).
8. **Solver and depth test.** The flip is one more state bit in the lock-and-key graph (S-049); the solver proves the
   whole path (§5 rooms → annex → flip → Q and P re-solved → latch → `B`), every chest, and no soft-lock whether the
   player flips early or late. Extend `tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` (S-060b) with this dungeon's row: 16 rooms, 14 counted, ≥ 23 beats (planned 26), ≥ 4 compound rooms (planned 6); the test prints the per-room table
   for the PR body.
9. **Two waves from Act III** (dungeons.md R14; CANON §10.3): every combat room of this dungeon gets a second wave of mixed archetypes at the land's pattern tier (creatures.md#pattern-tiers), spawning 1.5 s after the first is calmed, at most 2 attackers at once; each room's `clear` waits for both. List the second waves in each room's entry in `dungeons.md`.

#### Assets
- The annex rooms reuse this dungeon's kit, textures and palette (no new generated images; the annex rooms are within
  the realm bundle already budgeted).
- **`int_sand_pump`** (code-built, ≤ 140 tris; the flip prop): a brass hand pump with a long handle on a sandstone base and a funnel hopper full of sand. ART BRIEF: silhouette as described;
  size 1.5 m; palette `#c99a4a` `#d9b080` `#8a5a30`; toon materials; 140 / 600 tris (code / painted GLB); pose static, it
  turns or swings once when used; image prompt "<style line> a brass hand pump with a long handle on a sandstone base and a funnel hopper full of sand, cosy storybook dungeon prop, three-quarter
  view"; 3D prompt "stylised low-poly a brass hand pump with a long handle on a sandstone base and a funnel hopper full of sand, hand-painted texture, game-ready, under 600
  triangles, 1.5 m". Manifest key `int_sand_pump` (commented-out GLB slot). Fallback: the code-built model.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (this dungeon's prints are refreshed in this PR, inside its own build milestone,
  before the land is unhidden), *Hidden until whole*, *One save slot* (no bump), *Budgets* (R11: every annex room view
  ≤ 120 draw calls and ≤ 150 k triangles), *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the annex blocks and the edited §5 block; data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves the full path, every chest, the flip in both orders, and no soft-lock; the depth test row passes
  the thresholds above.
- In page: hitting the flip changes Q and P exactly as listed; `B` stays shut until the latch (or d1's plate) and the
  Root Key; the hints show in Ask the Tree.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/dungeon-depth.test.js` and the
  dungeon's solver test.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `buried_academy_depth`: `G.debug.realm('<id>')`,
  play the intended path from the annex stair with `G.debug.dungeon.solve('<id>', { from: 'annex' })`, assert the flip's
  changes in Q and P, the latch, and `B`).
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (prints refreshed, budgets).

#### Out of scope
- The boss (S-252); any new chest, key or reward (the 48-chest ledger is fixed); the land overworld.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the flip makes a room unsolvable from the side the player returns by; the solver run in both flip orders is the
guard, and the bible's fallback (§5A.1) adds a block and plate. Rollback: revert the PR; the §5 rooms work alone
(stair warps to a missing annex show as sealed hatches) and the realm stays hidden.

<a id="s-252"></a>

### S-252 · feat: Tutor Scarabelle's exam, papers 1 and 2: dodging and aiming

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-251b, S-160, S-246 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[creatures#boss-scarabelle](../bible/creatures.md#boss-scarabelle), [creatures#encounter-kinds](../bible/creatures.md#encounter-kinds),
[creatures#boss-grammar](../bible/creatures.md#boss-grammar), [cast#tutor-scarabelle](../bible/cast.md#tutor-scarabelle),
[art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data)

**Decision (her look):** creatures.md's ART BRIEF (shell `#c9873a`, an hourglass-shaped body with a glass waist panel of
trickling sand, round spectacles, a small mortarboard with a gold tassel) is the model; cast.md adds her wooden pointer
`#8a5a30` and a ribbon-bound hand-bell at her collar `#c99a4a`, both kept (the bell is the one she rings to reset paper 3).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Act III's Wardens vary in kind (CANON §9.1): Pelagia is a
duel of wits, Fumarole a bout, Scarabelle an **exam**. Nobody fights a teacher; Pip sits her final exam, and a dizzy only
restarts the paper.

#### What
1. **Encounter kind `exam`** (`js/boss/exam.js`, a runner beside the move runner; shares intro, seal, dizzy-reset and
   handover with the other kinds): `def.papers = [paper1, paper2, paper3]`; the boss bar shows "Paper n/3"; the
   hourglass prop (S-251) runs during a paper; **a dizzy Pip wakes at the hall's `@`** (dungeon rule) and the current
   paper restarts from its beginning (earlier papers stay handed in, saved in `S.dungeons.buried_academy.exam = { paper,
   grade1 }`). Papers start on `[E] Talk · Tutor Scarabelle` at her lectern. Between papers she turns the hourglass (3 s)
   and says `sg.scarabelle.between.2` "Paper two. Begin when ready." / `.between.3` "Paper three. Begin when ready."
2. **Def** `js/boss/scarabelle.js`: `{ id: 'saltglass.scarabelle', key: 'buried_academy:scarabelle', name: 'Tutor
   Scarabelle', title: 'Examiner of the Lamplighter Academy', kind: 'exam', model: 'boss_scarabelle', height: 2.2, r: 0.9,
   keepOut: 1.4, room: 2.0, hp: null, arena: { anchor: 'buried_academy:M', grid: { origin: [14, 28], tile: 4, n: 6 },
   seal: 'bossdoor' }, after: …, plushie: …, rematch: …, bestiary: … }` (S-253 fills `after`, `plushie`, `rematch`).
   Intro (2.4 s): name card "Tutor Scarabelle" / "Examiner of the Lamplighter Academy"; her intro line
   `sg.scarabelle.intro` "Student. You are late, by approximately a century. Pencils down. ...You haven't got a pencil.
   Never mind. Begin when ready." She never moves off her lectern and is never hittable (`hittable: false`; a Swing on her
   plays "Pencils down.").
3. **Paper 1, Dodging** (45 s; her sand is the pattern; **at most two threats** live; every shape drawn ≥ 0.55 s ahead):
   - **0-15 s, rings**: a sand ring every 2.5 s from the lectern at t = 0.8, 3.3, 5.8, 8.3, 10.8, 13.3 (6 rings): the
     lectern glows and the ring's start line is drawn **0.8 s** before release; the ring band (0.6 m deep, 0.3 m high)
     expands at **7 m/s**; hop over it or roll through it.
   - **15-30 s, row stripes**: a whole tile row (4 m wide, 24 m long) is drawn **0.8 s**, then sand slides across it in
     0.6 s. Schedule (rows 1-6 north to south): 15.0 row 2 · 17.0 rows 4 + 6 · 19.5 row 1 · 21.5 rows 3 + 5 · 24.0 row 6 ·
     26.0 rows 2 + 4 · 28.0 row 5. Never two adjacent rows; two rows at once count as one threat each (≤ 2).
   - **30-45 s, falling sand**: every 2.0 s (t = 30, 32, … 44, 8 volleys) three circles r 1.2 m on tile centres: one on
     Pip's tile, two on the tiles from the fixed list `[B2, E5, C4, F1, A6, D3, E2, B5]` taken in order two at a time,
     skipping Pip's tile; tell **1.0 s**; a volley counts as one threat.
   - Each hit is 1 HP (half a heart); the hit count gives the cosmetic **grade**: A 0-1, B 2-4, C 5 or more (kept for the
     certificate). The paper ends at 45 s: `sg.scarabelle.p1.end` "Time! Pencils down."
4. **Paper 2, Aiming**: the receivers' bronze shutters open **two at a time** (a bell ding `sfx examDing` and the shutter
   flipping up are the twin), each staying open **6 s**; Pip lights an open receiver by holding the Sunglass beam on it
   for **0.5 s** (the exam's own fill time; the overworld receiver keeps 1.0 s). Order of pairs (ids from the S-251
   list: 1 N-low (15,25), 2 N-high (25,25), 3 E-low (29,31), 4 E-high (29,36), 5 S-low (25,41), 6 S-high (15,41), 7 W-low
   (11,36), 8 W-high (11,31)): [1, 5], [3, 7], [2, 6], [4, 8]; then any still unlit reopen in that order, two at a time.
   The next pair opens when both of the current pair are lit or closed. A **slow sand ring** rolls out from the lectern
   every 5 s (4 m/s, drawn 0.8 s ahead; one threat). All 8 lit ends **round 1**: `sg.scarabelle.p2.r2` "Now, from the
   gallery." **Round 2** (her escalation, creatures#boss-scarabelle): every shutter closes, then the same pair order
   reopens with each shutter open **4.5 s** and needing **0.8 s** of beam; the four high receivers (3.5 m: 2, 4, 6, 8) are
   hidden behind the hourglass's glow from the floor and can only be lit from the ivy gallery (S-251's lattices); the
   sand ring comes every **3.5 s**. All 8 lit again ends the paper: `sg.scarabelle.p2.end` "Eight of eight, twice. Time!
   Pencils down." First person: each open receiver also blinks a gold rim visible through the crosshair at any distance
   in the hall.
5. **Lines** (`js/text/en/sg.js`; tic "Pencils down." / "Time!"; calls Pip "student"): `sg.scarabelle.p1.start` "Paper
   one: dodging. A Lamplighter's feet must be quicker than the sand. Begin." · `sg.scarabelle.p2.start` "Paper two:
   aiming. Light where you are asked, when you are asked. Begin." · on a dizzy restart `sg.scarabelle.retry` "Again,
   student. From the top. There is no hurry; the sand will wait."
6. **Music** `exam` from [art-audio#track-data](../bible/art-audio.md#track-data) verbatim (`bpm: 116`, chords `'Am Dm E Am
   F Dm E7 E'`, the 8-bar melody, `lead: 'pluck'`, bass `'r . r . f . r .'`, comp `'. 3 . 3 . 3 . 3'` harp, perc tick-tock
   `'t . t . t . t .'` 0.5 and `'w . . . . . . .'` 0.3, sparkle false); it plays during papers; between papers the
   `buried_academy` variant.
7. **Debug**: `G.debug.exam.paper(n)` starts paper n; `G.debug.exam.state()` → `{ paper, t, hits, lit }`;
   `G.debug.exam.bot(on)` (dodges by reading drawn shapes; aims at open receivers with the real beam).

#### Assets
- **`boss_scarabelle`** (code-built, ≤ 4,500 tris): an upright beetle whose body pinches at the middle like an hourglass;
  the waist is a glass panel (alpha 0.6, `#d9eef2`) over an inner `sand` mesh (`#e8c98a`) the code scales with the paper
  timer; shell `#c9873a`; legs `#5a3a2a` (six capsules); round spectacles `#3b3540`; a small mortarboard `#2f2a3a` with a
  gold tassel `#f2c14e`; a wooden pointer `#8a5a30`; a ribbon-bound hand-bell `#c99a4a` at her collar. Poses: lectern tap,
  turn hourglass (she turns herself over and the sand runs the other way, 3 s), peer over spectacles, nod. ART BRIEF
  (batch 13, from creatures): 2.2 m · 4,500 / 12,000 tris · card *a tall dignified beetle with an hourglass-shaped body
  full of trickling sand, round spectacles and a tiny mortarboard with a gold tassel, standing at a lectern in a sandy old
  school hall.* · 3D *a cute chunky upright beetle teacher, body shaped like an hourglass with sand inside, round
  spectacles, tiny graduation cap with gold tassel, holding a small hourglass, hand-painted toon texture, low-poly under
  12k triangles.* (GLB: the sand as a separate node `sand`.) Fallback: two lathe glass bulbs, brass frame, sand cones,
  six capsule legs, `face()` with lenses.
- **`portrait_scarabelle`** (`portrait`, 3-frame strip neutral / peering / proud, sliced 512², ≤ 120 KB, `realm:
  saltglass`): the cast prompt ("A tall, dignified beetle schoolteacher whose back is a brass-framed hourglass with golden
  sand flowing inside, half-moon reading lenses, holding a wooden pointer, a small bell tied with ribbon at her collar,
  strict but secretly proud.") with "round spectacles and a tiny mortarboard with a gold tassel" in place of "half-moon
  reading lenses" (the decision above). Fallback: circle `#c9873a` with "S".
- **`card_boss_scarabelle`** (`card-beast`, 384²): the creatures card line + "Palette: #c9873a #d9eef2 #e8c98a #f2c14e."

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line.
- `tests/creatures.test.js`: `saltglass.scarabelle` is `kind: 'exam'`; paper 1's schedule has ≤ 2 live threats at every
  0.05 s step and every shape drawn ≥ 0.55 s before it can hurt (pure schedule test); the stripe schedule never draws two
  adjacent rows; paper 2's pair order, the 0.5 s fill in round 1 and the 4.5 s / 0.8 s round 2.
- In-page `bosses` case `scarabelle_12`: intro card; the bot passes paper 1 (grade recorded) and paper 2 (8 lit in each
  round, round 2 high receivers lit from the gallery); papers 1 + 2 take the careful bot 95-135 s; a forced
  dizzy in paper 2 (`G.debug.player.hp(0)`) wakes at `@` and restarts paper 2 only; Swinging at her does nothing but say
  "Pencils down.".
- Realm suite r14 view with all paper-1 shapes live ≤ 120 draw calls.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test`. `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses`. `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
Paper 3, the result, the stone, the plushie and the Resit (S-253).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; r14 returns to an empty hall with a lectern.

<a id="s-253"></a>

### S-253 · feat: the exam's puzzle paper, the result and the bell's stone

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-252, S-045 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: realm hidden · Bible:
[creatures#boss-scarabelle](../bible/creatures.md#boss-scarabelle), [creatures#hp-formula](../bible/creatures.md#hp-formula),
[creatures#intro-handover](../bible/creatures.md#intro-handover), [story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17),
[side-content#plushies](../bible/side-content.md#plushies)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. The exam's last question is the game's thesis in three
tiles: what does a Lamplighter do? **Light, give, climb.** Passing earns the bell's weight, the third Stair Stone.

#### What
1. **Paper 3, Puzzle** (`js/boss/exam.js` paper runner + pure grid module `js/content/exam-grids.js`, unit-tested): the
   floor turns to slick sand (the exam grid glows faintly); stone blocks rise 0.6 s on their tiles; once Pip steps in a
   direction (input snapped to the four grid directions) he **slides tile to tile at 6 m/s** until the next tile is a
   block or the wall; **answer tiles** stop him when he enters them: **L** (a lamp glyph), **H** (an open hand), **S**
   (a stair); the start mat `@` also stops him. Order: **L, then H, then S**. An answer tile entered out of order plays a
   soft "not yet" note (`sfx notYet`, with the tile flickering as its twin) and does nothing (it still stops him). Holding
   `{interact}` for 1 s anywhere rings her hand-bell and returns Pip to `@` (no cost). Pip cannot be hurt in paper 3.
   Grid (rows 1 north … 6 south; `#` block):

   ```
        A B C D E F
     1  . . # . S .
     2  . # . . . .
     3  L . . # . #
     4  . . . . . .
     5  # . H . # .
     6  . . . @ . .
   ```
   Solution (9 slides): N → D4, W → A4, N → A3 (L); E → C3, S → C5 (H); N → C2, E → F2, N → F1, W → E1 (S).
2. **Result**: after S, `sg.scarabelle.p3.end` "Light. Give. Climb. ...Pencils down." then the **handover** (creatures,
   verbatim, her portrait, no dizzy sit since nobody was fought: she sits on her lectern stool instead):
   `sg.scarabelle.ho.1` "Pencils down. ...Well, well. A full paper, and such tidy handwriting." · `.ho.2` "My students went
   home a hundred years ago. Someone must keep the school standing, in case one comes back. And look. One did." · `.ho.3`
   "The bell's stone is yours, Lamplighter. You have passed. Mind the bell on your way out." Then scene
   **`stone-give-saltglass`** (25 s, [story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17)): `say scarabelle sg.stone.1` "Final exam:
   passed. Grade: acceptable. That is the highest grade I give." · `say scarabelle sg.stone.2` "The bell's weight is yours,
   as is the custom. Ring for the Academy now and then. So it remembers it's a school." · `emit item stone_saltglass`
   ("You got a Stair Stone! One of the three keystones of the old Starstair. Set it at the Summit Crystal Cairn.") · `flag
   set:stoneHeld:saltglass`, `set:boss:buried_academy:scarabelle` (the S-045 calmed write) · `give:certificate_exam`
   (key item "Exam Certificate", desc reads the grade: "Final exam, Lamplighter Academy. Grade: {grade}. Signed, S.";
   shown in the Items tab). The ch15-17 isle change (the bell ringing over the Flats) is S-254's.
3. **After** (def `after`): by day (05:00-20:00) at the exam garden (−10, 70) on Academy Hill (atlas spot
   `saltglass.scarabelle`), at night at her lectern in r14 (creatures); both are her "after spots" and Talk works at
   either. Talk line `sg.scarabelle.after.1` "Attendance: one. Excellent." (rotation with `.after.2` "Sit up straight,
   student. Lamplighters slouch only when carrying lamps.").
4. **Resit** (the `exam` kind's rematch, through Talk, never Wake): a Talk option "Resit the final exam?" (yes / not now)
   from the first time Pip leaves the realm after passing; loads r14 if she is at the garden. Paper 1 runs **60 s** with
   tier-2 patterns: rings come as **double rings** (a second ring 1.0 s behind the first, each drawn 0.8 s ahead), stripes
   draw **0.7 s** (≥ 0.55 kept), circles as paper 1; paper 2 needs **12 lights, three receivers at a time**, in the triples
   [1, 5, 3], [7, 2, 6], [4, 8, 1], [5, 3, 7] (a receiver already lit dims when its shutter opens again and must be lit
   again; a closed-unlit triple member reopens after the four triples); paper 3 uses the resit grid:

   ```
        A B C D E F
     1  . # . . S .
     2  . . . # . .
     3  # . L . . .
     4  . . . . . #
     5  . . # H . .
     6  # . . @ . #
   ```
   Solution (10 slides): N → D5, N → D3, W → C3 (L); W → B3, S → B6, E → D6, N → D5 (H); S → D6, E → E6, N → E1 (S).
   The first pass pays **60 gems once** (`S.rematches['buried_academy:scarabelle']`) and counts in the Rematches row
   ("Scarabelle's Exam Retake", systems). The retake line `sg.scarabelle.resit.pass` "Passed again. You are making the
   rest of the class look bad. There is no rest of the class."
5. **Plushie** (`plush_scarabelle`, side-content plushies rules): Miso stocks it once Scarabelle is calmed and the shop is
   bought out; **100 gems**; desc "Comes with a tiny hourglass. Do turn it."; got "A Tutor Scarabelle plushie! She's
   marking my stitches~". Icon `plush_scarabelle` (128² cell).
6. **Bestiary**: `card_boss_scarabelle`, Hoot "Tutor Scarabelle failed me once. I was not enrolled." / Clover "Lamp, hand,
   stair. Light, give, climb!" (shown after the first pass).
7. **Debug**: `G.debug.exam.grid(id)` returns the grid; `G.debug.exam.solve()` plays the solution slides.

#### Assets
- Answer tiles (code-built 4 m floor decals on canvas: a lamp glyph, an open hand, a stair; gold `#f2c14e` on sand
  `#e8c98a`), stone blocks (4 × 4 × 1.2 m sandstone boxes `#d9b080`, instanced), the hand-bell (in her model). No
  generated images beyond `plush_scarabelle`: PREAMBLE + `icon` + "a small plush toy of a beetle teacher with an hourglass
  body and a tiny mortarboard". Fallback: canvas circle.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line.
- `tests/exam-grids.test.js`: a breadth-first solver over both grids finds the stated solutions (9 and 10 slides) as the
  shortest; from every reachable (tile, progress) state every remaining answer tile can still be reached (no soft-lock);
  out-of-order answer tiles do not advance progress.
- `tests/creatures.test.js`: Scarabelle's `rematch.gems` 60; plushie price 100; the resit paper-1 schedule keeps ≤ 2
  threats and ≥ 0.55 s leads with double rings.
- **Balance** (creatures#balance-targets, the act-closer/Act III window): the careful exam bot (S-252's `G.debug.exam.bot`
  plus a paper-3 bot that searches the grid by sight, not `solve()`) completes papers 1-3 including the two 3 s hourglass
  turns in **120-180 s** in ≥ 90 % of 20 seeded runs, losing ≤ 3 hearts; times per paper in the PR body.
- In-page `bosses` case `scarabelle_3`: the bot solves paper 3 (`G.debug.exam.solve()`); the handover and stone scene play;
  `stone_saltglass` held, `stoneHeld:saltglass` and `boss:buried_academy:scarabelle` set; the certificate shows the grade;
  holding interact returns Pip to `@`; leaving and Talking offers the Resit; a passed Resit pays 60 gems once.
- Economy test includes the plushie sink and the 60-gem retake source.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test`. `node tests/inpage.mjs bosses`. `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
The main quest wrapper, the bell's ringing over the Flats, setting the stone and unhiding (S-254). ss-43/ss-44 (M29).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the exam stops after paper 2 with no reward. The realm is hidden.

<a id="s-254"></a>

### S-254 · feat: Saltglass's main chain: the Academy bell rings and a stone is set

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-253, S-246, S-212, S-030 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: unhides `saltglass` and
`buried_academy` · Bible: [story#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17), [story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats),
[atlas-far#saltglass-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-states), [systems#quests](../bible/systems.md#quests),
[atlas-home#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky), [atlas-home#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots),
[systems#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness), [art-audio#saltglass](../bible/art-audio.md#saltglass)

**Decision (the people steps on the ground):** story.md's step 2 ("Pip digs out the Academy's roof hatch") and step 3
("Pip rings the tower's hand-bell") are placed as: a classroom roof poking out of the dunes at the hill's west foot
(`w:roofHatch` (−115, 35), in the Glass Dunes region) whose hatch is under soft sand; inside it, on a hook, hangs the
Academy's brass hand-bell with a tag "Ring at the tower for admission"; Pip rings it at the Drift Door on the tower
(Academy Hill), and the voice from below opens the door. The dungeon's own flag `academyDepths` (dungeons.md) is set at
the same moment as step 3 completes, so both names hold.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Chapter 15, 16 or 17 (by arrival order): the Flats' main
quest, the bell ringing for the first time in a century, the third flight of the Starstair and, when it is the third
stone, memories 10-12. The realm is whole: it is unhidden here.

#### What
1. **Quest** `mq-saltglass` (`js/content/quests/saltglass.js`, n 17, kind `main`, title `sg.q.title` "The Buried
   Academy", owner `sable`, start `flag:ch14`, chapter banner by arrival order, story.md decision 10):

   | # | id | Journal (`sg.q.s<n>`) | Done | On | Hint nudge / answer (`sg.q.h<n>.n` / `.a`) |
   |---|---|---|---|---|---|
   | 0 | `camp` | Find the Lamplighter Academy. | `flag:sg.sableAsked` | `TALK_END` | "Someone at the camp knows what's under the hill." / "Talk to Sable at her finds table in Sablecamp." |
   | 1 | `dunes` | Dig down to the Academy. | `item:hand_bell` | `ITEM_GET` | "A roof pokes out of the dunes west of the hill." / "Dig the soft sand off the roof hatch at the west foot of Academy Hill with {tool}, then open it." |
   | 2 | `enrol` | Enrol. | `flag:academyDepths` | `SCENE_END` | "Schools have bells for a reason." / "Climb the sand drift to the door high on the bell tower and ring the hand-bell there." |
   | 3 | `depths` | Find the exam hall. | `flag:sg.examHall` (set by `dungeon-mod` on entering r14) | `REALM` | "Sand rivers, brass doors, and somewhere an exam." / "Go in through the Drift Door and work down to the Exam Hall behind the big brass door." |
   | 4 | `exam` | Pass the final exam. | `flag:stoneHeld:saltglass` | `SCENE_END` | "Pencils down when she says." / "Talk to Tutor Scarabelle to begin each paper. Dodge, aim, then light, give, climb." |
   | 5 | `place` | Set the Stair Stone at the Cairn. | `flag:stone:saltglass>=1` | `STONE` | "Home, to the Cairn." / "Carry the Stair Stone to the Summit Crystal Cairn on Frostcap and place it." |

   Compass short labels (`.short`, ≤ 32 characters): "Sable · Sablecamp", "Roof hatch · the dunes", "The Drift Door",
   "The Exam Hall", "Tutor Scarabelle", "Set the Stair Stone · the Cairn" (the last is story.md's wording).
   Reward on done: `set:ch-saltglass` (a story flag for chapter specs; the stone flags come from S-212's scene).
2. **Step 0, Sable** (dialogue priority 80, `when: 'quest:mq-saltglass.active'` at step 0): `sg.sable.q.1` "Colleague!
   Do you know what's under this hill? POTTERY, Pip. POTTERY. A whole school of it!" · `sg.sable.q.2` "The Lamplighter
   Academy. Buried to its bell tower. There's a classroom roof poking out of the dunes, west of the hill. ...Or a hat. A
   very big hat." · `sg.sable.q.3` "You've got digging mitts. Dig it out gently. Gently! Brush, don't dig. ...Dig a
   little." → `set:sg.sableAsked`, `set:met:sable`.
3. **Step 1, the roof hatch** (`placeStates`, `new RNG(40277 * 109 + 21)`, the main-chain pass): a classroom-roof stamp
   (S-244's `saltglass_ruin_roof` model, a fixed extra item) at `w:roofHatch` (−115, 35) nearest walkable within 8 m,
   with a 1.2 × 1.2 m wooden hatch on its slope, covered by a `soft_earth` mound r 1.6 m (`[T] Dig · Soft earth`, Paws,
   1.0 s). Dug: the hatch shows `[E] Open · Hatch`: an FP-safe close shot (6 s, scene `sg-hatch`, replayable): the
   hatch creaks open on a sand-filled attic, a brass hand-bell hangs on a hook with a paper tag; `[E] Pick up ·
   Hand-bell` → key item `hand_bell` (kind `delivery`, "The Academy's hand-bell", desc "A brass hand-bell with a tag:
   'Ring at the tower for admission.'"), item-get beat.
4. **Step 2, enrolment**: at the Drift Door landing, while `item:hand_bell`, `[E] Ring · Hand-bell` plays scene
   `sg-enrol` (12 s, replayable without its flags): the bell rings twice (`sfx handBell`, the bell swings in the
   viewmodel's left hand); a pause; from below, speaker "A voice below" (frame `default`, no portrait):
   `sg.voice.1` "Enrolment is OPEN." · `sg.voice.2` "Wipe your feet. Mind the sand. Classes begin when the student
   arrives. That is you. Hurry along." · the door unlatches (0.8 s) · effects `take:hand_bell` (the bell then hangs on a
   hook beside the door as a prop, so nothing seems lost), `set:academyDepths`. The S-250 portal `saltglass:academy` now
   shows (`quest:mq-saltglass>=3`).
5. **The isle change within 10 s of the stone** (`stoneHeld:saltglass` set by S-253's scene, in r14): the Academy bell
   rings **three times** (`sfx academyBell`, a deep D3 bell, 4 s apart; in the dungeon fine sand trickles from the ceiling
   as its twin). Saltglass's healed states (`placeStates` records keyed by flag, [atlas-far#saltglass-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-states)):
   - the bell in the belfry swings on its rings; from now on it rings **once at 12:00 and once at dusk (19:48)** every day
     (the sound carries across the whole Flats at −12 dB beyond 200 m; the swing is the twin);
   - the 16 glass sculptures hum a chord at 12:00 (each glints, `saltglass_card_glassglint`);
   - ruin windows glow at night (`#ffcf7a`, the `nightGlow` input; 1 draw for all);
   - **Sablecamp's kites go up** (story.md): three diamond kites (code-built, 1.2 m, `#b55d4c` / `#efe3c8` / `#8ec9d8`,
     string lines to the tents, swaying on a 7 s sine; instanced, 1 draw);
   - music `saltglass` goes to layer 1 (the bell `lead2` joins) and `saltglass_dunes`' `dim` lifts (the dunes sing);
     art-audio's dim rule.
   - The compass changes to "Set the Stair Stone · the Cairn".
6. **The stone at the Cairn**: S-212's framework does the rest with no new code: `[E] Place · Stair Stone` sets
   `stone:saltglass` = next order and clears `stoneHeld:saltglass`; the scene `stone-set` raises flight n, lights crown
   lantern n, plays Wenna's line for `stones` = n (table in [story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats)) and opens
   that triple's memory knots (10-12 when n = 3). Data to add: horizon card **f**'s night window lights when
   `stone:saltglass` is set ([atlas-home#sky](../bible/atlas-home.md#sky): "e, f … lit again when its Stair Stone is
   set"; window point (0.62, 0.40) on the card); the chart's Saltglass node shows a small bell glyph.
7. **Plaza visitor** (`home:spot-visit-saltglass`, bearing 215° from the plaza centre, r 12-15 m, from
   [atlas-home#spots](../bible/atlas-home.md#spots)): from `stone:saltglass`, Sable visits 08:00-20:00 every fourth day
   (day index mod 4 = 2; the other visitors keep their days). Lines (priority 20): `home.sable.visit.1` "Your village has
   a TREE in it. A real one! Not a pot! ...I'm going to label it anyway." · `.visit.2` "Mayor Ribbit asked if I'd dig up
   his garden for history. I said yes. He meant no. We're both confused."
8. **Unhide** `REALMS.saltglass.hidden = false` and `REALMS.buried_academy.hidden = false`; the buoy's fog lock lifts (the
   Glassway's `glassway:buoy_saltglass` portal works with `flag:ch14`); after the first visit the chart's Saltglass button
   lights (atlas-far decision 3: first reached through the buoy).
9. **Sable's dialogue after**: priority 20 `when: 'quest:mq-saltglass>=3'`: `sg.sable.enrol.1` "A voice? From UNDER the
   hill? ...A teacher, I bet. Teachers always know when you're late. Mind your manners down there, colleague." ("ghost" is
   a code-only word, [charter#gentle-words](../bible/charter.md#gentle-words).)
10. **Chapter test** `ch-saltglass`: `tests/fixtures/saves/ch-saltglass-start.json` = the `ch-kettle` end fixture (the
    canonical chain order is bell → kettle → saltglass; S-256 covers the other five) plus nothing else; spec
    `tests/fixtures/chapters/ch-saltglass.json`:

    ```json
    { "id": "ch-saltglass", "start": "saves/ch-saltglass-start.json", "realm": "glassway", "spawn": "reef",
      "steps": [
        { "do": "realm", "id": "saltglass", "spawn": "jetty" },
        { "do": "talk", "npc": "sable" }, { "do": "scene.skip" },
        { "do": "warpTo", "anchor": "w:roofHatch" }, { "do": "tool", "anchor": "w:roofHatch" },
        { "do": "use", "id": "saltglass:hatch" }, { "do": "scene.skip" }, { "do": "use", "id": "saltglass:handbell" },
        { "do": "warpTo", "anchor": "w:driftDoor" }, { "do": "use", "id": "saltglass:ring" }, { "do": "scene.skip" },
        { "do": "use", "id": "saltglass:academy" },
        { "do": "realm", "id": "buried_academy", "spawn": "r14" }, { "do": "give", "item": "gloves" },
        { "do": "killBoss", "key": "buried_academy:scarabelle" }, { "do": "scene.skip" },
        { "do": "realm", "id": "home", "spawn": "cairn" }, { "do": "use", "id": "home:cairn" }, { "do": "scene.skip" } ],
      "expect": { "flags": { "academyDepths": true, "stone:saltglass": 3, "ch-saltglass": true },
                  "items": { "gloves": 1, "stone_saltglass": 0, "certificate_exam": 1 },
                  "quests": { "mq-saltglass": "done" }, "lanterns": 7 } }
    ```
    (`killBoss` on an exam boss runs its pass path: the three papers handed in with grade A, the handover and the stone
    scene. The dungeon itself is covered by S-250/S-251's in-page cases, so the spec jumps to r14 and gives the Gloves.) The
    CI matrix gains the job `chapter:ch-saltglass`. The chain unit test checks `ch-kettle` end → `ch-saltglass` start.
11. **Dungeon spawn** `r14` (the hall's entry tile inside the boss door) for the spec, and `cairn` on home if S-212 named
    it differently (use the shipped name).

#### Assets
- Kites (code-built, 1.2 m diamond + tail ribbon, 30 tris each). The hatch (wooden 1.2 m square, 24 tris). The hand-bell
  (code-built 0.2 m brass bell with a wooden handle, 80 tris; held in the viewmodel's left hand for the ring; ART BRIEF:
  "a small brass school hand-bell with a turned wooden handle and a paper tag" · 0.2 m · `#c99a4a`, `#8a5a30` · 80 / 300
  · 3D "low-poly toon brass hand-bell with wooden handle, 300 tris"). Icon `icon_hand_bell` (128² cell): PREAMBLE + `icon`
  + "a small brass school hand-bell with a wooden handle". Fallback canvas bell.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all. Home prints unchanged (the card f window and the visitor spot are data on existing items).
- `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-saltglass` passes; the chain test passes; `mq-saltglass` has a two-part hint per step.
- The isle change happens within 10 s of `stoneHeld:saltglass` (in-page assert from the flag's set time to the first
  bell `sfx` event and the kite meshes visible on the next Saltglass load); noon and dusk bells ring on
  `G.debug.tod(12)` / `G.debug.tod(19.8)`; ruin windows glow at night.
- With the third stone: knots 10-12 open (S-212a data; their scripts are S-254b, so until it lands they say "This one
  is still tying itself."); with the second: 7-9 (asserted here once for Saltglass as the 3rd, all orders in S-256). Horizon card f's window lights at night.
- `saltglass` and `buried_academy` are unhidden; the buoy works; the chart button lights after the first visit.
- Content lint green.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test` (quests, content, chain). `node tests/chapter.mjs ch-saltglass`. `node tests/inpage.mjs side` is
  not needed; `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (Saltglass healed views within budget, kites included). `bun run
  test:smoke`; `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I untouched).

#### Out of scope
Hollows, Dash, Rook (S-255); memories 10-12's content (S-254b: this step only opens knots 10-12 through S-212a's
count data); all six stone orders (S-256); Saltglass side stories (M29).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR: the realm is hidden again and the buoy locks. A save that set `stone:saltglass` keeps its flag (nothing
earned is taken back); the stone counts still read correctly because S-212 owns them.
