[story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats)

**Decision (staging):** M24-M26's R9 extends to memories 10-12: **lore.md** owns spawn, hour, cast, staging and lines;
atlas-home#remembered-isle owns only the realm's skin and the v1 pass. atlas-home's m10-m12 rows (Shores beach 02:00,
"everyone indoors", lights at 292.5°/67.5°/202.5°, 05:00) are rewritten in this PR to match lore (three one-line
edits, as S-190a did for m01). Lore's three bearings are used: ENE (67.5°), SSW (202.5°) and S (180°).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. "The Storm" group: the third Stair Stone (whichever site
it is) opens knots 10-12, which show the Storm putting out the Ladder of Lights, the three keystones falling to the
three places Pip has just been, and a young turtle writing the bottle by lamplight (the set-up for twist T7). Chapter
18 waits for `stones==3 & memories==12` (story#stone-beats), so without this step the knots say "This one is still
tying itself." for ever, `mq-remember` never finishes and S-256's "every memory once" fails. Play: about 0.4 h,
counted inside M28's +5 h (the header's "overworld finds on the way" share drops from 1.0 to 0.6 h).

#### What
1. **Data** in `js/content/memories.js`, group 4 (opens `stones>=3`, knot glow sea `#7fd3d0`), three entries `{ id,
   knot, group: 4, title, when, tod, spawn, face, cast[], dressing[], scenes[] }` in S-190a's schema, every line,
   label, staging note, Look line and return line copied **verbatim** from lore.md. Text ids `rm.mem10.<n>`,
   `rm.mem11.<n>`, `rm.mem12.<n>` in `js/text/en/remembered.js`; Look lines `rm.mem1N.look.<who>.<n>`.

   | Memory | Card | When / `tod` | Spawn · face | Cast (look id at near(x, z), each nudged to `ok()`) |
   |---|---|---|---|---|
   | 10 | "Memory 10 · The Storm" | night 13, 23:30 (`tod` 0.9792) | near (0, −8) under the Great Tree's north roots, facing east toward the Cairn | `keeper_tallow` (3.0, −9.5), `young_wick` (2.2, −8.8), `lamplighter_1` (4.5, −7.0), `lamplighter_2` (5.0, −10.0), `lamplighter_3` (3.8, −11.5), `young_shellby` (2.5, −6.8), `villager_duck` (5.8, −8.2), `villager_hedgehog` (6.2, −6.5); all crouched (pose `sit`) facing the trunk |
   | 11 | "Memory 11 · Three Stones Fall" | night 13, 23:50 (`tod` 0.9931) | near (10, 112) by the lighthouse door, facing north-east toward the Cairn | `young_pinch` in the doorway near (8.5, 116) holding a lamp, wearing `prop_paper_hat` (S-212b); `lamplighter_4` (13.0, 109.0), `lamplighter_5` (14.5, 111.0); `young_pelagia` in the water nearest (2, 121) with depth ≥ 0.3 m, in the lee of the lighthouse rocks |
   | 12 | "Memory 12 · To Whoever Finds This" | night 14, 21:00, calm and clear (`tod` 0.875) | near (6, 115), the lighthouse's sea-side rocks, facing the LOCKED bottle spot (7.2, 119.7) | `young_shellby` sitting on a rock at near (6.6, 117.2) with a small lamp (`remembered_lamp` held) and a slate on his knees |

   Labels exactly as lore#memory-rules: "Keeper Tallow", "Young Wick", "A Lamplighter", "A villager", "Young Pinch",
   "Young Pelagia", "Wenna", and "A young turtle" (computed to "Young Shellby" after `trueEnding`, S-189's rule).
   **No line says "Shellby".**
2. **Scripts** (one scene each, ≤ 90 s of script time unskipped; the steps are lore's table rows in order):
   - **Memory 10.** Lines 1-3 (A Lamplighter, Young Wick, Keeper Tallow). Then `memory.lamps {set: 'stair', from: 66,
     to: 1, on: false, rate: 3}` (22 s) with `sfx.lightOut` per lamp (the runner's 8-per-second cap holds), while on
     `sky:skyIsle` a single small light stays lit (a `nightGlow` sprite, `#ffe9a8`, r 0.6 m on the sky layer); `shot
     look at sky:skyIsle` for 3 s. Line 4 (A young turtle), line 5 (Keeper Tallow), line 6 (Wenna, from the Tree's face
     decal above the spawn: a `shot look at w:greatTree.face` first); at line 6 the rain density eases from 1.0 to 0.5
     over 3 s.
   - **Memory 11.** `shot wide` toward the Cairn: the stair's flights sway (±0.3 m, 0.5 Hz) with `sfx.stoneHum`; three
     pale lights (`#f4f0dc`, the S-190b star-streak card reversed so it falls outward) leave the stair one after
     another 1.5 s apart and arc over the sea toward bearings 67.5°, 202.5° and 180°, each 6 s, each dropping past the
     horizon with `sfx.starFall` at the far end; a `shot look` follows lights 2 and 3 (they pass overhead) for 2 s each.
     Then the new event `memory.stair {drift: true, dur: 6}` lifts every `stair` step up 25-40 m and apart (each step
     its own direction from its index) while fading to 0 opacity, after which the set is hidden. `shot two at
     lamplighter_4` → line 1 (A Lamplighter); `shot two at young_pinch` → line 2 (Young Pinch), and the paper hat
     blows off along a 2 s arc out to sea (`move` step on the prop, then hidden); line 3 (A Lamplighter). Then
     `sfx.seaBell` from bearing 180° at −6 dB (the far bell, no visual). `shot look at young_pelagia` → line 4 (Young
     Pelagia); `shot two at young_pinch` → line 5 (Young Pinch).
   - **Memory 12.** `shot two` over the young turtle's shoulder → line 1; the `letter` frame then shows bottle 12's
     text in `hand_shellby` (the LOCKED shipped text from lore#bottle-12, word by word at 40 characters a second;
     about 4 s; the player may tap to finish it, never to skip the scene). Lines 2 and 3; at line 3 he lifts the pen
     and stops (no signature drawn). Line 4: he rolls the letter into a bottle (`kit_bottle` appears in his hand) and
     corks it. He throws it (a 1.0 s arc, `sfx.plop`) into the rock pool 1 m away and fishes it out (0.8 s). Line 5:
     he tucks it into the rocks at (7.2, 119.7) (the bottle becomes a static `kit_bottle` there, no prompt) and pats
     it twice. Line 6, then he looks up at `sky:skyIsle` (`shot look` 2 s) and walks home up the beach with his lamp
     along a fixed path to near (−4, 98) (`go: true`, 1.0 m/s); at the halfway point the hat slips over his eyes once
     (a 0.4 s tilt of the hat node and back).
3. **Dressing** (per memory, on top of the `remembered` skin and the v1 pass, which S-188 shipped):
   - **Rain** (memories 10 and 11 only): new module `js/fx/rain.js`, an instanced streak quad (0.02 × 0.5 m, `#e9d8b0`
     at 35 % opacity, falling 9 m/s, 12° wind slant) in a 25 m cylinder round the camera, ≤ 400 instances, **one draw**;
     `density` 1.0 in memory 10 until its line 6, 0.4 in memory 11. Ambient bed: `sfx.rainSoft` loop (procedural
     filtered noise, −14 dB) plus a far rumble `sfx.rumbleFar` (low-passed noise swell, 2 s, every 12-20 s, no
     flash, memory 10 only). Foliage sway × 2.5 through the shipped wind uniform if the foliage shader has one; if it
     has none, skip the sway (note it in the PR).
   - **Memory 10:** stair lamps 1-66 lit, 67-88 dark (`memory.lamps` at scene start with `rate: 0`); the waymarker lamp
     posts (the path line) dark; cottage windows dark.
   - **Memory 11:** the stair set present with every lamp dark; rain easing; Pip's lamp light from `young_pinch`'s
     lamp only (`nightGlow`).
   - **Memory 12:** the `stair` set hidden (it fell); clear sky with stars; water `glass` setting (wave amplitude ×
     0.2); the single tiny light on `sky:skyIsle`; the shipped bottle keepsake at (7.2, 119.7) is **not** drawn in this
     memory until line 5 places the scene's own `kit_bottle` (the `remembered` realm already hides chests and
     keepsakes; assert it).
   - Runner addition: the memory event `memory.stair {drift, dur}` and a dressing key `stair: 'lit66' | 'dark' |
     'gone'` in `js/content/memories.js`'s schema, with a unit test; nothing else in S-190a's runner changes.
4. **Look lines** (`[E] Look · <label>`, memory frame, in order, then the last repeats), verbatim: 10 A Lamplighter "My
   whiskers are soaked. Do moths have whiskers? Mine are soaked." / A young turtle "I'm not scared. My shell's
   waterproof." · 11 Young Pinch "I'll get a proper hat. A proper one. With an anchor." / A Lamplighter "Three stones,
   three ways. We'll never find them all." · 12 none.
5. **Return lines** (`rd.wenna.mem10`-`mem12`, once each, `wenna` frame, voiced from the Taproot, S-190a's rule): 10 "I
   held them all under my roots that night and told them it was only wind. It was only wind. There was just a great
   deal of it." · 11 "My roots felt each one land. One in the shallows. One in something hot. One under sand. I've been
   holding those three places ever since." · 12 "That bottle waited in those rocks for a hundred years. I felt it
   there, every tide. Waiting for somebody." After all three are watched, the one-time Taproot line
   (story#stone-beats "Coming back from a memory triple") `rd.wenna.trip4` "...Who was he writing to, I wonder."
6. **Flags and quest:** `mem:10`, `mem:11`, `mem:12` are set at each memory's first step (S-190a's rule, R52); replays
   set nothing. When all three hold, `mq-remember`'s step `m101112` (S-212a) completes and the quest is done (`memories`
   = 12), so `stones>=3 & memories>=12` holds for chapter 18 (S-264).
7. **Music** (group 4, "music box under soft rain, then alone"): memories 10 and 11 play the memory-box track with
   the rain bed under it (`{ from: 'memorybox', bpm: −8, add: { pad: { voice: 'pad', vol: 0.3 } } }`, the
   `storm` variant in art-audio's grammar, if S-190 did not register one); memory 12 plays the music box alone
   (`{ from: 'memorybox', drop: ['comp', 'pad'] }`, variant `alone`).
8. **Journal replays:** memories 10-12 appear in Field Notes → Lore → Memories once watched (S-190b's list; `Replay ✿`
   works, sets no flags). Memory 12's replay shows the same `letter` frame text.
9. **Spoilers:** memory 12 carries only the T7 hint (lore#spoiler-ladder: at `stones>=3`, never the name). A content
   test asserts `hand_shellby` is used only by bottle 12, `rm.mem12.letter` and the Nook card id, and that no
   `rm.mem1*` text contains "Shellby".
10. **Debug:** `G.debug.memory.play(n)` (if S-190a named it differently, use the shipped name) plays memory n from the
    Taproot without flags for screenshots.
11. **Echoes for memories 10-12** (S-190b's runtime; lore#memory-echoes), verbatim from the **Echoes** tables under
    lore#mem-10, #mem-11 and #mem-12 (text ids `rm.mem1N.echo.<k>`), placed with `near(x, z, 0, 4)` when free roam
    starts: 10: A Lamplighter near (10, 110) shielding a lamp "Hold, little lamp. Hold. ...Good. Good lamp."; A villager
    near (13.3, −13) closing a shutter; Young Frostbun near (110, −21) in the wind · 11: A Lamplighter near (57, 2) with
    a chart "One went that way. And one that way. And one... that way. I'll draw it on the chart before I forget.";
    Young Grumble near (−3.2, −110) peeking from behind a stump; A villager near (3, 14) by the pedestal "Her roots are
    humming. I can feel it in my feet. Feet don't lie." · 12: A Lamplighter near (57, 2) packing a crate; Keeper Tallow
    near (95, −47) sitting on a crate "Night fourteen. No light coming down. ...Sit with me a while, Wick. We'll watch
    anyway."; Young Pelagia near islet 1 (−40, 139) floating on her back. In memories 10 and 11 the echoes stand in the
    rain (the rain cylinder follows the camera, so no extra draws). Memory 12 keeps its spawn at the rocks in this step;
    S-254d moves it to the plaza for vignette 4.

#### Assets
- `fx_rain_streak`: code-built (one quad, canvas gradient texture 8 × 64 px), no generated image. ART BRIEF: none needed.
- The memory 12 slate (code-built `rbox` 0.25 × 0.18 × 0.02 m `#4a4a4a` with a wood rim `#8a5a30`, ≤ 30 tris) and the
  held lamp reuse `remembered_lamp`; the bottle reuses `kit_bottle`; Young Pinch's hat reuses `prop_paper_hat` (S-212b).
- Sounds `sfx.rainSoft`, `sfx.rumbleFar`, `sfx.plop`: procedural WebAudio recipes in `js/audio.js`'s sfx table (no files).
- No generated images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all (home prints unaffected: memories run in the `remembered` variant; the v1 snapshot is unchanged
  because the stair's drift and hiding are runtime only).
- `bun test`: lint over `rm.mem10-12.*` (≤ 220 characters, allowed labels, no "Shellby"); every scene ≤ 90 s of script
  time; the FP speaker rule over the scene data (within 8 m and the view cone, or a shot first); the `hand_shellby`
  placement test; the `memory.stair` unit test; group 4 opens exactly at `stones>=3`.
- In-page (`stones` suite, case `memories-101112`): from `ch-kettle2-end.json`, `G.debug.stone.give('saltglass')` +
  `G.debug.stone.set('saltglass')` (stones = 3); knots 10-12 glow sea `#7fd3d0`; touch knots 10, 11, 12 (any order):
  each shows its card, plays, skips cleanly to the same end state, and returns with its return line; `mem:10`-`mem:12`
  set; the triple line plays once; `mq-remember` done; `memories` = 12; the rain mesh is one draw and absent in memory
  12; the `stair` set is hidden after memory 11's drift and in memory 12; replaying memory 12 from the Lore tab sets
  nothing.
- No knot says "This one is still tying itself." with three stones set.
- Perf: memory 10's view (rain + 66 lit stair lamps + the cast) stays within 350 draw calls and 1.3 M tris.
- Each of memories 10-12 shows its three echoes after free roam starts; Look shows the lore line verbatim and sets
  `seen:echo:mNN.k`; the echo lint (no "Shellby", ≤ 220 characters) and placement test pass for the nine rows.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test` (`tests/memories.test.js`, `tests/content.test.js`).
- `node tests/inpage.mjs stones` (case `memories-101112`); `node tests/inpage.mjs variant` (the `remembered` realm's
  memory cases still pass); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (the `remembered` row); `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- Shellby's quieter lines after memory 12 and the Act III reactions (S-260). Chapter 18 (S-264, S-272). All six stone
  orders (S-256). The Nook card in `hand_shellby` (its own step).

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: the 22 s lamp-out run and three overhead lights must read in first person; the `shot look` steps cover them and
every event has a sound twin. Rollback: revert; knots 10-12 fall back to "This one is still tying itself." and the
`mem:*` flags stay valid.

<a id="s-254c"></a>

### S-254c · feat: The Lamp Walk joins the main chain: Saltglass's night walk to the Academy door

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-254, S-249b, S-091b · Size: M (about 300 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[../bible/story.md#set-pieces](../bible/story.md#set-pieces), [../bible/story.md#ch15-17](../bible/story.md#ch15-17),
[../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass)

Added in review (see [Index changes](#index-changes)); the id S-254c is CANON §11.1's (S-254b is memories 10-12).
Filed **after** S-254 (Saltglass is open), so it adds a `before` sub-objective to `mq-saltglass`'s step `enrol` and
never shifts a saved step index.

#### Why

Part of **Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Saltglass's main-path set piece (15-25 minutes, no fail): the
Academy only admits students after dark, so before Pip may ring the hand-bell he walks the students' old road at dusk
with the Lantern and relights its twelve practice lamps. S-249b built the lamps as an optional find; this promotes
them onto the main path and gives each lamp a nameplate, so the walk tells the Academy's story as it goes.

#### What

1. **Data** (`mq-saltglass`, step `enrol`): `before: [{ id: 'lampwalk', until: 'flag:gate:saltglass:lampwalk', minutes:
   18, text: 'sg.q.lampwalk', short: 'sg.q.lampwalk.short', target: 'w:saltglass.lamp-next', hint:
   ['sg.q.lampwalk.n', 'sg.q.lampwalk.a'] }]`. Journal "Light the Lamp Walk at dusk ({n}/12)." · compass "Light the
   Lamp Walk" · nudge "The Academy took students after dark. The old road has lamps." · answer "From the jetty, kindle
   each of the twelve lamp posts with the Lantern after 17:24; play *Dusk* on the Starsong if it is day."
2. **Admission** (talk to the voice at the Drift Door, or using the hand-bell there, while the walk is unlit): "Admission
   is after dark, and only by the Lamp Walk. Lamps first, then the bell. Those are the rules. I did not write them. I
   did enforce them for ninety-one years." The bell does nothing until the walk is lit.
3. **Nameplates** (S-249b's 12 posts, `saltglass:lamp-1..12`): each kindled lamp lights a small brass nameplate at its
   foot, Look-able, one student per post in order (`sg.lamp.plate.<n>`): 1 "BRAMBLE · first year · 'Trim, then
   flame.'" 2 "OSIER · first year · lost three wicks in one week" 3 "TALLOW · first year · top of the knot test" 4 "SORREL
   · second year · 'Light the far one first.'" 5 "WICKERLEY · second year · always late, always lit" 6 "PERCH · second
   year · fell asleep on watch, once" 7 "BRINDLE · third year · kept the Organ in tune" 8 "MALLOW · third year · wrote
   home every week" 9 "KESTREL · third year · first to the top of the Ladder" 10 "FEN · fourth year · carried the
   chart" 11 "TAPER · fourth year · copied the old song" 12 "(unnamed) · keeper · 'The last lamp is the door.'"
   Each ≤ 220 characters; no living cast member's name is reused except Keeper Taper (lore.md), here as a student.
4. **The walk** keeps S-249b's rules (Lantern lit, at night; 12-bit `saltglass.lamps`, cleared at dawn until solved).
   Two Sandsnoots (S-247's dune species) rise from the sand beside lamps 5 and 9 the first time each is kindled
   (combat, shipped numbers; dizzy keeps the lit lamps for the night). On the twelfth: S-249b's chest c3 rises at the
   gate as before, and scene `sg-lampwalk` (14 s): the camera follows the line of lamps from the jetty to the Academy
   gate; the bell tower's window lights; the voice: "Lamps are lit. You may ring." `after:
   ['set:gate:saltglass:lampwalk']`.
5. **Saves**: a save past `enrol` (`flag:academyDepths`) loads with `gate:saltglass:lampwalk` set (the lamps light
   nightly), nothing taken back; a save that already solved c3 skips the `before`.
6. **Chapter test** `ch-saltglass` (S-254's spec): before the bell, `G.debug.tod(19)` and `G.debug.sg.lightWalk()`
   (kindles the twelve with the real interaction); expect `gate:saltglass:lampwalk`. **Debug:**
   `G.debug.sg.lightWalk()`.

#### Assets

- Nameplates: canvas text on a code-built brass plate (`box` 0.3 × 0.12 m, `#c99a4a`), instanced. No generated art.

#### Acceptance criteria

- On a Saltglass save at step `enrol`, the bell does nothing until the twelve lamps are lit at night; each lamp lights
  its nameplate; the twelfth plays `sg-lampwalk` and the bell then works; c3's reward is unchanged.
- Saves past `enrol` load with the walk lit.
- Invariants: *Frozen realms*, *Tone* (no fail; nothing missable), *Budgets per view* (the Lamp Walk view with 12
  lights: the four nearest real lights, the rest emissive cards), *Machine*.

#### Verification plan

```sh
bun test tests/quests.test.js tests/content.test.js && bun test
free -h && node tests/inpage.mjs saltglass
node tests/chapter.mjs ch-saltglass
```

#### Out of scope

The memories (S-254b, S-254d); the Buried Academy (S-250, S-251); Saltglass's Hollows and Dash (S-255).

#### Risk & rollback

A quest field entry and text on existing props. Rollback: revert; the Lamp Walk is an optional find again.

<a id="s-254d"></a>

### S-254d · feat: vignette 4 The Last Lamp: carry the young turtle's lamp-glow to the lighthouse in memory 12

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-254b, S-190c, S-212e · Size: M (about 320 lines) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[lore#vignette-last-lamp](../bible/lore.md#vignette-last-lamp), [lore#memory-vignettes](../bible/lore.md#memory-vignettes),
[lore#mem-12](../bible/lore.md#mem-12), [lore#memory-hours](../bible/lore.md#memory-hours),
[charter#first-person](../bible/charter.md#first-person), [art-audio#kinds](../bible/art-audio.md#kinds)

Added by this file (see [Index changes](#index-changes)): the playable part of memory group 4.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. "The Storm" group's vignette, and the last: on the calm
night after the Storm, Pip carries a small lamp-glow from the plaza down to the lighthouse rocks, following a little
turtle's prints, while the storm flickers away far out at sea; at the rocks the glow becomes the young turtle's lamp
and the bottle scene (the T7 hint) plays. It turns Act III's best scene from watched into walked (about 5 of the
memories' 90 minutes, lore#memory-hours). Pip changes nothing: he carries the light along the way it went.

#### What
1. **`carry` vignette mode** (`js/vignettes.js`, `registerVignetteMode('carry', ...)`): a pickup `{ label, at, sprite,
   light: { r: 6, color } }` with `[E] Carry · <label>`. While carried: the glow sits in the viewmodel's left hand
   (third person: both hands, the `hold` pose the Starseed carry uses if it exists; else the left-hand attach node);
   Pip walks and runs at normal speed; dash, attack and the full jump are off (jump becomes a 0.3 m hop); one point
   light (r 6 m, intensity 1.2, no shadows) follows the glow. `[E] Set down`, or entering water deeper than 0.5 m, leaves
   the glow floating 0.8 m above the ground where Pip stood (pickup again with the same prompt); the compass target
   switches to the glow while it is down. The mode ends when Pip, carrying, is within `end.r` of `end`.
2. **Memory 12's vignette** (lore#vignette-last-lamp, verbatim): memory 12's `spawn` becomes near (0, 13) facing south;
   `sceneAt` near (6, 115) facing the bottle spot (7.2, 119.7) (S-254b's spawn); `vignette: { id: 'lastLamp', mode:
   'carry', when: 'before', pickup: { label: "A lamp's glow", at: near(0, 9.6) (the pedestal step), sprite:
   'fx_lamp_glow', light: { r: 6, color: '#ffd27a' } }, end: { x: 6, z: 115, r: 3 } }`. Dressing for the vignette: every
   Ladder lamp dark (`stair` hidden as S-254b's memory 12 already does, `landing` and `path` dark); cottage windows lit;
   the young turtle hidden until the end.
3. **Prints:** `decal_prints_turtle` (S-212e's texture, pair cell) along a fixed route plaza → south path → the Shores →
   the beach → (6, 115), one pair per 1.5 m, each nudged to walkable; a decal is drawn only within 6 m of the carried
   glow (a distance fade in the decal shader, one draw); about 80 pairs.
4. **The storm going away:** a cloud-bank billboard band (3 cards, bearings 150°, 180°, 210°, 260 m out at y 10-40,
   texture `sky_storm_bank`, drifting south 0.5 m/s, always inside the 300 m far plane and fog-tinted) and a flicker
   driver: one pulse every 3-6 s (its own stream `new RNG(7151 * 241 + 179)`, a variant-realm constant this step adds to
   atlas-home's pass table as row `v1s`), 0.4 s up and 0.8 s down, raising only the band's emissive toward
   `#cfd8ff` with a luminance change ≤ 0.12 and never two pulses within 2 s (a photosensitivity guard enforced in the
   driver and unit-tested); sound twin `sfx.thunderFar` (a low-passed noise swell at −30 dB) 2 s after each pulse.
5. **Overheard lines** (`rm.mem12.v.1-2`, memory frame, once each, positional): leaving the plaza near (0, 25), A
   villager (from cottage 3's lit window, (−15.9, 11.5)) "Is that the little one, out with his lamp at this hour?
   ...Leave him be. He's got somewhere to be." · at the top of the beach near (5, 95), A Lamplighter (from the
   lighthouse door) "Storm's going out to sea. Good riddance. Take your wind with you."
6. **Arrival:** carrying, within 3 m of (6, 115): the glow floats from Pip's hand to the young turtle's lamp (1 s arc)
   as he fades in on his rock with the slate, and S-254b's scene starts (its first line has a `shot two` over his
   shoulder, so the first-person rule holds). Without the glow the rocks stay empty and the compass points to it.
7. **Skip:** Pip placed at (6, 115) facing the bottle spot, the glow consumed, the scene starts. Replays run the
   vignette again.
8. **Debug:** `G.debug.memory.vignette()` reports `{ carried: bool, distToEnd }`; `G.debug.storm.pulses()` returns the
   last 20 pulse times (for the guard test).

#### Assets
- `fx_lamp_glow` (lore assets table): **generate** a 128² alpha image, prompt: art-audio preamble + `fx` suffix + "a
  small warm lamp-glow, a soft round orb of golden light with a bright cream centre, soft painterly storybook style,
  transparent background. Palette: #fff1c4 #ffd27a #ffe9a8."; process with `tools/export_sprites.py` to webp ≤ 8 KB;
  manifest key `fx_lamp_glow` (`fx`, `remembered`, lazy). Fallback: canvas radial gradient `#fff1c4` → `#ffd27a` →
  transparent.
- `sky_storm_bank`: **generate** a 1024 × 256 alpha image, prompt: preamble + `sky` suffix + "a long low bank of dark
  soft storm clouds far away over a calm night sea, seen from a beach, edges feathered to transparent, soft painterly
  storybook style. Palette: #3b4058 #565d7a #8c93b0 #cfd8ff."; make the left and right edges tile with
  `tools/make_seamless.py --axis x`; export to webp ≤ 60 KB; key `sky_storm_bank` (`sky`, `remembered`, lazy).
  Fallback: a canvas band, vertical gradient `#565d7a` → transparent with three soft lumps. Original art.
- `sfx.thunderFar` is a procedural recipe in `js/audio.js` (no file).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Unit (`tests/vignettes.test.js`): `carry` disables dash, attack and the full jump while carrying and restores them
  after; set-down and deep water leave the glow in place and never remove it; the mode ends only while carrying inside
  `end.r`. The flicker driver over 10 min of simulated time never fires two pulses within 2 s, never changes
  luminance by more than 0.12, and its intervals all lie in 3-6 s.
- In-page (`stones` suite, case `last-lamp`): from a save with `stones = 3`, knot 12 enters at (0, 13) facing south; the
  glow is on the pedestal step with `[E] Carry · A lamp's glow`; carried, prints show only within 6 m; the bot warps
  along the route in 3 m steps; both overheard lines fire once each; setting the glow down at the beach moves the
  compass to it, picking it up again works; within 3 m of (6, 115) the young turtle fades in and memory 12's scene
  (S-254b) plays; arriving without the glow plays nothing.
- Skip places Pip at (6, 115) and starts the scene; `G.debug.memory.skip()` from memory start ends memory 12 with the
  mote at (6, 115); `mem:12` was set at the vignette's start; a replay runs the vignette again and sets nothing.
- Budget: the beach view toward the lighthouse with the storm band, prints and the glow light stays ≤ 350 draw calls
  and ≤ 1.3 M tris (band 1 draw, prints 1, glow sprite 1).
- Invariants: all lines (no save change; the storm RNG constant is new and recorded; home prints unaffected).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `bun test tests/vignettes.test.js tests/memories.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `node tests/inpage.mjs stones` (new case `last-lamp`); `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` (the `remembered` row);
  `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
- The scene itself (S-254b); Shellby's quieter lines (S-260); any other use of the carry mode.

#### Risk & rollback
Risk: a point light in first person over a long walk costs more than budgeted on software rendering; if the budget
check fails, replace the point light with the glow sprite plus a ground decal disc (no light) and record it. Risk:
the flicker reads as lightning; the guard keeps it a slow soft pulse with no white. Rollback: revert; memory 12 spawns
at the rocks again and plays its scene as S-254b shipped it.

<a id="s-255"></a>

### S-255 · feat: Saltglass's three Hollows, its Dash course and Rook's race finale

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-249b, S-073, S-234 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[dungeons#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [dungeons#h21](../bible/dungeons.md#h21), [dungeons#h22](../bible/dungeons.md#h22),
[dungeons#h23](../bible/dungeons.md#h23), [side-content#act-dash](../bible/side-content.md#act-dash),
[side-content#ss-49](../bible/side-content.md#ss-49), [cast#rook](../bible/cast.md#rook)

**Decisions:**
- **Dash course 9** follows side-content (the activity's owner): "Sablecamp → across the Glass Dunes → Academy Hill, 14
  rings, about 460 m, no tool". atlas-far's route to the Glass Organ is not used.
- **Rook's part 5** follows side-content: start at Sablecamp, finish on the bell tower's belfry deck, Ivy Gloves on the
  best line, the feather as the reward (cast's bottle cap and its "part 4 in Saltglass" order are superseded by
  side-content and INDEX). Rook's Saltglass start spot is `saltglass.rook_start` at Sablecamp (106, −140) (cast's
  spot id; the atlas's dunes coordinate is not used). The bible's "about 520 m" is approximate: the route is whatever the
  careful bot runs (about 250 m plus the 22 m climb), and Rook's time is that × 1.15.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Three puzzle caves (two heart petals and a gem bag), the
ninth Dash course and the end of Rook's five races.

#### What
1. **Hollows** (`js/realms/hollows/hollow_saltglass_1.js` … `_3.js`; `kind: 'hollow'`, grid 48 m; the shared Hollow
   rules: no map, Chime Stone or keys; prize on a root pedestal; dizzy wakes at `@`; puzzle state in
   `S.dungeons['hollow_saltglass_<n>']` until solved). Maps copied **verbatim** from
   [dungeons#h21](../bible/dungeons.md#h21) - [#h23](../bible/dungeons.md#h23):
   - **`hollow_saltglass_1` The Dune Hollow** (door (−240, −120), a dune cave): three west-flowing sand lanes (rows 5-7)
     and a pit (row 4) with a one-tile bridge (2,4); crossing north drifts Pip about 4 tiles west, so starting at column
     6 lands him on the bridge; heart petal `+` (5,1) → `collect.petal['hollow_saltglass_1:p1']`. Hint: "The sand carries
     you west as you cross." / "Start crossing the sand lanes from the sixth column from the left. You will land on the
     narrow bridge."
   - **`hollow_saltglass_2` The Trellis Hollow** (door (−95, 30), a ruined classroom roof): ivy (1-3,5) climbs onto the
     west terrace (digit 9); glide east over the pit (cols 4-6) onto the east ledge (digit 6): **60-gem bag** `*` (9,1).
     Needs `climb` and `glide`. Hint: "Climb, then glide." / "Climb the ivy to the high terrace, then glide east to the
     lower ledge."
   - **`hollow_saltglass_3` The Sun-Dial Hollow** (door (200, 150), the salt-block hut; the door opens with its receiver,
     S-249b): receiver (1,4) behind pillar (3,4) over a pit tile (1,5); mirror (1,6) starts `/`, two hits make it `\`; a
     beam west along row 6 turns north into the receiver (latch) and the bridge `=` (5,2)-(5,3) rises; heart petal `+`
     (5,1) → `hollow_saltglass_3:p1`. Hint: "A sunflower round a corner." / "Bonk the mirror twice, then shine your beam
     west along its row."
   - **Portals** `saltglass:hollow1..3` (`[E] Enter · Spiral door`, [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table)),
     on the S-249a door props: 1 and 2 always; 3 `needs: 'flag:gate:saltglass:hut'` (the receiver). Solved doors glow gold
     and the chart shows a gold spiral. Toast on entering: the Hollow's name. Save `collect.hollow['hollow_saltglass_<n>']` (the realm id; S-071's `normalize` drops any other key).
2. **Dash course 9** (`js/content/activities/dash.js` row, S-112 framework; `placeSideMarks` stream `40277 * 107 + 19`):
   start post at Sablecamp (108, −146) (≥ 4 m from bench b1); 14 rings (2.4 m hoops at 1.2 m above ground, nearest
   walkable within 4 m, in order): (95, −128), (78, −112), (62, −95), (45, −76), (20, −80), (−5, −90), (−40, −95),
   (−80, −85), (−120, −70), (−135, −35), (−130, 0), (−110, 30), (−95, 60), (−82, 78); finish post (−72, 86) by Dormitory
   Row (Academy Hill). About 450 m over the Lamp Walk and the north dune crests. Par `P` = the careful bot's time
   recorded by this step's in-page test; medals gold ≤ 1.10 P, silver ≤ 1.30 P, bronze ≤ 1.60 P; first bronze 15 gems,
   silver 20, gold a ribbon on Clover's windowsill (Saltglass's colour `#c9a46b`). `collect.dash['dash:saltglass']` (tenths of a second, 1-36000).
3. **Rook part 5** (`ss-49` step `race5`; S-113 framework): Rook's spot `saltglass.rook_start` (106, −140), 08:00-18:00,
   from `realm:saltglass.visited` and part 4 won. `[E] Talk · Rook` → 3-2-1 → Pip runs to the finish post on the belfry
   deck (−20, 40, y 36) (the S-249b tower lattice is the best line); Rook flies a fixed spline (a ghost, no collisions)
   from the start over the Lamp Walk to the belfry; his time = the careful bot's time × 1.15. Lines (cast tic "Called
   it!"; calls Pip "rival"): start `sg.rook.race5.start` "Last race, rival. The top of that bell tower. Called it! ...I'm
   calling it now. That counts." · win (side-content, verbatim) `sg.rook.race5.win` "Five for five. ...You're the best
   treasure-hunter on the Wide Blue. Don't tell anyone I said. Here." → gift `nook_gift_feather` (item-get "Rook's
   feather! He polished it first."), 8 gems, `set:ss49.done` · lose (cycling, verbatim) "Ha! Rematch? Go on. I'll even
   pretend to stretch." / "Finders keepers! ...Again?". World mark: a small flag in the belfry "Pip was here first. —R.
   (grudgingly)" (`[E] Read · Flag`). ss-49's journal done text (Hoot, verbatim): "Five races. Rook gave Pip a feather.
   Rook says it is 'not a big deal'. Rook polished it first."
4. **Completion / economy**: Saltglass Hollows 3, petals +2, gem bag 60, Dash 9 tiers (35), Rook 8; regenerate
   `stats.js`; economy test.

#### Assets
- Dash ring and posts: the S-112 kit with Saltglass's ribbon colour. Rook's belfry flag: the S-113 flag prop. No
  generated images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line.
- `bun test`: each Hollow compiles; the solver proves the prize reachable with the stated tools and no soft-lock; the
  map test equals the bible maps; dash ring order and spacing (consecutive rings 18-45 m apart).
- In-page `dungeons` cases for the three Hollows (enter, solve via debug drives of the real mechanics, prize once, door
  glows); `activities` case `dash-saltglass` (the bot finishes; par recorded in the PR; medals pay once); `side` case
  `ss-49-5` (the bot climbs the tower and beats Rook's ghost; the feather and `ss49.done`; a deliberate slow run loses
  with a rematch offered).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test`. `node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons`, then `activities`, then `side` (one at a time). `bun run
  test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
The other Dash courses and Rook parts (shipped); the Driftholm Hollow and Dash (S-267).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the three Hollow doors say "The spiral is cold." again.

<a id="s-255b"></a>

### S-255b · feat: Saltglass Flats's Hollows get their second stage, and The Sun-Dial Hollow becomes a Deep Hollow

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-255, S-090b · Size: M (200-500; mostly map data) · Save: none · Flag: the land's Hollow realms keep the land's hidden flag · Bible: [../bible/dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows), [../bible/dungeons.md#h23](../bible/dungeons.md#h23), [../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs](../bible/lore.md#lamp-logs), [../bible/story.md#motes-traces](../bible/story.md#motes-traces)

Added by the playtime review (dungeons.md §7 and R15, CANON §11.1 A3; see [Index changes](#index-changes)). Filed after S-255.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Every Hollow was a one-step room that played in 2-3 minutes and all 24 shared one
shape. Now every Hollow has two stages with the prize behind the second, and one Hollow per land is a three-room
**Deep Hollow** (15-20 min) that ends in a reading nook with a Lamp Log and a note from the Lamp Thief, so the
optional bucket of CANON §11 is real play and each land has one hidden place worth remembering.

#### What
1. **The reliquary** (if S-093b has not added it yet, this step does; later steps reuse it): entity `reliquary: { when:
   [...] }` on a Hollow's prize tile; a code-built cage of four curved roots `#6f5a44` with gold buds `#ffe9a8`, 1.0 m,
   ≤ 120 tris; it opens in 1.0 s with `chime_up` when its condition holds; its state lives in
   `S.dungeons['<hollow>'].stage` until the prize is taken, then is dropped (the shipped Hollow rule). The solver proves
   stage 1, then stage 2, then the prize.
2. **Stage 2 for H21 The Dune Hollow, H22 The Trellis Hollow** (copy each Hollow's **Stage 2** line from [dungeons.md#hollows](../bible/dungeons.md#hollows)):
   - **H21 The Dune Hollow** (`hollow_saltglass_1`): a receiver lock: hold the Sunglass beam on receiver `R` (9,1) for 1.0 s; condition `lit:[R]`.
   - **H22 The Trellis Hollow** (`hollow_saltglass_2`): receiver `R` on the east ledge at (8,2), lit by the Sunglass from the ledge after the glide; condition `lit:[R]`.
3. **The Deep Hollow H23 The Sun-Dial Hollow** (`hollow_saltglass_3`): replace its one-room data with the 31 × 11 composite map
   from [dungeons.md#h23](../bible/dungeons.md#h23) (copy, never paraphrase; realm grid 78 m). Room 1 is the old
   puzzle (stage 1) with a door (10,1) where its prize stood. **Room 2 The Dial Court**: push block (12,2) east onto the flow (13-16,2): it rides onto hold plate (17,2); light receiver (19,1) with the Sunglass; the bridge (19,4)-(19,6) rises while both hold; exit door (20,9). **Room 3 The Reading
   Nook**: the prize on its pedestal (25,1); **Lamp Log 30** on a page pinned to stone (23,3) (`Read · Lamp Log`;
   it moves here from its atlas slot in Saltglass Flats: edit that land's finds data so the slot is skipped, update the
   atlas row and run `node tools/realm-stats.mjs`; land and game totals unchanged); a scrap jam-jar lamp (`prop_scrap_lamp`
   from S-090b, or its fallback) holding a folded note (27,3), `[E] Read · Note`: "The sun goes round this dial and never misses a mark. I have missed so many. — M" (text id
   `lore.hollow.saltglass`, ≤ 220 characters; it never names him, the charter spoiler rule; reading it adds the Lore
   entry "A note in a Hollow" and sets `trace:hollow:saltglass`; if story.md's traces define this land's note, its
   text wins); and its own exit `X` (25,10) back to the spiral door.
4. **Hints** (Ask the Tree): one pair per stage-2 line (nudge = the condition's object, plain = the line), and for the
   Deep Hollow's room 2: "Let the sand carry the block." / "Push the block onto the moving sand so it rides onto the plate, then shine the Sunglass at the receiver.".
5. `STATS` is regenerated (`node tools/realm-stats.mjs`): same Hollow ids, same prizes, the log's new position.

#### Assets
- `int_reliquary` (code-built as above; ART BRIEF: a small cage of four curved roots with gold buds over a stone
  pedestal; 1.0 m; palette `#6f5a44` `#8a5a3c` `#ffe9a8`; toon bark; 120 / 500 tris; it opens like a flower; image prompt
  "<style line> a small cage of four curved tree roots with tiny gold buds over a mossy stone pedestal, cosy"; 3D prompt
  "stylised low-poly root cage over a stone pedestal, hand-painted, under 500 triangles, 1.0 m"). Fallback: four bent
  cylinders. The Deep Hollow reuses the land's Hollow kit and the scrap lamp; no new images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- `tests/dungeon-maps.test.js` passes with the edited Hollow blocks (16 `+` and 8 `*` still counted across the Hollows);
  data equals the bible blocks.
- The solver proves each Hollow's two stages and the Deep Hollow's three rooms in order, with no soft-lock, using only
  the tools listed in the Hollow table.
- In page: a bot solves each stage-2 Hollow and the Deep Hollow; reading the note sets `trace:hollow:saltglass`; the
  Lamp Log counts once; prizes and `collect.hollow` keys are unchanged.
- Content lint (≤ 220 characters, no spoiler name). Invariants: keep *Frozen realms* (Hollow realms only; the land's
  overworld prints are untouched except the log slot, refreshed in this PR before the land is unhidden or, if it is
  already unhidden, by a finds-data change that the prints test lists), *One save slot*, *Tone*, *Assets*, *Machine*.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`; `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- bun test tests/dungeon-maps.test.js tests/stats.test.js tests/content.test.js`.
- `agentctl heavy --job <job> -- node tests/inpage.mjs dungeons` (new case `hollows_saltglass`).

#### Out of scope
- New prizes or Hollows; the land's other finds.

#### Risk & rollback
Data changes inside Hollow realms plus one log slot. Revert the PR: the one-stage Hollows and the log's atlas slot come
back.

<a id="s-256"></a>

### S-256 · test: all six Stair Stone orders open every memory once

Milestone M28 · Depends on: S-254, S-254b, S-232b, S-030 · Size: S (<200) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[systems#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness), [story#stone-beats](../bible/story.md#stone-beats),
[lore#memories](../bible/lore.md#memories), [systems#crown-lanterns](../bible/systems.md#crown-lanterns)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M28 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**. Act III's promise is "any order": whatever order the three
stones are set in, the memories come by count (4-6, then 7-9 with twist T5, then 10-12), the stair grows one flight per
stone, and nothing plays twice or is skipped.

#### What
1. **Runner** `tests/stone-orders.mjs` (uses `tests/chapter.mjs`'s browser harness and step verbs; one browser at a
   time): for each of the six permutations of `[bell, kettle, saltglass]`, start from `tests/fixtures/saves/ch14-end.json`
   (Act II done, memories 1-3 watched, `ch14` set, every Act II tool and the Paws, no Act III tool), then for each site in
   order: `realm` into the site's Warden room, `give` that dungeon's tool (Starsong / Sling / Gloves), `killBoss` the
   Warden (`sunken_bell:pelagia`, `kettles_belly:fumarole`, `buried_academy:scarabelle`; the real handover and
   `stone-give-<site>` scenes), `realm home cairn`, `use home:cairn`, `scene.skip`; then `realm rootdeep taproot` and
   touch each newly opened knot (`use rootdeep:knot<n>`, `scene.skip`, return).
2. **Assertions after stone k** (k = 1, 2, 3) in every order:
   - `stone:<site>` = k; `stones` = k; flights visible = k (`G.debug.sky.flights()`); crown lanterns = 4 + k (with the 4
     Act II lights);
   - the knots open are exactly memories 1-3 plus 4…(3 + 3k), and the three newly opened are `3k+1 … 3k+3`;
   - Wenna's stone line is the one for `stones = k` (text id `home.scene.stone.<k>`), never the site's;
   - after touching the new knots: `mem:<n>` set once each; replaying a knot does not re-set flags (scene replay rule);
   - after k = 2: twist T5's memory (memory 9, "Who would they wake up next to?") has played exactly once.
3. **Final assertions** (k = 3): `memories` = 12; `q:remember` done; the condition `stones==3 && memories==12` holds (the
   ch18 trigger S-264 will read); `stoneHeld:*` all cleared; each `stone:<site>` value matches its position in the order;
   the three isles' healed states are on.
4. **Fixtures** written: `tests/fixtures/chapters/stones-<a>-<b>-<c>.json` end states (six files, about 6 KB each).
5. **CI**: a job `stones` with two shards (`stones:1/2` runs orders 1-3, `stones:2/2` orders 4-6), each ≤ 15 min; add to
   `.github/workflows/ci.yml`'s in-page matrix list.
6. **Pure unit test** `tests/stone-count.test.js`: the knot-open function `knotsOpen(stones, memoriesWatched)` and the
   lantern count over all 6 orders × 4 states (no browser).

#### Assets
None.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep the save, tone and machine lines (no content changes).
- Both CI shards pass; all six end fixtures equal on `memories`, `lanterns`, `flags` except the three `stone:*` order
  values; the unit test passes.
- If any order fails, the fix is a bug step against S-212 or the failing land's step, linked in the PR (the test lands
  marked `todo` for that order only, never deleted).

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test tests/stone-count.test.js`. `node tests/stone-orders.mjs 1/2` then `node tests/stone-orders.mjs
  2/2` (never both at once).

#### Out of scope
Chapter 18 (M30).

#### Risk & rollback
Test-only; revert the PR.

<a id="s-258a"></a>

### S-258a · feat: the Academy library and side story 42 Sable's Dig

Milestone M29 · Depends on: S-254 · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-42](../bible/side-content.md#ss-42), [atlas-far#saltglass-interiors](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-interiors),
[atlas-far#saltglass-pois](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-pois), [atlas-far#saltglass-states](../bible/atlas-far.md#saltglass-states),
[cast#sable](../bible/cast.md#sable), [systems#portal-table](../bible/systems.md#portal-table), [charter#verbs-new](../bible/charter.md#verbs-new)

INDEX's S-258 is split: this part ships the library interior (which ss-43 and ss-44 both use) and ss-42; S-258b
ships ss-43 Roll Call (see Index changes).

**Decisions:** the interior's id is `academy_library` ([systems#realm-registry](../bible/systems.md#realm-registry)),
not the atlas's `int_academy_library`. Brushing is the Paws' verb, so the pill is `[T] Dig · Dig site` with a 2 s hold
(the charter puts Dig on the tool key; side-content's `[E]` is read as "the dig prompt"). Atlas-far's 12 brush spots are
six ss-42 dig sites round the trenches (this step), three "rock" sites on the hill's skirts (this step) and three
nameplate sites on the hill's skirts (S-258b).

#### Why
Roadmap item **M29 · Saltglass stories and the road to the sky**. Sable finally finds a real thing (a student's lamp,
which she calls a pot), and the camp gets a museum-shaped tent.

#### What
1. **Interior `academy_library`** (`js/realms/interiors/academy_library.js`, the S-054 interior recipe; 2 m tiles,
   realm grid 64 m, parent `saltglass`, clock keeps running, light warm `#ffe2a8` ambient 0.7 with two window cards):

   ```
   #################
   #t.......t#t...t#
   #.........#.....#
   #.........#.....#
   #.........D.....#
   #.........#.....#
   #.........#.....#
   #t...@...t#t...t#
   #####X###########
   ```
   Reading room (left, cols 1-9) and card catalogue (right, cols 11-15), joined by an open doorway `D` (10,4) (`always`).
   Props by tile: bookcases along the west wall (1,2)-(1,6) and the north wall (2,1)-(8,1) except the board; **the Roll
   Call board** on the north wall at (5,1) (nine brass hooks in a 3 × 3 grid, empty; S-258b); **Sable's workroom desk**
   (6,3) (lamp, notebook, a magnifier); **the Retake desk** (3,5) facing a slate on the west wall (1,4) (S-259); card
   cabinets (12-14, 2) and (12-14, 6); a reading lamp (13,4). One curio in the catalogue room, `[E] Read · Card`
   `sg.lib.card` "A catalogue card: 'LADDER OF LIGHTS, THE. See: Book of the Lamplighters, pages 1-30.' Someone has
   written underneath: 'Pages sent away.'" (spoiler class B: Saltglass is a class-B land.)
   Portal **`saltglass:library`** on the S-246 Library Dig doorway (30, 50): `[E] Enter · The Academy library`, `needs:
   'quest:mq-saltglass>=3'`, to spawn `entry` (5,7); `X` back to a spawn 2 m outside the doorway.
2. **Sable's schedule** (S-246) changes its 20:00 row from the tent to `academy_library:desk` (indoors) once the library is
   open (`quest:mq-saltglass>=3`); the tent remains her fallback before then.
3. **ss-42 Sable's Dig** (`js/content/quests/saltglass.js`, id `ss-42`, n 142, owner `sable`, unlock
   `['realm:saltglass.visited', 'ability:dig']`, reward `gems:+35`, `set:ss42.done`, minutes 20):

   | Step | Text (`sg.ss42.s<n>`) | Done | On |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `ask` | Sable has found a pot (probably) | `flag:ss42.asked` | `TALK_END` |
   | `brush` | Brush the six dig sites ({n}/6) | `flag:ss42.n>=6` | `ITEM_GET` |
   | `show` | Show Sable the finds | `flag:ss42.shown` | `SCENE_END` |

   - Start: Sable's priority-40 entry: `sg.ss42.sable.1` "A POT! Lamplighter! Probably! Possibly a hat! Brush, don't dig.
     Digging is for moles. No offence to moles." → `quest:ss-42.start`, `set:ss42.asked`.
   - **The six dig sites** (`placeSideMarks`, `new RNG(40277 * 107 + 19)`, each nearest walkable within 4 m, ≥ 3 m from
     every prop, spot and trench edge): (85, −150), (95, −125), (120, −128), (135, −145), (128, −175), (98, −172). Each is
     a stake with a red ribbon and a ring of string (the S-244 trench string). `[T] Dig · Dig site` needs a **2.0 s hold**
     (the Paws' brush variant: a soft brush swish every 0.4 s and sand puffs as its twin); letting go keeps the progress
     for that site while Pip stays in the realm. Finds (site 1-6 → key items, kind `delivery`, item-get "A find! {name}"):
     `find_slate` "A chalk slate" · `find_lamp` "A student's lamp" · `find_clapper` "A bell clapper" · `find_tin` "A lunch
     tin" · `find_quill` "A moth-wing quill" · `find_tile` "A spiral floor tile". Each sets `inc:ss42.n`.
   - **Show** (Talk with Sable with all six): scene `ss42-show` (≈ 40 s, `replay: true`): for each find, Sable's label,
     then, if `boss:buried_academy:scarabelle`, Scarabelle's correction (she is `spawn`ed beside the table for the scene):

     | Find | Sable (`sg.ss42.label.<n>`) | Scarabelle (`sg.ss42.fix.<n>`) |
     |---|---|---|
     | slate | "A royal serving platter! For very flat food!" | "A slate. For sums. Mostly wrong sums, by the look of it." |
     | lamp | "A POT! A real one! ...With a handle. And a wick. A very ambitious pot." | "A first-year lamp. Every student made one. This one was made badly, and with love." |
     | clapper | "A giant's spoon! Or a very small giant's spoon." | "The clapper from the old practice bell. Not the bell's stone. Do not get ideas." |
     | tin | "A ceremonial vessel for... sacred sandwiches!" (bible `sable.2`) | "It is a lunch tin. It was Glim's. He never finished his crusts." (bible `scarabelle.1`) |
     | quill | "A ceremonial feather duster. For very small ceremonies." | "A quill. Moth-wing. For copying out the Book. Someone had very tidy handwriting." |
     | tile | "A map! Of a snail! A snail's map of itself!" | "A floor tile from the entrance hall. Every student stepped on that spiral on their first day." |

     Then `sg.ss42.sable.3` "Six finds! A museum! Well. A tent. A museum-shaped tent." Effects: `take:` each find,
     `set:ss42.shown`.
   - **World mark**: a museum tent at Sablecamp (placeSideMarks, nearest walkable to (125, −182), the S-244 tent model
     with a painted "MUSEUM" board, canvas text on a plank) holding six stands with the finds; each stand `[E] Look · <find>`
     shows Sable's label and, once `boss:buried_academy:scarabelle` holds, Scarabelle's sticky note as a second line
     (so a player who did ss-42 before the exam still gets the corrections later; nothing is missable).
   - Journal (Hoot, verbatim): open "Sable brushes sand off old things. I brush crumbs off old papers. Kindred spirits." /
     done "Six finds. Sable's labels are wrong. Scarabelle's notes are right. I have adopted Sable's." Hints: 1 ["Sable
     has opinions about digging.", "Talk to Sable at her finds table in Sablecamp."] 2 ["Ribbons on stakes, round the
     trenches.", "Hold {tool} at each of the six ribboned dig sites round Sablecamp's trenches for two seconds."] 3 ["Six
     finds, one very excited jerboa.", "Bring all six finds to Sable and talk to her."]
4. **Three rock sites** on the hill's skirts (same stream, after the six): at r 90 from (−20, 40) on bearings 100°, 160°,
   300° → anchors (68.6, 55.6), (10.8, 124.6), (−97.9, −5.0). Same 2 s dig; each reveals a rock with a label and a Look
   line, no reward: `sg.rock.1` "A rock. Sable has already labelled it: 'POT (hopefully)'." · `sg.rock.2` "A rock shaped
   exactly like a pot. It is a rock." · `sg.rock.3` "A very round rock. Sable will want to see it. Sable will be
   disappointed. Sable will label it anyway."
5. **Coverage**: raise Saltglass's asserted POI minimum from 148 (S-249b) to **157** (+9 sites); S-258b raises it to the
   full rule (160 at 200,000 m², or measured land / 1,250).
6. **Economy**: ss-42 35 gems.

#### Assets
- Finds (code-built primitives, 30-100 tris each, side-content `prop_dig_finds`): chalk slate (dark board in a wooden
  frame), student lamp (a little clay lamp with a handle), bell clapper (a brass teardrop on a rod), lunch tin (a dented
  tin box), moth-wing quill (a pale feather-shaped wing), spiral tile (a square tile with a gold spiral). Icons: one
  `sheet-items` generation, N = 6: PREAMBLE + `sheet-items` + "six old school artefacts: a chalk slate, a small clay
  lamp, a brass bell clapper, a dented lunch tin, a feather quill made of a moth's wing, a floor tile with a spiral.
  Palette: #c9a46b #efe3c8 #b55d4c #8ec9d8." Slice with `tools/slice_sheet.py --cols 3 --rows 2`, export 128² cells into
  the icon atlas (`icon_find_<id>`, `realm: saltglass`). Fallback: canvas glyphs.
- Museum board, stakes, stands: code-built (plank + canvas text; stake + ribbon; a 0.8 m wooden stand).
- Library props: bookcases, desks, cabinets from the S-054 interior kit, tinted to the Academy palette.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line. Saltglass's realm prints are unchanged (new things are post-pass items and
  an interior realm with its own prints file `tests/fixtures/realm-prints/academy_library.json`).
- ss-42 runs from start to done; the dig hold keeps progress on release; each find given once; the show scene plays with
  or without Scarabelle; museum labels gain the notes later; 35 gems once.
- The library portal shows from `quest:mq-saltglass>=3`; entering and leaving lands on walkable spawns; Sable is at her
  desk from 20:00.
- Coverage: Saltglass POI count ≥ 157 and every cell still passes.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test` (quests, content, coverage, interior compile). `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (new case `ss-42`: dig
  the six sites with `G.debug.tool.hold('dig', 2.1)` at each anchor; show; assert the tent stands). `node tests/inpage.mjs
  realm` (library interior leak and spawn checks). `node tools/build-pages.mjs && bun run test:pwa`.

#### Out of scope
ss-43's nameplates and hooks (S-258b); the Retake (S-259).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the doorway shows its sign only again.

<a id="s-258b"></a>

### S-258b · feat: side story 43 Roll Call: nine nameplates and the dawn roll bell

Milestone M29 · Depends on: S-258a · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-43](../bible/side-content.md#ss-43), [dungeons#d9](../bible/dungeons.md#d9),
[cast#tutor-scarabelle](../bible/cast.md#tutor-scarabelle), [cast#granny-wick](../bible/cast.md#granny-wick),
[cast#pelagia](../bible/cast.md#pelagia), [systems#scenes](../bible/systems.md#scenes)

**Decisions:**
- Unlock: side-content's `flag:stone.academy` is written `boss:buried_academy:scarabelle` (the passed exam; systems'
  boss key).
- The student "Ashwing, second year, beacons" is written **"Ashmoth, second year, beacons"**: dungeon 9 already ships the
  plate "F. Ashmoth" (S-250), and one student must have one name. The other eight names are side-content's.
- Where the nine are (side-content: four in the Academy's cleared rooms, three under drifts on Academy Hill, two behind
  ivy): **Wick**, **Pelagia** and **Ashmoth** are the three dungeon door plates (after the exam, each nameplate door
  shows `[E] Pick up · Nameplate` and the door stays open); **Glim** sits on the shelf of r6 The Sand Pump Room;
  **Tallow**, **Brightfern** and **Quill** are under three hill-skirt dig sites; **Lanthorn** and **Nettle** are on the two
  ruin-wall decks S-249b reached by ivy. The student Tallow is the girl who later became Keeper Tallow, Wick's mother
  (lore: the keepers studied here); nothing says so in this story.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M29 · Saltglass Flats and the third stone**'s follow-on: Scarabelle has called a roll nobody answered for a
hundred years. Pip answers for two old students he knows.

#### What
1. **Quest** `ss-43` (`js/content/quests/saltglass.js`, n 143, owner `scarabelle`, unlock `boss:buried_academy:scarabelle`,
   reward `gems:+35`, `set:ss43.done`, minutes 20):

   | Step | Text (`sg.ss43.s<n>`) | Done | On |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | `ask` | Tutor Scarabelle calls the roll | `flag:ss43.asked` | `TALK_END` |
   | `plates` | Find the nine student nameplates ({n}/9) | `flag:ss43.n>=9` | `ITEM_GET` |
   | `hang` | Hang the nameplates on the classroom hooks | `flag:ss43.hung>=9` | `ITEM_GET` |
   | `roll` | Answer the roll for two old students | `flag:ss43.roll` | `SCENE_END` |

   - Start (Scarabelle's priority-40 entry; side-content: "talk at dawn or any time after the first visit", so any time):
     `sg.ss43.scarabelle.1` "Roll call. Nobody has answered in a hundred years. I call it anyway. A school is a school." →
     `quest:ss-43.start`, `set:ss43.asked`.
2. **The nine nameplates** (key items `plate_<name>`, kind `delivery`, `[E] Pick up · Nameplate`; text `sg.ss43.plate.<n>`
   verbatim from side-content except Ashmoth): 1 "Wick, first year, lamps" · 2 "Pelagia, first year, bells (late)" · 3
   "Tallow, second year, wicks" · 4 "Glim, second year, crusts" · 5 "Lanthorn, third year, mirrors" · 6 "Brightfern, third
   year, songs" · 7 "Quill, first year, logbook" · 8 "Ashmoth, second year, beacons" · 9 "Nettle, first year, stairs".
   Where: Wick on the d9 door N (30,4), Pelagia on N (30,10), Ashmoth on N (10,12) (each door keeps its engraved name and
   stays open after the plate is taken); Glim on r6's shelf at tile (24,9) (a new pickup in `buried_academy`, visible
   only after the exam); Tallow, Brightfern, Quill under three hill-skirt dig sites (S-258a's stream, after the rock
   sites) at r 90 from (−20, 40) on bearings 40°, 220°, 250° → anchors (37.9, −28.9), (−77.9, 108.9), (−104.6, 70.8),
   the same 2 s `[T] Dig · Dig site`; Lanthorn on the ruin-wall deck nearest (−95, 25) and Nettle on the deck nearest
   (−60, −5) (S-249b's ivy decks). Each pickup `inc:ss43.n`. Picking up Quill's adds Scarabelle's aside when hung:
   `sg.ss43.quill` "Different Quill." (side-content's joke; the porcupine is Quill too).
3. **Hanging**: at the Roll Call board in the library, `[E] Place · Nameplate` hangs every held plate in its fixed hook
   (row-major in the list order), `take:plate_<name>`, `inc:ss43.hung` per plate.
4. **The roll** (scene `ss43-roll`, interactive, `replay: false`; [systems#scenes](../bible/systems.md#scenes) lists it as
   one of the non-replayable scenes): at the next dawn (05:00-08:00) once all nine hang, with Pip within 30 m of the exam
   garden (−10, 70) (Scarabelle's day spot); a **roll bell** on a post (placeSideMarks, nearest walkable to (−6, 72)).
   Scarabelle reads the nine names in list order, 3 s apart; at "Wick?" and "Pelagia?" she pauses and waits up to 8 s for
   `[E] Ring · Roll bell` (the prompt pulses gold; its ring is the twin of the voice line). If Pip rings: "...Present."
   If not, she waits, then asks again (it cannot be missed). Other names: a soft pencil tick, no line. End:
   `sg.ss43.scarabelle.2` "Wick? ...Present. Pelagia? ...Present. Well. That's two. Two is a class." → `set:ss43.roll`.
5. **World marks** (on `ss43.done`): the nine plates hang on the board (Look shows each text); the **roll bell rings at
   dawn** (05:30) across Academy Hill every day (`sfx rollBell`, a small high bell; the post's bell swings); the eight
   Dormitory Row door plates (S-244) become polished and readable with the matching names for dorms 1-8 (the plate decal
   gets canvas text: dorm-1 "W.", dorm-2 "P.", then "T.", "G.", "L.", "B.", "Q.", "N."; atlas-far: "one says W. and one
   says P."); Granny Wick and Pelagia each gain one line (priority 30, `once`):
   - Granny Wick (Duskwick) `cc.ss43.wick.1` (side-content, verbatim) "She still calls the roll? Tell her I'm present.
     Tell her I did my homework. I didn't."
   - Pelagia (Glassway; her tic is word pairs and "Ting.") `gw.ss43.pelagia.1` "She still calls it? ...Late, as always.
     Present, though. Tell her. Ting."
6. **Journal** (Hoot, verbatim): open "A beetle calls a roll nobody answers. I would answer. I was never asked." / done
   "Two answered, through Pip. Scarabelle marked them present. She marked me 'visiting'. I accept." Hints: 1 ["She keeps
   a register.", "Talk to Tutor Scarabelle in the exam garden behind the bell tower."] 2 ["Doors, a shelf, the hill, and
   two high walls.", "Take the three plates from the Academy's brass doors and Glim's from the Sand Pump Room shelf; dig
   three on the hill's skirts; climb the ivy on two ruined walls."] 3 ["The library has hooks.", "Place the nameplates on
   the Roll Call board in the Academy library."] 4 ["The roll is called at dawn.", "Be in the exam garden at dawn and ring
   the roll bell when she calls Wick and Pelagia."]
7. **Coverage**: raise Saltglass's POI minimum to the full rule (≥ 160, or measured land / 1,250 if larger).
8. **Economy**: ss-43 35 gems.

#### Assets
- Roll bell on a post (reuse `gate_bell_target`'s bell on a 1.6 m post with a blue ribbon `#8ec9d8`). Nameplates reuse
  `decal_nameplate` with canvas text; `icon_nameplate` (S-250).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line.
- Nine plates findable after the exam (none missable: the dungeon doors stay open; dig and ivy sites have no time limit);
  hanging takes them; the roll scene waits for the two rings and cannot fail; 35 gems once; the dawn bell, the dorm
  plates and the two new lines follow.
- Lint: all lines ≤ 220; "Ting." only in Pelagia's line; no `~` or `!!`.
- Coverage: Saltglass passes the full POI density rule.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test`. `node tests/inpage.mjs side` (case `ss-43`: from the `ch-saltglass` end fixture, collect the nine
  via warps and the real pickup/dig/climb handlers, hang them, `G.debug.tod(5.2)`, drive the two rings, assert `ss43.done`).
  `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
ss-44 (S-259).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the board's hooks stay empty.

<a id="s-259"></a>

### S-259 · feat: side story 44 The Exam Retake: a quiz built from gossip the player heard

Milestone M29 · Depends on: S-254, S-258a · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[side-content#ss-44](../bible/side-content.md#ss-44), [cast#tutor-scarabelle](../bible/cast.md#tutor-scarabelle),
[systems#conditions](../bible/systems.md#conditions), [systems#dialogue](../bible/systems.md#dialogue)

**Decisions:** unlock is `boss:buried_academy:scarabelle` (side-content's `flag:stone.academy`). Each bank row's "Heard"
column becomes a condition in the table below; where the line that told the gossip is a `once` dialogue entry, its
`seen:<entry id>` flag is preferred (S-032 sets it) and the listed condition is the fallback the PR uses if that entry id
does not exist. The quiz order is deterministic: a draw on `new RNG(dayIndex * 31 + 7)` where `dayIndex = floor(S.playTime
/ 480)`, so a test can predict it.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M29 · Saltglass stories and the road to the sky**. The exam of listening: every question is something a
character told the player. It cannot be failed.

#### What
1. **Quest** `ss-44` (n 144, owner `scarabelle`, unlock `boss:buried_academy:scarabelle`, reward `['gems:+35',
   'give:nook_gift_certificate']`, `set:ss44.done`, minutes 15): steps `ask` (talk; `sg.ss44.scarabelle.1` "Everyone
   deserves a second exam. Most people deserve a third. Sit. Pencils are optional; you haven't got one." →
   `quest:ss-44.start`) · `quiz` "Answer ten questions" (`[E] Use · Retake desk` in the library, S-258a; Scarabelle is
   spawned at the slate for the quiz) · `grade` "Collect your certificate" (talk; `sg.ss44.scarabelle.2` "I grade on
   effort. Yours was visible. A-plus. The 'plus' is for listening to people." → item-get "Certificate of Lamplighting
   (Honorary)").
2. **Quiz module** `js/content/retake.js` (pure, unit-tested): `BANK` = the 20 rows of
   [side-content#ss-44](../bible/side-content.md#ss-44) verbatim (question, answer, two wrong options), each with `heard`:

   | # | `heard` | # | `heard` |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | 1 | `met:miso` | 11 | `secret:bottle` (the shipped bottle; add the atom, see 4) |
   | 2 | `boss:woods` | 12 | `quest:ss-33=done` |
   | 3 | `boss:peaks` | 13 | `quest:ss-18=done` |
   | 4 | `boss:shores` | 14 | `quest:ss-32>=1` |
   | 5 | `{ any: ['quest:ss-04=done', 'flag:jettyOpen'] }` | 15 | `flag:ss39.promise` |
   | 6 | `flag:ch8` | 16 | `quest:ss-34=done` |
   | 7 | `met:gloop` | 17 | `free` (always) |
   | 8 | `boss:whistlehollow:mossmantle` | 18 | `free` |
   | 9 | `boss:granary_vaults:honksworth` | 19 | `free` |
   | 10 | `quest:ss-02>=1` | 20 | `free` |

   `pickQuiz(S, dayIndex)` → 10 rows: all heard non-free rows, drawn without replacement by the RNG until 10; if fewer
   than 10 qualify, the free rows fill the rest in bank order. Options are the answer and the two wrong ones, shuffled by
   the same RNG. The free rows' answers are in their questions (side-content).
3. **UI**: the dialogue box in a `choice` mode (`ui.choose(textId, options) → Promise<index>`; keys 1/2/3 and mouse,
   pad D-pad + A, touch buttons; at phone width the three options stack). If S-032's dialogue has no choices yet, add
   this mode in this PR with a `hud.audit()` check at 1080p and 390 × 844. A wrong answer shows `sg.ss44.wrong` "Not
   quite. The answer is '{answer}'. Again." and asks the same question again (it cannot be failed); a right one shows one
   of `sg.ss44.right.1..3` "Correct." / "Quite right." / "Mm. Yes." in rotation. Counter "Question n/10" in the box title.
4. **Condition atom** `secret:<id>` in `js/cond.js` (holds when `S.secrets[id]`), with a unit test and a row in the
   content lint's atom list. (The shipped bottle is keepsake `bottle` in `js/data.js`.)
5. **World marks**: the certificate hangs on Pip's Nook wall (gift slot, S-302; Items tab until then); the library slate
   reads "RETAKE: PASSED ✿" (canvas text on the S-258a slate, `[E] Read · Slate`).
6. **Journal** (verbatim): open "Scarabelle sets an exam on gossip. I have been preparing my whole life." / done "Pip
   passed. The exam was about listening. I would have failed. I talk." Hints: 1 ["She believes in second chances.", "Talk
   to Tutor Scarabelle after passing her final exam."] 2 ["Everything you heard counts.", "Sit at the retake desk in the
   Academy library. Wrong answers are corrected; you can't fail."] 3 ["A-plus, probably.", "Talk to Scarabelle for your
   certificate."]
7. **Economy**: ss-44 35 gems.

#### Assets
- `nook_gift_certificate` (code-built framed paper 0.4 × 0.3 m, canvas text "Certificate of Lamplighting (Honorary)",
  frame `#c99a4a`). Icon: canvas. No generated images.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep all except the home line and the budget lines (no new view).
- `tests/retake.test.js`: with a fresh-after-exam fixture the quiz has 10 rows, all heard or free; a save that heard
  every row draws 10 of the 16 non-free rows, none free; the same dayIndex gives the same quiz; every question's options
  contain its answer once; every text ≤ 220.
- In-page `side` case `ss-44`: answer every question wrong once then right (the bot), finish, +35 gems once, the gift and
  the slate.
- `hud.audit()` passes with the choice box at 1080p and 390 × 844.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test` (retake, cond, content). `node tests/inpage.mjs side`. `bun run test:smoke`.

#### Out of scope
The Resit (S-253, shipped).

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; the Retake desk stays a desk.

<a id="s-260"></a>

### S-260 · feat: Wenna and the villagers react to Act III

Milestone M29 · Depends on: S-254, S-254b, S-170, S-033a · Size: M (200-500) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[story#wenna-lines](../bible/story.md#wenna-lines), [story#act-III-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-III-reactions),
[story#memory-lines](../bible/story.md#memory-lines), [charter#memory-line-rules](../bible/charter.md#memory-line-rules),
[systems#dialogue](../bible/systems.md#dialogue)

INDEX sizes this S; it is about 70 lines of text plus their entries, so it is M by the charter's line rule (dialogue
tables count) and still one PR.

#### Why
Roadmap item **M29 · Saltglass stories and the road to the sky**. The land as the progress bar, in words: Wenna's next
sixteen lines as the stones come home, each villager noticing what this player did in Act III, and Shellby going quiet
after the memory of a young turtle writing by lamplight.

#### What
1. **Wenna's lines w29-w44** (stages S8-S11) into the S-065 stage table (`js/content/wenna.js`, text `home.wenna.w<n>`),
   verbatim from [story#wenna-lines](../bible/story.md#wenna-lines):
   - **S8** `stoneSites` and `stones==0`: w29-w32. **S9** `stones==1`: w33-w36. **S10** `stones==2`: w37-w40. **S11**
     `stones==3` and `!act3done`: w41-w44.
   - Pick rule (S-065, unchanged): the lowest-numbered unseen line of the current stage, then rotation; lines of a passed
     stage are not shown later (the Lore tab's "Wenna said" list keeps every line heard).
2. **Act III reactions** (`js/content/dialogue/home.js` + Nell's table; priority 60 count beat, `once`), the whole table of
   [story#act-III-reactions](../bible/story.md#act-III-reactions) verbatim for Elder Shellby, Mayor Ribbit, Miso, Clover,
   Professor Hoot, Digby and Nell Pelliwick, beats: **C13** `flag:stoneSites`, **ch14** `flag:ch14`, **s1** `stones>=1`,
   **s2** `stones>=2`, **s3** `stones>=3`, **A3** `flag:act3done` (the A3 column ships now and plays from M30). Shellby's
   s1/s2/s3 are keyed to memories **6, 9 and 12 watched** (`flag:mem:6`, `mem:9`, `mem:12`) instead of stone counts.
   - **Only the newest beat shows**: each villager's entries carry `when` = their beat **and not the next beat**, so a
     save that is already past ch14 skips C13 and ch14 and gets the current one (no stale lines; nothing is "missed"
     because the Lore tab is not involved). Text ids `home.<npc>.a3.<beat>`. If S-191 or S-203 already shipped some of
     these rows (check by text), keep theirs and do not duplicate.
   - Catchers at the plaza after `act3done` (priority 60, `once`), verbatim: Grumble "Up there? Bring him down for tea. Or
     don't. Hmph. ...Do." · Pinch "Three stones set. That's a sailor's work, lad. Proper." · Frostbun "Stairs are cold. Hop
     quick. Come back."
3. **Shellby goes quiet** after `mem:12`: (a) his first Talk after `mem:12` shows a silent box "..." (text
   `home.shellby.quiet.0`, one box, no portrait change) before whatever line comes next (story: "his A3 line is preceded
   by a silent box on the first Talk"; this shows it before the s3 line, and again once before the A3 line when that
   arrives); (b) until `act3done` his idle rotation is replaced by `home.shellby.quiet.1` "..." and `home.shellby.quiet.2`
   "Hm? Oh. Just remembering, dear. Old storms. Old friends." (never a hint that he wrote the bottle: charter name-use,
   spoiler rule for T7).
4. **Villager memory lines, Act III** (priority 30, the S-012/S-170 mechanism: on the first Talk in act 3 the first fact
   that holds is spoken once and sets `mem:<npc>:3`), verbatim from [story#memory-lines](../bible/story.md#memory-lines)
   Act III table: Shellby (`quest:ss-09=done` → …; `flag:stone:bell=1` → …; default), Ribbit (`plushies>=8` … the count of
   `S.plushies`; default), Miso (`quest:ss-41=done`; default), Clover (`quest:ss-23=done`; default), Hoot
   (`quest:ss-20=done`; default), Digby (`quest:ss-33=done`; default). A `plushies>=n` fact uses the count of true keys in
   `S.plushies` (add a derived condition atom `plushies>=<n>` to `js/cond.js` if absent, with a unit test).
5. **Lint**: every new villager has exactly one memory line per shipped act (charter decision 30); the content test's
   "one memory line per act" check now includes act 3.

#### Assets
None.

#### Acceptance criteria
- Invariants: keep the save, tone, original IP and machine lines.
- `bun test`: content lint (≤ 220; `~` only in Miso's lines, e.g. "Fine~", "Typical~"; no Pip lines); the dialogue picker
  replays fixtures `ch14-end`, the six `stones-*` end fixtures (S-256) and `ch-saltglass` end, and asserts the expected
  newest-beat line per villager and the Wenna stage per fixture; the memory-line check for act 3.
- In-page `side` case `act3-lines`: from the `stones-bell-kettle-saltglass` fixture, Talk to each villager once (each
  says one memory line then the s3 reaction), Talk to Wenna four times (w41-w44 in order), watch memory 12 via
  `G.debug.flags.set('mem:12', true)` and Talk to Shellby: the "..." box precedes his line.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h`. `bun test`. `node tests/inpage.mjs side`. `bun run test:smoke`; `bun run test:playthrough` (Act I lines
  unchanged).

#### Out of scope
Wenna's lines w45-w60 and act 5 memory lines (S-291); Act IV reactions.

#### Risk & rollback
Revert the PR; villagers fall back to their idle rotations and Wenna to stage S7's lines.

<a id="s-261"></a>

### S-261 · perf: checkpoint 6: ten realms and the precache; close Saltglass stories

Milestone M29 · Depends on: S-254, S-258b, S-259, S-260, S-256 · Size: S (<200) · Save: none · Flag: none · Bible:
[charter#budgets](../bible/charter.md#budgets), [charter#budgets-over](../bible/charter.md#budgets-over),
[charter#close-out-perf](../bible/charter.md#close-out-perf), [charter#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone),
[art-audio#bundles](../bible/art-audio.md#bundles), [art-audio#brief-index](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-index),
[systems#test-harness](../bible/systems.md#test-harness)

#### Why
Roadmap item **M29 · Saltglass stories and the road to the sky**. Before the sky lands are built, prove every shipped
realm and the precache are inside budget (checkpoint 6 of 8), fix what is not, and close M29 with the thirteenth brief
batch.

#### What
1. **Measure** (perf close-out, [charter#close-out-perf](../bible/charter.md#close-out-perf)), each realm's 4 fixed views
   (`REALMS[id].views`) in **both** cameras via `G.debug.perf.views(id)`: main and total draw calls, main and total
   triangles; locally the main-pass GPU median and 90th percentile with the heavy perf sweep. The ten realms: `home`,
   `duskwick`, `pinewhistle`, `archwater`, `threemills`, `rootdeep`, `glassway`, `kettle`, `saltglass` and the variant
   `moonmere` (plus `remembered` if its views are defined). Every dungeon's worst room (the realm suite's per-room
   numbers) and the three Saltglass interiors (`buried_academy` r14 during paper 1, `academy_library`, the Hollows).
2. **Swap soak**: `node tests/inpage.mjs realm` with the soak flag: a 10× loop home → duskwick → pinewhistle →
   archwater → threemills → rootdeep → glassway → kettle → saltglass → home; after each lap `renderer.info.memory` back
   to baseline ± 2 and the browser cgroup's `memory.current` recorded (never summed RSS); swap times per pair (≤ 1.5 s
   overworld to overworld in software WebGL).
3. **genMs** per overworld (≤ 1.2 s desktop) and **precache**: `node tools/build-pages.mjs` output: core MB (≤ 30) and each
   bundle MB (≤ 2.5 without GLBs). If core is over: move single-land portraits into their land's bundle (art-audio
   decision 8: the 24 home and recurring-cast portraits stay core); do not remove `title.jpg`/`title_fp.jpg` here (that is
   S-307).
4. **Fix** every number over budget in the [charter order](../bible/charter.md#budgets-over) (cull, instance or merge,
   LOD, fewer types, shadows, move content; never the far plane or fog), in this PR if the fix is under ~500 lines, else
   file the next step naming the over-budget view.
5. **Record**: `docs/roadmap/progress.md` (checkpoint 6 entry, template) and the DESIGN §14 performance table (the new
   worst view); save size of the `ch-saltglass` end + M29 fixture (< 80 KB).
6. **Close M29** ([charter#close-out-milestone](../bible/charter.md#close-out-milestone) steps 1-10): S-258a, S-258b, S-259,
   S-260 stacked or merged; the full suite once (order as in S-257, plus the `stones` shards); DESIGN rows for ss-42,
   ss-43, ss-44, the Academy library, Act III reactions and Wenna's stage table; hours +2 h (cumulative 74.0 h) vs a
   careful run, with the gap rule.
7. **`docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-13.md`**: one brief per code-built model of M28-M29 without an owner GLB, in the
   [brief template](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-template), from the step PRs (fill gaps from the bible files named in
   [art-audio#brief-index](../bible/art-audio.md#brief-index)): `npc_sable`, `npc_dig_crew` (Grit, Quill);
   `enemy_sandsnoot`, `enemy_prickle_pear`, `enemy_sun_beetle`, `enemy_dust_puff`; `boss_scarabelle`; `tool_gloves`,
   `sword_starsteel` (S-233, per the index); the Saltglass kit (`saltglass_belltower`, `saltglass_glass_organ`,
   `saltglass_practice_beacon`, `saltglass_windcatcher`, `saltglass_glass`, `saltglass_ruin_roof`, `saltglass_ruin_wall`,
   `saltglass_column`, `saltglass_dorm`, `saltglass_tent`, `saltglass_trench`, `saltglass_finds_table`,
   `saltglass_lamp_post`, `saltglass_salt_block`, `saltglass_prickle`, `saltglass_nameplate`, `saltglass_mirage_well`,
   `saltglass_acacia`); dungeon 9 (`int_door_nameplate`, `int_desk`, `int_hourglass`); home's Crown Balcony; the
   hand-bell, the kites, the dig finds. Add commented-out `models` slots in `assets/manifest.js` for each.

#### Assets
None new (records and the batch document; manifest gains commented-out GLB slots).

#### Acceptance criteria
- Every view of every listed realm ≤ 200 main / ≤ 300 total draw calls (hard cap 350) and ≤ 1.3 M triangles in both
  cameras, or a filed follow-up step names the view that is not; each dungeon room ≤ 120 calls.
- Soak: no leak beyond ± 2 geometries/textures per lap; swap times recorded; no `modErrs`.
- Core precache ≤ 30 MB; every realm bundle ≤ 2.5 MB; numbers in progress.md and DESIGN §14.
- `docs/roadmap/briefs/batch-13.md` complete (the PR pastes `grep -c '^### '` = the list length, 34).
- The M29 close-out steps done; release state: sky realms hidden; chapter chain green.

#### Verification plan
- `free -h` (≥ 6 GB available) before each heavy job; one at a time: `bun test`; `node tools/build-pages.mjs` + `bun run
  test:pwa`; `bun run test:smoke`; `bun run test:opening`; `bun run test:playthrough`; every in-page suite and shard;
  every chapter test; `node tests/stone-orders.mjs 1/2` and `2/2`; then the perf sweep (`node tests/inpage.mjs realm`
  with the soak and views flags). Paste numbers and wall times in the PR body.

#### Out of scope
The sky lands (M30). Removing the old title art (S-307).

#### Risk & rollback
Records only, plus any small budget fix (each listed in the PR so it can be reverted alone).
